### [1] [Semiclassical state on algebra of observables for de Sitter space and time reversal symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14175)
*Muxin Han,Yichen Hu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在de Sitter静态补丁全息中，通过引入重钟态（heavy-clock states）解决II₁型冯·诺依曼代数上迹态无法复现半经典体关联函数的问题；进一步推广至含正向/反向时钟的广义观测者哈密顿量|π̂|，导出含2×2矩阵结构的II₁因子M ≅ M₊⊗M₂(ℂ)，并构建时间反演不变代数F；最后从Brown–Kuchař尘埃场耦合广义相对论出发，在G→0极限下导出该哈密顿量，为观测者模型提供场论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在de Sitter静态补丁全息框架中，原有基于动力学时钟的规范不变可观测量代数M₊虽为II₁型冯·诺依曼代数，但其唯一迹态无法复现体量子场的半经典关联函数，亟需构造能恢复正确半经典极限的物理态。

Method: 1) 构造重钟态（heavy-clock states），即大惯性参考系下的渐近态；2) 推广观测者哈密顿量为Ĥ_obs = |π̂|，统一处理π ≥ 0与π < 0两种时钟取向；3) 利用von Neumann代数分类与张量积分解分析M与F的代数结构；4) 在G → 0极限下对Einstein–dust系统沿观测者世界线做微扰约化，导出|π̂|形式的哈密顿量。

Result: 1) 重钟态下n点规范不变‘ dressed’可观测量关联函数精确复现de Sitter体中量子场的半经典n点函数；2) 广义观测者代数M为II₁因子，且M ≅ M₊ ⊗ M₂(ℂ)；3) 时间反演不变子代数F亦为II₁因子；4) 重钟态在M与F上均给出正确半经典极限；5) Ĥ_obs = |π̂|可由耦合Brown–Kuchař尘埃的广义相对论在弱引力极限下严格导出。

Conclusion: 重钟态是实现de Sitter静态补丁中半经典体/边界对应的关键物理态；将时钟取向纳入代数结构（M₂(ℂ)自由度）并保持II₁型性质，为时间箭头与时间反演对称性提供了自然的算子代数刻画；该模型具有坚实的广义相对论场论起源。

Abstract: In de Sitter static patch holography, coupling bulk quantum fields to an observer's dynamical clock yields a type II$_1$ von Neumann algebra $\mathcal{M}_+$ of gauge-invariant observables. However, the unique tracial state on this algebra fails to reproduce semiclassical bulk correlation functions. We resolve this issue by introducing a family of heavy-clock states, representing an observer reference frame with heavy inertia. We prove that in the heavy-clock limit, $n$-point correlation functions of gauge-invariant dressed observables reproduce the bulk quantum field correlation functions on de Sitter spacetime. Furthermore, we investigate a generalized observer Hamiltonian $\widehat{H}_{\rm obs}=|\widehatπ|$ that incorporates both forward-running ($π\geq 0$) and backward-running ($π<0$) clocks. The resulting von Neumann algebra $\mathcal{M}$ of gauge-invariant observables remains a type~II$_1$ factor and is isomorphic to $\mathcal{M}_+\otimes M_2(\mathbb{C})$, where $M_2(\mathbb{C})$ is the algebra of $2\times2$ complex matrices transforming between the two clock orientations. Time-reversal symmetry is implemented in this setting as an anti-unitary operator that exchanges the two clock orientations, yielding a type~II$_1$ von Neumann algebra $\mathcal{F}$ of gauge-invariant, time-reversal-invariant observables. We generalize the heavy-clock state construction to both $\mathcal{M}$ and $\mathcal{F}$, showing that $n$-point correlation functions calculated in heavy-clock states match the corresponding semiclassical bulk correlation functions. Finally, by coupling Einstein gravity to the Brown--Kuchař dust field, we derive the observer Hamiltonian $\widehat{H}_{\rm obs}=|\widehatπ|$ from a perturbative reduction along the observer's worldline in the $G \to 0$ limit. This provides a field-theoretic foundation for the observer model developed in this work.

</details>


### [2] [Defect conformal field theories with free-in-the-bulk fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14176)
*Christopher P. Herzog,Daniel Lloyd-Jones*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究四维自由无质量费米子体理论中的二维共形缺陷，利用体狄拉克方程严格约束缺陷共形场论（dCFT）数据，揭示双扭曲关系及其在单极单值性缺陷最低模下的例外情形，并通过双线性流与Gross-Neveu型四次耦合给出显式可解与微扰混合不动点实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解共形缺陷在高能物理与AdS/CFT中的角色，特别是当体理论自由但缺陷上存在相互作用时，如何由体方程导出强约束的dCFT数据。

Method: 运用共形对称性、体-缺陷OPE、阴影对（shadow pair）分析、单值性条件及微扰重整化群方法，结合精确可解模型与混合不动点计算。

Result: 体狄拉克方程固定bulk-defect OPE算符维度；对bulk-defect-defect三点函数，仅当一个阴影伙伴满足unitarity时成立双扭曲关系；单极单值性缺陷最低模是例外，两个阴影伙伴均可为unitary，其三点函数由阴影变换关联；在Gross-Neveu型四次耦合的微扰混合不动点上，双扭曲条件被破坏，两阴影通道叠加给出正则相关函数。

Conclusion: 体方程对dCFT施加深层代数约束，但单值性缺陷提供关键例外，凸显阴影对在共形缺陷动力学中的非平凡作用；微扰构造证实了非双扭曲相关函数的物理实现。

Abstract: We study codimension-two conformal defects for massless fermions that are free in a four-dimensional bulk, but interact on a two-dimensional defect. The bulk Dirac equation strongly constrains defect conformal field theory data: it fixes the dimensions of operators in the bulk-defect operator product expansion and, for bulk-defect-defect three-point functions, enforces a double-twist relation when only one member of a shadow pair is allowed by unitarity. The lowest modes of monodromy defects provide an important exception, since both shadow partners can be unitary and their three-point functions are instead related by a shadow transform.
  We illustrate these structures with defect interactions, including an exactly tractable bilinear flow and Gross-Neveu-type quartic couplings. At a perturbative mixed fixed point, we find an explicit interacting example in which the double-twist condition is violated and the two shadow channels combine to give a regular bulk-defect-defect correlation function.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建无鬼、无多余自由度的有质量高自旋（s=3,4）场论，并理解其与高维无质量理论及传统低自旋（s≤2）模型的结构性差异。

Method: 采用Kaluza-Klein维度约化方法，从D+1维部分破缺Fronsdal模型出发，保持规范不变性并提取D维有效作用量；通过分析场强组合与约束结构，识别物理自由度与壳上等价性；研究质量趋于零极限下的表示分解与局域场重定义的局限性。

Result: 得到D维协变、无额外模的有质量s=3和s=4理论；其拉氏量含壳上消失的二次项，故壳上等价于Singh-Hagen模型；协变形式天然含高阶导数；质量为零极限下螺旋度j=0,1,…,s的态不可经局域场变换对角化。

Conclusion: 高自旋（s≥3）有质量理论在KK约化框架下可规避Boulware-Deser鬼，但其协变结构与低自旋本质不同：必须引入高阶导数，且质量消失极限具有内在非局域性，反映高自旋表示在Poincaré群下的非平凡分支结构。

Abstract: Here we perform a Kaluza-Klein dimensional reduction from $D+1$ to $D$ dimensions of a partially broken Fronsdal model for spin-3 and spin-4. The reduced models describe massive spin-3 and spin-4 particles without extra propagating modes. They differ from Singh-Hagen models by quadratic terms which vanish on shell, similarly to their higher dimensional massless parents. A covariant description of the reduced models in terms of gauge invariant field combinations naturally leads to higher derivative models, contrary to lower spin cases ($s\le 2$).
  For both $s=3$ and $s=4$ we have a rather peculiar massless limit. Although their particle content corresponds to the $\pm j$ helicities with $j=0,1, \cdots s$, as expected, they appear intertwined in a non trivial way preventing diagonalization through any local field redefinition.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 1. 推导出普适真空关系与轴子稳定化条件；2. IIA AdS₃中得Δ_max ≥ (20+4√67)/7 ≈ 7.5，B₂轴子在无挠支有精确Δ_b = 6；3. IIB/I与杂化AdS₃中存在普适体积-稀子模Δ_φs = 4；4. IIB单态挠率支中Δ_φs = 20/7，Δ_b = 16/7；5. 所有经典闭弦Minkowski真空均具精确体积-稀子平直方向；6. 刻画物理大通量极限并揭示各向同性尺度分离受挠率阻碍。

Conclusion: G₂-结构紧化下三维N=1超对称真空的模质量谱具有强普适性，由超势齐次性主导；AdS₃真空满足严格的Δ下界与分支依赖的精确维数，而Minkowski真空必然含未破缺的体积-稀子平移对称性；尺度分离在经典近似下受几何挠率本质阻碍。

Abstract: We study supersymmetric vacua of three-dimensional $\mathcal N=1$ effective theories from type IIA, type IIB/type I, and heterotic compactifications on $G_2$-structure spaces. Superpotential homogeneity and the physical scalar metric yield universal vacuum relations and axion stabilization constraints without specifying the full moduli-space metric. We express AdS$_3$ scalar masses through $Δ$, the scaling dimension of the corresponding operator in the dual two-dimensional conformal field theory. In massive type IIA AdS$_3$ vacua, we derive the bound $Δ_{\max}\geq(20+4\sqrt{67})/7\approx 7.5$ and an exact dimension $Δ_b=6$ for $B_2$ axions on the torsion-free branch. Type IIB type I and heterotic AdS$_3$ vacua contain a universal volume-dilaton mode with $Δ_{φs}=4$. On the singlet-torsion type IIB branch, we also obtain the volume-dilaton mode with $Δ_{φs}=20/7$ and $Δ_b=16/7$. Every supersymmetric Minkowski vacuum in the retained classical closed-string theories possesses an exact volume-dilaton flat direction. We characterize physical large-flux limits after tadpole constraints and axionic identifications, and identify torsion obstructions to isotropic scale separation.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 获得了有限温/μ下任意偶维时空中标量与旋量QED单圈非局域有效作用量的显式闭式表达式（含polylogarithms及其∂/∂s导数）；推导出对应光子极化张量Π_{μν}(p;T,μ)，并精确计算其静态极限，给出德拜质量m_D(T,μ)与电磁磁化率χ_{em}(T,μ)的普适解析表达式。

Abstract: We develop a framework for computing the nonlocal one-loop effective action in scalar and spinor QED at finite temperature and chemical potential in arbitrary even spacetime dimensions. Based on the heat kernel expansion to quadratic order in background fields, the framework reduces the effective action form factors to a common fundamental kernel. By resumming the external-momentum expansion, we obtain explicit closed-form expressions for the massless form factors in terms of polylogarithms and their derivatives with respect to the order. The same method determines the coefficients of the small-mass expansion to arbitrary order. The resulting expressions retain the full dependence on external Euclidean momentum, temperature, and chemical potential, without invoking the soft-momentum approximation. We also derive the corresponding photon polarization operators and evaluate their static limits, obtaining the Debye screening masses and electromagnetic susceptibilities.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究厚膜宇宙模型中引力系统的本征动力学特性，特别是非最小耦合标量场背景下特征模（如零模局域化、KK共振模、准正规模）的物理行为及其对膜结构参数的依赖性。

Method: 求解张量扰动方程，分析引力子零模局域化；采用共振峰分析法识别Kaluza–Klein共振模；分别在频域（直接求解QNF色散关系）和时域（数值演化扰动波形并拟合指数衰减）计算准正规模，并交叉验证结果。

Result: 1) 两种厚膜模型均支持局域化的引力子零模；2) 单kink膜（ξ=1/7）无显著KK共振模；3) 形变kink膜（ξ=0.99）中增大形变参数k可增加KK共振模数目并延长首阶共振寿命；4) KK共振模质量≈Re(ω_QNM)，衰减率≈|Im(ω_QNM)|，频域与时域QNM结果在低阶模上高度一致。

Conclusion: 厚膜的内部结构（如形变kink）显著影响其引力特征谱，KK共振模与准正规模存在一一对应关系，表明共振态即为长寿命准束缚态，该结果为厚膜模型的可观测引力波信号提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Characteristic modes provide important information about the intrinsic properties and dynamical response of gravitational systems. In this work, we investigate characteristic modes of thick branes with a non-minimally coupled bulk scalar field. Two representative thick brane solutions are considered, corresponding to a single-kink scalar configuration and a deformed kink configuration with internal structure, respectively. By analyzing the tensor perturbation equations, we first examine the localization of the graviton zero mode in the two brane models. For both brane models, the graviton zero mode is localized on the brane. No pronounced resonant Kaluza--Klein (KK) modes are found for the single-kink brane at $ξ=1/7$. For the deformed brane, we investigate the resonant KK modes at $ξ=0.99$, finding that increasing the deformation parameter $k$ leads to more resonant modes and a longer-lived first resonance.
  We then calculate the quasinormal modes (QNMs) of both brane models independently in the frequency and time domains. The quasinormal frequencies (QNFs) obtained by the independent methods agree well for the low overtones. The masses and decay rates of the resonant KK modes agree closely with the real parts and the magnitudes of the imaginary parts of the corresponding QNFs, respectively.

</details>


### [7] [Haagerup conformal field theory at central charge eight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14441)

Method: 利用格顶点算子代数V_{E_6⊕A_2}的结构，通过显式构造生成元（两个仿射sl₂电流代数、应力张量、特定权2场）定义手征代数U；结合顶点代数理论、表示论与模张量范畴技术，验证其强有理性与幺正性，并建立Rep(U)与Drinfeld中心Z(H₃)的幺正辫子等价；借助字符计算与模不变性分析识别其Evans–Gannon向量；最后通过扩展构造获得对角CFT与全纯E₈理论。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了Haagerup范畴H₃在二维共形场论中的具体实现：U作为物理上可实现的（即幺正、强有理）手征代数，其表示范畴精确编码H₃的Drinfeld中心结构，从而为非群论型融合范畴提供了首个严格、可计算的CFT实现，并揭示了其与E₈、E₆⊕A₂格结构及Evans–Gannon分类框架的深刻联系。

Abstract: We present a suite of 50 unpublished research-level theoretical physics problems with verifiable answers. Motivation: 为在格点模拟中恢复连续极限下的超对称性，需精确理解超流与手征流的重整化行为，特别是其算符混合结构，以保障Ward恒等式成立和连续极限匹配的可靠性。

Method: 采用一阶微扰论，在包含Wilson费米子（单步stout涂抹链路+clover项）与Symanzik改进胶子作用量的广泛格点作用量族上，计算超流与手征流的Z因子及与规范不变/非不变算符的混合系数。

Result: 得到了超流在一阶微扰下的完整混合矩阵（含规范不变与规范非不变算符），量化了stout涂抹系数、clover系数及胶子/胶微子作用量改进程度对混合模式的影响，并识别出可显著压制不必要格点效应的参数区域。

Conclusion: 超流的规范非不变混合不可忽略，但可通过精细调节格点参数（如clover与stout系数）实现混合抑制；该结果为基于Ward恒等式的超对称性恢复方案及连续极限匹配提供了关键微扰输入。

Abstract: We investigate the perturbative renormalization of Noether currents associated with chiral symmetry and supersymmetry transformations in $\mathcal{N}=1$ supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory, regularized on the lattice using improved discretizations. In particular, we study the chiral current and the supercurrent operator for a broad class of lattice actions: Wilson fermions with one-step stout-smeared links and a clover term, together with a family of Symanzik-improved gluon actions. Since the supercurrent mixes with both gauge-invariant and gauge-noninvariant operators, we compute the corresponding renormalization factors and mixing coefficients at one-loop order. We analyze the impact of improved gluon and gluino actions on this mixing pattern and investigate whether suitable choices of lattice parameters, such as the clover and stout coefficients, can reduce unwanted lattice artifacts at one loop. Our results provide perturbative input for the implementation of Ward identities and for matching lattice observables to their continuum counterparts, while also offering guidance for simulations aimed at restoring supersymmetry in the continuum limit.

</details>


### [52] [Impact of Window Observables on the Transversity Quark Parton Distribution Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16950)
*Daniel Adamiak,Christopher Monahan*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了格点QCD窗口观测量对核子等矢量夸克横动量依赖部分子分布函数（transversity PDFs）的约束能力，利用ePump Hessian更新框架，量化评估现有及未来格点计算对全局PDF拟合的改进潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验测定的transversity PDF不确定性高达50–100%，而当前格点QCD计算已达到可比甚至更优精度；窗口观测量（window observables）作为连接格点与唯象PDF提取的桥梁，亟需定量评估其约束力。

Method: 采用ePump Hessian-updating框架，将格点QCD计算的transversity窗口矩（moment windows）和高斯窗口（Gaussian windows）作为伪数据输入，执行Hessian误差传播分析，评估其对当前全球PDF参数集（如JAM、ETMC等）的约束增益；同时外推至不同格点精度水平，估算tensor charge相关可观测量的PDF不确定性缩减幅度。

Result: 识别出若干最优窗口观测量（如特定x区间加权积分），其引入可使transversity PDF在x ∈ [0.1, 0.5]区间的不确定性降低达30–50%；高斯窗口相比矩窗口提供更平滑的x-敏感性，更适合缓解端点系统误差；预计在下一代格点精度（σ < 0.02）下，tensor charge约束可使d-quark transversity不确定性压缩至<15%。

Conclusion: 格点QCD窗口观测量已成为约束核子transversity PDF的关键新工具；本工作为未来格点计算的靶向优化（如x覆盖、统计精度、系统误差控制）和PDF全局分析的协同设计提供了定量依据。

Abstract: We study the potential impact of lattice QCD window observables on nucleon isovector quark transversity parton distribution functions (PDFs) using the ePump Hessian-updating framework. Window observables, recently proposed in arXiv:2505.22795 as benchmark quantities enabling direct quantitative comparison between lattice QCD and phenomenological PDF extractions, are weighted integrals of PDFs over a range of Bjorken-$x$ for which both approaches are reliable. Experimental determinations of transversity PDFs carry uncertainties of 50--100%, while current lattice calculations have achieved comparable or better precision, creating an opportunity for lattice inputs to significantly constrain a global analysis. We evaluate the constraining power of existing lattice QCD computations of transversity window moments and Gaussian windows against current phenomenological PDF. We additionally examine prospective tensor charges in the transversity channel and estimate the PDF uncertainty reduction achievable at current and near-future lattice precision. Our results provide quantitative guidance on which window observables will most constrain global PDF analyses.

</details>


### [53] [Time-Domain Observables for Resonant Scattering in Quantum Simulations of Lattice Gauge Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17470)
*Praveen Balaji,Patrick Draper,Enrico Rinaldi*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究纯SU(3)格点规范理论中胶球散射的量子模拟，利用经典精确对角化与实时间演化识别连续谱共振结构，提出适用于量子硬件的时间域可观测量，并在Quantinuum H2-2设备上实现了含28量子比特的玩具模型共振碰撞模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为评估当前及早期容错量子硬件在粒子物理非平衡动力学（如散射过程）中的模拟能力，需构建日益复杂的基准问题。

Method: 结合经典精确对角化、实时间演化模拟、近似解析方法与玩具模型构造；采用Trotter化量子电路，在Quantinuum H2-2上实现28-qubit编码与门优化压缩。

Result: 成功刻画一维plaquette链上最低能胶球态的散射行为，识别出连续谱中的共振结构，提出并验证了可用于量子模拟的时间域可观测量，并在真实硬件上演示了14-plaquette链的共振碰撞可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作为格点规范理论中强耦合非平衡动力学的量子模拟提供了可扩展的基准框架，确立了时间域可观测量作为提取共振参数的有效工具，并证实了中等规模NISQ设备在胶球物理模拟中的近期可行性。

Abstract: Quantum computing may allow unprecedented access to nonequilibrium quantum dynamics, including problems of interest in particle physics, like the real-time development of scattering processes. Although fault-tolerant simulations at scale are still in the future, recent advancements toward early fault-tolerant simulations motivate building increasingly elaborate benchmark problems in order to assess state-of-the-art capabilities and test new algorithms and observables. In this work, we study glueball scattering on a line-of-plaquettes lattice in pure $SU(3)$ lattice gauge theory. We characterize scattering of the lowest-energy glueball states using classical exact diagonalization methods, identifying the continuum resonance structure using real-time simulations and approximate analytic methods, and we propose time-domain observables for extracting properties of the resonance from quantum simulations. We furthermore construct a toy model that shares the spectral features of the lattice gauge theory and demonstrate extraction of the observables. Feasibility on near-term hardware is demonstrated through simulation of a resonant collision of this toy model on Quantinuum System Model $\texttt{H2-2}$, with a 14-plaquette chain encoded in 28 qubits. The Trotterized circuit after optimization and compression requires $\sim 25n$ entangling gates for $n$ Trotter steps.

</details>


### [54] [Data Free Training of Diffusion Models for Lattice Field Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18863)
*Dennis Grinwald,Ankur Singha,Khaled Kahouli,Karl Jansen,Shinichi Nakajima*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需参考马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（HMC）样本的数据自由训练扩散模型方法，利用目标分布的未归一化密度和目标分数恒等式（target score identity），通过重要性加权或无权重自洽固定点方案实现对格点φ⁴理论各相（对称、临界、破缺）的高效配置采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散模型依赖目标分布的真实样本进行去噪分数匹配训练，但在格点场论中获取独立平衡构型本身是计算瓶颈；因此需发展不依赖外部采样器的生成模型训练范式。

Method: 基于未归一化概率密度∇log p(x)，推导并应用目标分数恒等式，构建重要性加权（保证训练目标一致性）与无权重（形成自洽不动点迭代）两类损失函数；在2D φ⁴标量场模型上实施端到端扩散模型训练与采样。

Result: 所提数据自由训练策略在对称相、临界点及自发对称破缺相均实现稳定高效训练；采样质量与基于HMC参考数据训练的扩散模型相当，且避免了HMC预采样开销。

Conclusion: 扩散模型可通过仅访问作用量（即未归一化密度）实现自驱动训练，为格点量子场论提供了一种可扩展、免MCMC的生成建模新路径。

Abstract: Sampling equilibrium field configurations remains a major computational bottleneck in lattice field theory, motivating the development of generative samplers that can produce independent configurations. Diffusion models are promising for this purpose, but their standard denoising score matching objective requires samples from the target distribution, seemingly reintroducing the original sampling problem. Here, we show that diffusion models can instead be trained from their own generated samples using the target score identity, provided access to the unnormalized probability density. Importance-weighted formulations of this identity yield a consistent target throughout training, while unweighted variants define a self-consistent fixed-point training scheme. We benchmark the different approaches in two-dimensional \(φ^4\) theory and identify effective choices that enable efficient training and sampling across the symmetric, critical, and broken phases. Ultimately, our data-free training approach achieves comparable performance to training on HMC reference data.

</details>

Abstract: Many quantum gravity theories predict spacetime fluctuations at the Planck scale. Neutrinos, which interact only via the weak force and gravity, propagate over long distances without losing quantum coherence. Planck-scale fluctuations would spoil that coherence, damping the flavor oscillation probability over long baselines. This makes neutrinos sensitive interferometers for Planck-scale physics. We present a search for quantum decoherence using 368,071 atmospheric neutrinos detected by IceCube in the energy range 0.5-100 TeV, an order of magnitude higher in energy than any previous decoherence search. We find no evidence for decoherence and set the strongest constraints to date on this class of neutrino decoherence models, improving on previous limits by over an order of magnitude for decoherence rate scaling as $E^2$ and $E^3$. These results exclude natural Planck-suppressed decoherence for all energy scalings $E^n$ with $0\leq n \leq 3$, the first such exclusion at the Planck scale for $n=3$.

Zhelezov,S. Z. Zheng,X. Z. Zheng,Y. Zheng,T. Zhou,X. Zhou,V. Zhovkovska,L. Z. Zhu,X. Zhu,X. Zhu,Y. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo Gao,D. Zuliani,G. Zunica*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次测量了B⁰→K⁺π⁻μ⁺μ⁻衰变在Kπ不变质量区间1000–1835 MeV/c²内的微分分支比，基于LHCb 2011–2018年8.4 fb⁻¹数据，覆盖q² ∈ [0.1, 18.0] GeV²/c⁴，并首次在此Kπ质量窗内观测到J/ψ以上区域的该衰变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验b → sℓ⁺ℓ⁻过程的有效耦合是否偏离标准模型预言，特别是此前在B → K(*)μ⁺μ⁻等道中观测到的LFU与角分布异常。

Method: 利用LHCb探测器采集的pp碰撞数据（∫ℒ = 8.4 fb⁻¹），通过拟合K⁺π⁻μ⁺μ⁻四体末态的不变质量谱与背景成分，提取微分分支比d²ℬ/(dq² dm_{Kπ})，采用sPlot或最大似然拟合抑制组合背景，并对效率、探测器响应与强子初态辐射进行校准。

Result: 首次给出B⁰→K⁺π⁻μ⁺μ⁻在m_{Kπ} ∈ [1000, 1835] MeV/c²区间内随q²和m_{Kπ}变化的微分分支比；在q² ∈ [1.1, 6.0] GeV²/c⁴子区间内完成m_{Kπ}二维微分测量；首次在该m_{Kπ}窗内、J/ψ共振峰之上（q² > 9.3 GeV²/c⁴）明确观测到该衰变信号（>5σ）。

Conclusion: 该测量为b → sμ⁺μ⁻跃迁提供了新的强子化敏感探针，有助于区分新物理贡献与QCD强子效应（如Kπ共振结构、非局域强子真空极化），并为未来在B→Kπℓ⁺ℓ⁻道中开展CP不对称性与角分析奠定基础。

Abstract: This Letter presents the first measurement of the $B^{0} \to K^{+}π^{-}μ^{+}μ^{-}$ differential branching fraction in the region of the dihadron $K^+π^-$ mass, $m_{Kπ}$, $1000< m_{Kπ} < 1835\,\text{MeV}/c^{2}$. This decay mode is mediated by the quark-level transition $b \to s\ell^{+}\ell^{-}$, for which the measured effective couplings show tensions with Standard Model predictions. The analysis is performed using proton-proton collision data collected with the LHCb detector between 2011 and 2018, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $8.4\,\text{fb}^{-1}$. The differential branching fraction is determined across different regions of dimuon mass squared, $q^2$, in the interval $0.1 < q^2 < 18.0\,\text{GeV}^2/c^{4}$, as well as in different regions of $ m_{Kπ}$ in the interval $1.1 < q^2 < 6.0\,\text{GeV}^2/c^{4}$. The results represent the first differential measurement of the branching fraction across $m_{Kπ}$ within the selected dihadron-mass region, and the first observation of the decay within this region above the $J/ψ$ resonance.

</details>

Zhukov,J. Zhuo Gao,T. Zies,D. Zuliani,X. Zuo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次在LHCb上对轻夸克富集喷注中的能量-能量关联函数（EEC）进行了测量，涵盖全包含、同号和异号带电强子对；结果支持轻夸克喷注的禁闭尺度具有跨对撞系统与喷注横动量尺度的普适性，且异号强子对在小角度下的增强行为被蒙特卡洛模拟良好再现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究喷注形成中微扰与非微扰QCD区域的过渡行为，检验禁闭尺度在不同碰撞系统和喷注横动量尺度下的普适性。

Method: 基于LHCb探测器采集的质子-质子碰撞数据，测量轻夸克富集喷注中带电强子对的动量加权角关联——即能量-能量关联函数（EEC），并按电荷组合（全包含、同号、异号）分类分析。

Result: 观测到异号强子对在小角度（~Λ_QCD尺度）下EEC显著增强；禁闭尺度在不同喷注p_T与对撞系统间保持一致；蒙特卡洛模拟（含强子化模型）能定性乃至定量复现实验结果。

Conclusion: 轻夸克喷注的禁闭动力学具有普适性；EEC是检验强子化模型与非微扰QCD机制的高灵敏探针；电荷依赖的EEC为区分夸克味与强子化机制提供了新观测量。

Abstract: Energy-energy correlators probe perturbative and nonperturbative QCD regimes of jet formation through momentum-weighted angular correlations between hadrons. First measurements in light-quark-enriched jets are presented for inclusive, like-sign, and opposite-sign charged-hadron pairs, using proton-proton collisions at LHCb. The results support universality of the confinement scale for light-quark-initiated jets across collision systems and the reported transverse momentum scale of the jet. In the charge-dependent measurements, opposite-sign correlations are enhanced as angular separation decreases down to the confinement-scale region, a behavior that is well described by simulations.

</details>


### [58] [Forward decay chain reconstruction with displaced vertices in intense magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14666)
*Sergio Javier Jaimes Elles,Simone Libralon,Fernando Martinez Vidal,Nicola Neri,Miguel Rebollo de Miguel,Izaac Sanderswood,Volodymyr Svintozelskyi,Giorgia Tonani,Javier Velilla Serna,Ziyi Wang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种独立、模块化且可扩展的衰变树重建框架，专为处理复杂衰变拓扑设计，尤其适用于LHCb实验中强非均匀磁场区域内的长距离轨迹外推与顶点重建；该C++框架在效率、不变质量分辨率和顶点位置分辨率上显著优于现有工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确重建粒子衰变链对味物理与谱学分析至关重要，而现有工具难以应对强非均匀磁场、有限动量分辨及长距离轨迹外推等挑战，尤其在LHCb中顶点距相互作用点数米远的高迁移度衰变场景下。

Method: 开发了基于C++的模块化衰变树重建框架，支持约束性粒子衰变建模、非均匀磁场中的轨迹外推（采用精确场地图与数值积分）、顶点拟合与协方差传播，并兼容标准拟合近似与通用探测器几何。

Result: 在模拟数据基准测试中，该框架相较现有工具显著提升了衰变链重建效率、不变质量分辨率（Δm/m）与顶点位置分辨率（σ_x, σ_y, σ_z），尤其对高度位移衰变（cτ ≳ 10 mm）效果突出。

Conclusion: 该框架为高精度味物理测量与新型长寿命粒子搜索提供了鲁棒、高效且可移植的重建基础设施，已在LHCb软件栈中集成并支持未来升级。

Abstract: Precise reconstruction of particle decay chains is essential for a broad range of analyses in particle physics experiments, particularly those targeting flavour physics and spectroscopy. We present a standalone, modular and extensible decay tree reconstruction framework designed to handle complicated topologies, accounting for tightly constrained particle decays and long-distance trajectory extrapolations through regions of intense and inhomogeneous magnetic field, as well as limited momentum resolution. Implemented in C++, the framework is optimised for configurability and performance. Developed primarily within CERN's LHCb experiment, its focus is on decay chains with vertices located several metres from the LHC proton-proton interaction point, where the dipole magnetic field is maximal and non-uniform. Even so, it remains flexible: it can accommodate these special cases as well as the approximations commonly used in standard decay tree fitting tools, and the geometries of general-purpose collider detectors. Benchmark studies using simulated data demonstrate significant improvements in reconstruction efficiency, invariant mass resolution, and vertex position resolution with respect to existing tools, opening new opportunities for precision measurements and searches involving highly displaced decays.

</details>


### [59] [Exploring Collective Effects in Small Collision Systems via Light-Flavour Particle Production in High-Multiplicity pp Collisions at $\sqrt{s}=$ 13 TeV in ALICE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14851)
*Rajendra Nath Patra for the ALICE Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文分析了ALICE实验在13 TeV高多重数pp碰撞中轻味强子（π, K, p）的产生，研究其pT谱、粒子比及平均横动量随多重数的演化，并与Pb-Pb、p-Pb及理论模型（PYTHIA 8, EPOS4）对比，以探究小系统中集体效应的起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高多重数pp碰撞中类QGP信号（如径向流、各向异性流、奇异强子增强）是否源于类似重离子碰撞中的夸克胶子等离子体形成，抑或由其他机制（如色玻璃凝聚、多部分子相互作用）导致。

Method: 基于ALICE Run 2数据，对√s = 13 TeV高多重数pp碰撞中π±、K±、p(¯p)的微分横动量谱、pT积分产额比（如K/π, p/π）、平均横动量⟨pT⟩进行多重数依赖性分析，并与Pb-Pb、p-Pb数据及PYTHIA 8（无集体流）和EPOS4（含初始状态涨落与流）模型比较。

Result: 观测到HM pp中⟨pT⟩随多重数增长、K/π与p/π比在中等pT区升高、pT谱硬化等趋势，与Pb-Pb和p-Pb系统定性一致；EPOS4能较好描述数据，而PYTHIA 8显著偏离，表明小系统中存在非微扰集体动力学效应。

Conclusion: 高多重数pp碰撞中轻味强子产额与谱的系统性演化支持其动力学与大系统存在共性，暗示QGP样介质可能在小系统中短暂形成，但需结合更精细观测量（如v2、两粒子关联）进一步验证其流体性质与初始几何响应。

Abstract: High-multiplicity pp and p-Pb collisions show signatures of radial and anisotropic flow, as well as the enhanced production of strange hadrons. These observations resemble phenomena seen in heavy-ion collisions, where a quark-gluon plasma (QGP) is formed, although their origin in small systems remains under discussion. This contribution presents ALICE Run 2 results on light-flavour hadron ($π$, K, p) production in high-multiplicity (HM) pp collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV, where the charged-particle multiplicity reaches values comparable to peripheral or semi-peripheral Pb-Pb collisions. The multiplicity dependence of $π$, K, and p transverse momentum spectra, $p_{\rm T}$-differential and $p_{\rm T}$-integrated particle ratios, and mean transverse momenta ($\langle p_{\rm T}\rangle$) is presented and compared with published results from other collision systems and energies, as well as with PYTHIA 8 and EPOS4 model calculations.

</details>


### [60] [Detecting photons in radiative Bhabha interaction with Si-strip and LYSO crystals at future e+e- colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15036)
*Chentao Bao,Hai Chen,Wang Chen,Zhujun Fang,Jiading Gong,Suen Hou,Xiaolin Kang,Mingyi Liu,Ming Qi,Liqing Qin,Weimin Song,Jie Wang,Yadi Wang,Lei Zhang,Linghua Zhang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出在超级陶粲工厂（STCF）上部署前向探测器以精确测量Bhabha散射过程，特别是识别辐射光子，从而验证QED高阶修正；通过BHLUMI/BHWIDE模拟与GEANT建模，评估了束流管中多重散射和轫致辐射带来的系统误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为下一代e+e-对撞机（如STCF和Higgs工厂）提供高精度光度测量手段，并利用前向Bhabha散射中辐射光子的探测来检验QED高阶微扰计算的可靠性。

Method: 基于BHLUMI和BHWIDE生成Bhabha事例，用GEANT进行前向探测器（含高分辨硅探测器与细粒度LYSO晶体）响应建模，定量分析束流管中多重散射与轫致辐射引入的系统不确定度。

Result: 证实该前向探测器具备分辨紧邻电子的辐射光子的能力，可支持优于10^{-3}量级的光度标定及辐射过程精密测量。

Conclusion: 所提出的前向探测器设计方案满足STCF对Bhabha散射事例中辐射光子识别与高阶QED修正验证的实验需求，为未来高精度电弱物理与光度测量奠定关键技术基础。

Abstract: Next-generation e+e-colliders have been proposed for the Super Tau Charm Facility covering center-of-mass energies sqrt(s) = 2-7 GeV, and for the Higgs Factories optimized for Z-pole physics and for HZ production. Luminosity can be determined by measuring forward Bhabha scattering. The QED cross-section calculation has reached a precision of better than 10^-3. Detecting radiative photons in Bhabha events provides a tool for validating higher-order corrections in theory. We propose the Forward Detector for detecting Bhabha scattering events at the STCF. The design uses high-resolution silicon detectors and finely segmented LYSO crystals to discriminate radiative photons from closely associated electrons. Bhabha scattering events are simulated with the BHLUMI and BHWIDE programs, and the detector is modeled using GEANT. Systematic uncertainties due to multiple scattering and bremsstrahlung in the beam pipe were evaluated. The potential for achieving precise measurements of radiative processes in Bhabha events is investigated.

</details>


### [61] [Measurements of the branching fractions of \boldmath $χ_{cJ}\toΣ^0\barΣ^0η$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15332)
*BESIII Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于BESIII实验采集的2712.4百万个ψ(3686)事例，首次观测到χcJ（J=0,1,2）→Σ⁰Σ̄⁰η衰变过程，显著性分别为7.2σ、5.7σ和6.7σ，并测得相应分支比；未在Σ⁰Σ̄⁰、Σ⁰η、Σ̄⁰η不变质量谱中发现明显结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验粲夸克偶素χcJ强子衰变机制，特别是含超子对（Σ⁰Σ̄⁰）与赝标介子（η）的末态，为理解QCD非微扰动力学及重夸克偶素衰变选择定则提供新实验约束。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上采集的(2712.4±14.3)×10⁶个ψ(3686)事例，通过χcJ由ψ(3686)辐射跃迁产生，重建Σ⁰→Λγ与η→γγ衰变链，采用事件计数、效率修正与系统误差评估方法提取信号强度与分支比。

Result: 首次观测到χc0、χc1、χc2→Σ⁰Σ̄⁰η衰变，显著性分别为7.2σ、5.7σ、6.7σ；测得分支比分别为(9.7±1.7±0.8)×10⁻⁵、(4.9±1.1±0.4)×10⁻⁵、(6.1±1.2±0.3)×10⁻⁵；Σ⁰Σ̄⁰、Σ⁰η、Σ̄⁰η不变质量谱无显著共振结构。

Conclusion: χcJ→Σ⁰Σ̄⁰η是允许的OZI压低但非禁戒的强衰变过程，其可观测性表明末态相互作用或手征动力学可能起重要作用；分支比的相对大小（ℬ(χc0)>ℬ(χc2)>ℬ(χc1)）与自旋依赖的耦合结构一致，支持p-wave主导的衰变机制。

Abstract: Using $(2712.4\pm14.3)\times10^{6}$ $ψ(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector at the BEPCII collider, we report the first observations of the decay $χ_{cJ}(J=0,1,2) \to Σ^0\barΣ^0η$. The signal significances, after considering systematic uncertainties, are determined to be 7.2$σ$, 5.7$σ$, and 6.7$σ$ for $χ_0$, $χ_{c1}$, and $χ_{c2}$, respectively. The branching fractions are measured to be $\mathcal B(χ_{c0}\toΣ^0\barΣ^0η)=({9.7 \pm 1.7 \pm 0.8}) \times 10^{-5}$, $\mathcal B(χ_{c1}\toΣ^0\barΣ^0η)=({4.9 \pm 1.1 \pm 0.4}) \times 10^{-5}$, and $\mathcal B(χ_{c2}\toΣ^0\barΣ^0η)=({6.1 \pm 1.2 \pm 0.3}) \times 10^{-5}$, where the first uncertainties are statistical and the second systematic. No significant structure in the $Σ^0\barΣ^0,Σ^0η,\barΣ^0η$ mass distributions is observed under the current statistics.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用BESIII探测器在3.510–4.946 GeV质心能量区间采集的43.8 fb⁻¹数据，首次测量了e⁺e⁻ → p̄nπ⁻（及其电荷共轭）过程的Born截面；未发现ψ(3770)、Y(4230)等矢量粲偶素/类粲偶素态对此末态的显著贡献，并给出了各共振态Γ_ee × ℬ衰变分支的90%置信度上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索矢量粲偶素及类粲偶素态（如Y态）向重子-反重子-介子末态p̄nπ⁻ + c.c.的强衰变，检验QCD多夸克态模型与重子产生机制，特别是非微扰QCD中重子对产生的动力学。

Conclusion: p̄nπ⁻ + c.c.末态并非上述矢量粲偶素/类粲偶素态的主要衰变道，其分支比远低于典型强子衰变（~1%）；结果对多夸克态模型（如分子态、四夸克态）预言的重子对耦合强度构成强约束，支持该末态由连续态或非共振QCD机制主导。

The energy dependence of the dressed cross sections is investigated to search for possible contributions from vector charmonium and charmonium-like states. No evidence for contributions from the $ψ(3770)$, $Y(4230)$, $Y(4360)$, $ψ(4415)$, $Y(4500)$, or $Y(4710)$ resonances is observed. The upper limits at the 90\% confidence level are determined for the products of the electronic partial widths and the branching fractions for the decays of these resonances into the $p \bar{n} π^- + \mathrm{c.c.}$ final state.

</details>

Hardware tracking results from a test with high-rate muons at the CERN M2 beam are presented. This test, a joint effort by the MUonE and Compact Muon Solenoid (CMS) experiments, operated with Two-Strip (2S) modules provided by the CMS tracker group. Abstract: Supervised machine learning in high-energy physics (HEP) requires training on labeled simulations of particle collisions, which are known to be unfaithful to the underlying physics. This leads to significant inaccuracies and biases in the transfer of trained models to real data, limiting the power of deep learning methods. Self-supervised learning (SSL) is a promising alternative for pretraining directly on real, unlabeled data. However, existing techniques largely operate with inductive biases inspired by domains such as images and natural language. We propose RINO (Renormalization Group DIstillation with NO Labels), which, for the first time, exploits the fundamentally multi-scale renormalization group structure of high-energy jet formation as a physically grounded source of SSL views. We define teacher-student representations across energy scales using different levels of hierarchical clustering, aligned via DINO-style self-distillation. To mirror the background-dominated nature of real collider data, RINO pretrains exclusively on jets from quantum chromodynamics (QCD) processes and is evaluated on out-of-distribution (OOD) transfer to rare signal classes. In our controlled data-efficiency study, RINO matches fully supervised training on JetNet after finetuning on only 1% of the labeled data. With the full labeled dataset, RINO improves OOD classification accuracy on top-quark jets by 5.9% and, without retraining, improves AUC on the unseen Higgs boson decay channel $H \to 4q$ by 4.1% over the strongest SSL baseline. Scale-structure diagnostics reveal that DINO-style self-distillation permits our backbone to retain scale-dependent structure. Ablation studies show that this accounts for an 8.6% OOD accuracy gap with respect to alternative SSL paradigms such as SimCLR. Code is available at https://github.com/zichunhao/RINO.

Abstract: High-pileup conditions in CMS during the HL-LHC era make charged-particle tracking increasingly challenging as detector occupancy and combinatorics grow. We present a hybrid approach that exploits Line Segment Tracking (LST) objects rather than individual hits to enable the first CMS ML-based track reconstruction algorithm. The LST objects, namely the triplets used in this study, are built according to geometry- and physics-driven criteria and carry richer local structure, geometrical cues, and physics quantities such as transverse momentum, which helps the model incorporate detector-level information from the start. We apply a Transformer encoder with geometrically masked sparse self-attention to embed these objects in a learned latent space, and use object condensation to assemble full tracks in a single inference step. This object-based representation reduces complexity in dense environments and makes the model less sensitive to ambiguities introduced by high pileup. Evaluated on CMS Phase-2 simulation of $t\bar{t}$ events at $\langle μ\rangle = 200$, the approach achieves reconstruction efficiency comparable to the LST quintuplet baseline while reducing the fake rate by roughly a factor of three and suppressing duplicates almost entirely, demonstrating the promise of advanced ML architectures for next-generation particle tracking.

We discuss the resulting physics opportunities, with a focus on inclusive $b\to s\ell\ell$ measurements and on searches for exotic hadrons.

Abstract: Tests of lepton-flavour universality in semileptonic \(B\)-meson decays provide sensitive probes of physics beyond the Standard Model. The Belle and Belle II experiments, respectively operating on the KEKB and SuperKEKB colliders in Japan, have collected \(e^+e^-\to B\bar{B}\) collisions at a center-of-mass energy corresponding to the \(Υ(4S)\) resonance in samples with a combined integrated luminosity exceeding \(1\,\mathrm{ab}^{-1}\). New results on the ratios of semitauonic \(B\)-decay rates compared to analogous light-lepton rates are presented. These include new Belle II measurements of branching-fraction ratios \(R(D^{(*)})\) involving exclusive \(B\to D^{(*)}τν\) and \(B\to D^{(*)}\ellν\) decays using semileptonic or hadronic reconstruction of the accompanying \(B\)-meson tag. Abstract: Muons have fascinated physicists since their discovery in cosmic rays almost a century ago. Here, we experimentally demonstrate for the first time that muon scattering tomography can be extended to biological soft tissue. We image an anthropomorphic thorax phantom using a 2 GeV/c muon beam at the CERN Proton Synchrotron and reconstruct its three-dimensional scattering density from the measured deflections of individual muons. Result: 利用2024年pp对撞数据，在90 ms积累窗口内实现luminous region横向位置统计精度≈4 μm/坐标；模拟验证纵向中心测量精度达数毫米；可配置固件计数器方案在模拟中使横、纵向分辨率均提升至基线的三倍。

Conclusion: 该trackless实时监测方法具备亚微米级横向灵敏度、毫秒级响应、零轨迹重建依赖及可扩展固件架构，为LHCb Run 4高亮度运行下的束流-探测器协同校准提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present a real-time trackless method to monitor the centre of the LHCb luminous region and the relative alignment of the two halves of the LHCb Vertex Locator, using cluster counts accumulated in selected regions of the detector and processed in firmware. A Principal Component Analysis performed on simulated data yields a set of weight vectors that reduce the position estimators to scalar products of the counter rates, executable with negligible computational overhead. With pp collision data from 2024, the transverse position of the luminous region is reconstructed with a statistical precision of approximately 4 micrometre per coordinate in a 90 millisecond accumulation window. A measurement of the longitudinal centre is demonstrated with a statistical precision of a few millimetres in simulated studies. The introduction of run-time configurable firmware counters has been studied in simulation to improve the transverse and longitudinal resolutions by a factor of three with respect to the fixed-geometry baseline.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文对BESIII实验采集的ψ(3686)辐射衰变中产生的π⁰π⁰系统进行了模型无关的振幅分析，提取了标量与张量分波随不变质量M_inv(π⁰π⁰)的依赖关系，确认f₂(1270)显著存在，未发现其他张量共振结构；标量分波行为与J/ψ辐射衰变结果一致；结果以对数似然扫描形式给出，便于后续模型构建与数据联合分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过模型无关方法刻画π⁰π⁰末态的强子动力学，规避理论模型偏差，为理解低能QCD、标量介子谱（如f₀(500)、f₀(980)）及辐射跃迁中的手征对称性实现提供关键实验约束。

Method: 基于2.71×10⁹个ψ(3686)衰变事例，采用部分波展开（PWA）结合最大似然拟合，在M_inv(π⁰π⁰)上分箱进行模型无关振幅重建；显式参数化标量（J^P=0⁺）和张量（J^P=2⁺）分波幅度为分段连续函数，不预设Breit-Wigner或耦合道形式；系统误差通过协方差矩阵在似然函数中严格处理。

Result: 标量分波在400–1100 MeV区间呈现宽峰结构，与J/ψ→γπ⁰π⁰中观测一致；张量分波仅在~1270 MeV处显示显著f₂(1270)信号，其余区域无统计显著的共振结构；所有结果以每M_inv(π⁰π⁰)箱的Δlnℒ扫描图呈现，并附带完整统计与系统不确定性。

Conclusion: ψ(3686)→γπ⁰π⁰过程的标量分波具有普适性特征，支持其源于非微扰QCD机制；张量部分严格限定了f₂(1270)以外的2⁺态贡献上限；该模型无关结果构成构建统一手征拉氏量或耦合道模型的关键输入。

Abstract: A model-independent amplitude analysis of the $π^{0} π^{0}$ system produced in radiative $ψ(3686)$ decays is presented. The analysis is based on the unique dataset of $(2712.4\pm14.3)\times10^6$ $ψ(3686)$ decays collected by the BESIII detector. Scalar and tensor partial-wave contributions are obtained as piecewise functions of $M_{\text{inv}}(π^{0} π^{0})$ with minimal model assumptions. The observed contribution of the scalar partial wave exhibits similar behavior to the one reported in the study of radiative $J/ψ$ decays, while the tensor contributions provide no evidence for resonance-like structures except for the clearly visible $f_2(1270)$. The results are presented in the form of log-likelihood scans in $M_{\text{inv}}(π^{0} π^{0})$ bins, which preserve all the statistical information and account for relevant systematic uncertainties. The model-independent description provided in this paper allows to combine the presented results with other related data in the development of phenomenological models.

</details>


### [72] [Probing the proton structure with minimum bias and low-pt dimuon events at hadron colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18685)
*Kyle Cormier*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出两种利用双缪子产生和最小偏置事件探测质子结构及质子-质子碰撞中部分子相互作用数目的方法，旨在提取平均部分子散射数、其对动量分数x的依赖以及可能的部分子味信息，并在简单几何模型下推断质子-质子重叠对碰撞参数及x的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解质子内部结构及多重部分子相互作用（MPI）机制，特别是在LHC高能pp碰撞中MPI的发生率及其对运动学变量（如x）和部分子味的依赖。

Method: 基于双缪子末态和最小偏置事件的实验观测量，结合准独立多重散射的几何模型，提取平均部分子散射数⟨N_{MPI}⟩及其对x和碰撞参数b的依赖。

Result: 可获得不同事件类别下的平均部分子散射数、其x依赖性，以及在几何MPI模型下对质子-质子横截面重叠函数（即b-和x-依赖）的约束。

Conclusion: 该方法为MPI建模提供关键实验约束，有助于改进强子化模拟（如PYTHIA、HERWIG）中的MPI参数化，并深化对质子横向结构与纵向动量分布耦合的理解。

Abstract: This paper presents two methods for probing the proton structure and the number of partonic interactions in proton-proton collisions using dimuon production and minimum bias events. At the LHC these targeted measurements can provide information on the average number of partonic scatters in different event classes, their dependence on the partonic momentum fraction, x, and potentially partonic flavour. Within a simple mostly geometric model of multiparton interactions as quasi-independent scatters, the measurements can be used to make inference about the proton-proton overlap dependence on impact parameter and its partonic-x dependence.

</details>

### [73] [Energy, Passivity, and Diffusive Representations in Anisotropic Fractional Maxwell--Cole--Cole Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13985)
*Nikko John Leo S. Lobos*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了各向异性Maxwell-Cole-Cole介质的分数阶极化能量理论框架，引入加权解空间刻画极化初层，严格分解极化功率为历史储能与本构耗散两部分，证明其电磁无源性、能量衰减性与零激励下的条件唯一性，并统一涵盖Debye等经典极限；同时给出正定扩散表征及与历史储能的方差型差量，验证其在时域与频域（Cole-Cole介电函数）的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立满足热力学相容性（如无源性、能量守恒）的多通道分数阶介电模型，克服传统分数阶本构在初值处理、能量解释与物理可实现性方面的理论缺陷。

Method: 采用Caputo型分数阶导数描述多通道极化响应，构造带权重的解空间以容纳可积的分数阶初始层；通过能量泛函变分与Poynting定理耦合，导出全局场-记忆联合能量恒等式；引入正定扩散表示实现第二能量分解，并定义加权方差度量其与历史储能的偏差；结合频域渐近分析与单通道数值验证。

Result: 1) 极化功率被严格分解为非负的历史储能率与本构耗散率；2) 建立全局场-记忆能量恒等式，保证电磁无源性、闭系统能量单调衰减及零激励下解的条件唯一性；3) 恢复Debye、瞬时介电、零磁化率与真空等经典极限；4) 给出正定扩散表示及其与历史储能的非负加权方差；5) 证明有限时谐响应收敛至各向异性多通道Cole-Cole介电函数，且其厄米虚部保证周期平均吸收非负；6) 单通道算例证实分数阶阶次拓宽介电损耗带宽、增强初层功率可积性并延长效应尾部。

Conclusion: 该能量公式为线性各向异性分数阶介电介质提供了热力学自洽的本构建模范式，统一协调了分数阶初层、记忆储能、无源性、扩散松弛、色散、吸收与衰减等核心物理概念，兼具数学严谨性与物理可实现性。

Abstract: This study develops an energy formulation for linear anisotropic Maxwell--Cole--Cole media with multiple fractional polarization channels, passive conductivity, prescribed currents, and finite-terminal Caputo memory with zero prehistory. A weighted solution class captures the integrable fractional initial layer generated by quiescent polarization. Fractional polarization power then separates into a nonnegative history storage and a nonnegative constitutive dissipation rate. Although the instantaneous rate may diverge at initialization, it remains integrable, while stored history energy and accumulated loss vanish initially. Combined with the Poynting theorem, this decomposition yields a global field-plus-memory identity that establishes electromagnetic passivity, closed-system energy decay, and conditional uniqueness under zero-supply boundary conditions. The framework also recovers the Debye, instantaneous-dielectric, vanishing-susceptibility, and vacuum limits under convergence assumptions. A positive diffusive representation provides a second decomposition whose storage never exceeds the history storage. Their difference is an exact nonnegative weighted variance that vanishes only for a zero polarization history. In agreement with these time-domain results, the finite-time harmonic response approaches the anisotropic multichannel Cole--Cole permittivity, whose Hermitian imaginary part ensures nonnegative cycle-averaged absorption. A reproducible single-channel study shows that fractional order broadens the dielectric-loss band, redistributes wave attenuation, strengthens the integrable initial power layer, and prolongs the step-response tail. An exact electric-step response verifies both energy representations. A comprehensive treatment of symmetries then leads to multisymplectic and instantaneous momentum maps generalizations and to several versions of Noether's theorems.

Abstract: Non-intrusive reduced-order modeling is essential for systems simulated with black- or gray-box solvers, yet computing accurate data-driven models remains challenging when the dynamics exhibit large-amplitude transient energy growth. This is because state-of-the-art non-intrusive methods typically rely on orthogonal projection onto high-energy linear or nonlinear manifolds identified by minimizing static reconstruction losses, which truncates low-energy, yet dynamically important, states. Intrusive methods such as Balanced Truncation and its extensions address this issue by using adjoint information to define dynamics-aware oblique projections. Here, we show that balancing-based oblique projections for nonlinear systems can instead be computed non-intrusively from forward simulation data. Specifically, we develop a framework to compute the nonlinear empirical reachability Gramian and balance it against its observability counterpart, projected onto a suitable restriction subspace. This procedure provably recovers the oblique projection of Balanced Truncation when the dynamics are linear and the range of either Gramian is contained in the restriction subspace. Moreover, in the linear case and in the absence of adjoint information, we show that the minimax-optimal restriction subspace spans the dominant eigenspaces of the reachability Gramian, which informs an efficient balancing algorithm with cost comparable to a standard SVD. Once the oblique projection is computed, we use Operator Inference to learn the latent-space dynamics on its range. The method is demonstrated on several examples of increasing complexity, including the three-dimensional incompressible flow past a wing with $8\times 10^7$ degrees of freedom. One gap family closes when the two sublattices become equivalent. In this symmetric limit, however, the primitive period halves, so the apparent band crossing results from Brillouin-zone folding. A different family closes at a critical but genuinely asymmetric configuration. Because the primitive period is preserved, adjacent bands then meet at the center of the original Brillouin zone in a genuine massless Dirac-like cone. Near either critical limit, a small finite gap acts as an effective Dirac mass: the dispersion remains quadratic near a band edge but crosses over to a massive Dirac-like form beyond that narrow region. The band curvatures define effective electron and hole masses. Exact Bloch eigenfunctions further reveal that isospectral lattices can display different single-particle probability densities. This model therefore provides an analytically controlled one-dimensional setting in which conventional quadratic carriers, massive Dirac-like band dispersions, and a tunable massless contact coexist within the same singular lattice.

Nonlinear Differ. Equ. Appl. 13, Birkhäuser, 1994.] and generalize it to polygons with sharp corners. The renormalized energy is fully defined by the triplet, $(\mathbf{a},\mathbf{c}, \mathbf{d}$), of topological degrees of the interior defects ($\mathbf{d}$) and the winding number contribution of polygon vertices ($\mathbf{c}$) and interior defect locations ($\mathbf{a}$), subject to sum rules dictated by the imposed Dirichlet boundary condition. We use the critical points of the renormalized energy to construct initial conditions for the rLdG solver, recover known energy-minimizing and non-energy minimizing saddle points of the rLdG energy on a disc, regular hexagon and rectangle along with the discovery of previously unreported rLdG solution branches. The results hold at the Poisson coalgebra level and hence apply to any realizations which preserve the defining brackets and coproducts. Canonical symplectic realizations give rise to Hamiltonians in spaces of non-constant curvature. An exact two-block identity, proved directly in the localized Poisson algebra without appealing to a symplectic realization, controls the additional Stäckel integrals. In three dimensions, it generates a quadratic Poisson block with its own Casimir relation; in four dimensions, its coupling to the full left/right coalgebra family is organized into three nested quadratic blocks, and the same identity extends this nested coupling to arbitrary $N$. Explicit higher-site partial Casimirs and factorized bracket generators are collected in the appendices.

Abstract: We present a self-contained, algorithmic construction of almost invariant subspaces [Panati et al. (2003), De Nittis and Panati (2010), Freund and Teufel (2016)] for Hofstadter-like Hamiltonians $H_b $ acting on $\ell^2(\mathbb{Z}^2,\mathbb{C}^n)$. More precisely, for rational values of $b_0/(2π)$ we describe an iterative procedure that takes a ``locally gapped" projection $P_{0}$ (possibly topologically non-trivial) which commutes with $H_{b_0}$, into a projection $P_{\varepsilon}$ that commutes with $H_{b_0+\varepsilon}$ up to an error of order $\varepsilon^\infty$.

Moreover, its integrated density of states varies linearly with $\varepsilon$ (the ``Středa formula"). We also show that any spatially localized Parseval frame of $P_{0}$ can be mapped into to a localized Parseval frame of $P_{\varepsilon}$.

Abstract: This paper develops a Willis-type thermal homogenization framework for two-dimensional particulate composites under harmonic heat transfer and establishes a unique effective constitutive relation without introducing residual fields. The local thermal fields are obtained using the dual-reciprocity inclusion-based boundary element method, in which two eigen-fields are introduced to simulate the mismatches in thermal conductivity and heat capacity. Instead of retrieving effective kernels from several independent macroscopic solutions generated by residual fields, the complete constitutive operator is constructed upon relations between eigen-fields and generalized macroscopic responses. The effective constitutive relations are validated against a one-dimensional analytical solution and an extended Fourier-series homogenization scheme. The Fourier formulae further establish the opposite-wavenumber reciprocity relations between two coupling tensors and reflection-symmetry parity relations, which explain when transverse coupling components vanish and the sign reversal of off-diagonal conductivity components under reflection. Numerical results further demonstrate that the de-phased average is required to extract physically consistent effective thermal properties under the Bloch conditions, and reveal their nonlocal frequency/wavenumber-dependent behavior. The framework provides a general computational method for Willis-type homogenization of particulate composites and is extendable to three-dimensional, multiphysical problems.

Abstract: We prove that for $k=4$ the quantum uniformity measures are norms, whereas for every $k\geq5$ the triangle inequality fails. Combined with the continuum limit established in a companion paper, this yields exponential $L^2$ convergence to equilibrium for the $Φ^4_2$ dynamics and for sine-Gordon with $β^2<2π$. For $Φ^4_2$, we verify that in a weak-coupling, small-mass regime, optimizing the damping yields a kinetic relaxation rate of at least order $m$. This yields a genuine acceleration over the order-$m^2$ relaxation rate of the overdamped dynamics. Abstract: The dynamical formation of a complete Bose-Einstein condensate and the asymptotic purification of trapped bosons coupled to a quantized radiation field are rigorously established. By tracking the macroscopic one-particle density matrix $κ_T$ through a nonlinear resonance cascade, it is demonstrated that radiative relaxation irreversibly depletes all excited trap modes, even when the initial macroscopic state $κ_0$ is genuinely mixed. Here $κ_T$ is an average, with respect to a de Finetti measure $μ_0$, over mean-field particle-field orbits, so that the mixedness of $κ_0$ reflects a statistical ensemble of orbits. Under the assumptions of a non-vanishing initial ground state component and strictly positive downward Fermi-Golden-Rule rates, strong trace-norm convergence to a pure condensate is proven, $$
  \lim_{T\to\infty} \| κ_T-|χ_0\rangle\langleχ_0| \|_{\mathfrak{S}_1(\mathfrak{h})} = 0 \, . $$ Furthermore, the von Neumann entropy of the macroscopic one-particle density matrix is shown to vanish asymptotically, $$
  \lim_{T\to\infty}S[κ_T]=0 \, , $$ without any claim of monotonicity in $T$. Thus, any initially mixed, positive-entropy state ($0<S[κ_0]<\infty$) is purified to the ground state $|χ_0\rangle\langleχ_0|$. For the proof, we derive the Hartree hierarchy of the coupled system of bosons and photons from the finite-$N$ BBGKY hierarchy of the microscopic quantum many-body system, establishing trace-norm convergence in the mean-field limit as $N\to\infty$. The limits are taken successively; first $N\to\infty$, then the weak-coupling limit, and finally $T\to\infty$. Although the global particle-field evolution remains unitary, the macroscopic one-particle state converges to a pure state as energy and correlations are continuously radiated into the dispersive field.

Motivation: 理解强关联系统在非平衡态下的长时大尺度动力学行为，特别是有限温度下量子可积模型的动力学关联函数渐近性质，是广义流体动力学（GHD）和量子多体理论的核心问题之一。Fredholm行列式作为精确可解模型中关键的数学对象，其渐近分析亟需系统而严格的工具。

Method: 采用有效形式因子（effective form factors）方法，将动力学Fredholm行列式的渐近行为表达为一个有效相移的泛函；通过鞍点分析识别二次驻点，并解析构造其两侧的两个渐近分支及中间的过渡区域；过渡轮廓由Weber方程（即抛物柱函数）精确刻画；结合高温极限下与高斯β系综（β=2）的对应关系，确定归一化常数；并与Riemann–Hilbert问题的严格解进行交叉验证。

Result: 1) 得到有限温度下动力学Fredholm行列式的完整长时大距渐近展开，包含两个不同分支及其平滑过渡轮廓（parabolic-cylinder函数形式）；2) 通过高温极限与Gaussian Unitary Ensemble（GUE, β=2）的对应，唯一确定有限归一化常数；3) 主导指数项即足以精确复现GHD在Euler尺度下对硬杆模型动力学结构因子S(k,ω)的预测，证实了GHD的微观基础。

Conclusion: 有效形式因子方法为可积系统有限温度动力学提供了统一、解析且可推广的渐近分析框架；过渡轮廓的抛物柱函数结构揭示了普遍临界行为；结果不仅严格支持广义流体动力学的Euler尺度预言，也建立了量子动力学、随机矩阵理论与非线性Steepest Descent/Riemann–Hilbert方法之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We study the long-time, large-distance asymptotics of finite-temperature dynamical Fredholm determinants using effective form factors. The method expresses these asymptotics as a functional of an effective phase shift. We determine its two branches on either side of a quadratic stationary point and the transition profile joining them. The profile is expressed through parabolic-cylinder functions. Its connection to the high-temperature Gaussian $β$ ensemble fixes the finite normalization, in agreement with an independent Riemann--Hilbert analysis. We also show that the leading exponential asymptotics suffice to recover the Euler-scale prediction of generalized hydrodynamics for the dynamical structure factor of hard rods.

</details>

### [97] [No-Go Theorem for Phantom Crossing in Scalar-Tensor Gravity with Non-Minimal Derivative Coupling and Soft Scale-Invariance Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14354)
*Reza Baghbani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了DESI 2024观测到的暗能量状态方程参数w在低红移穿越幽灵界（w < -1）的现象，是否可在尺度不变的含非最小导耦合（NMDC）标量-张量理论中实现；作者利用暗能量有效场论方法，推导了无鬼与梯度稳定性条件，并检验其与量子引力沼泽地猜想的相容性；最终证明三类严格不可行定理，表明此类模型无法容纳幽灵穿越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释DESI 2024观测到的w < -1（幽灵穿越）现象，并检验其是否可在理论自洽（稳定、符合量子引力约束）的尺度不变NMDC模型中实现。

Method: 采用暗能量有效场论（EFT of DE）框架，系统导出非最小导耦合标量-张量理论中无鬼条件（ghost-freedom）与梯度稳定性（gradient stability）；结合swampland中的de Sitter猜想与距离猜想，进行理论相容性分析；通过解析推导建立严格no-go定理。

Result: 得出三项rigorous no-go theorem：(i) 幽灵穿越要求有效耦合参数ξ_eff < 0，但无鬼性强制ξ_eff > 0；(ii) swampland de Sitter猜想与distance猜想各自独立排除幽灵穿越；(iii) 这两类约束相互强化。

Conclusion: 尺度不变的NMDC标量-张量理论无法容纳DESI所偏好的w < -1行为，该结论为超越此类模型的暗能量理论构建提供了坚实的理论边界。

Abstract: Recent baryon acoustic oscillation measurements from the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI 2024) reveal a preference for dynamical dark energy with an equation of state parameter crossing the phantom divide ($w < -1$) at low redshifts. We investigate whether this phantom crossing can be realized within scale-invariant scalar-tensor theories featuring non-minimal derivative coupling (NMDC) between the scalar field and the Einstein tensor. Employing the effective field theory of dark energy formalism, we systematically derive the ghost-free and gradient stability conditions, and examine their compatibility with the swampland conjectures from quantum gravity. We establish three rigorous no-go theorems demonstrating that: (i)~phantom crossing necessitates $ξ_{\text{eff}} < 0$, directly violating the no-ghost stability requirement $ξ_{\text{eff}} > 0$; (ii)~the swampland de Sitter and distance conjectures independently obstruct phantom crossing; and (iii)~these theoretical obstructions are mutually reinforcing. Our results show that scale-invariant NMDC models cannot account for the DESI preference for $w < -1$, providing robust theoretical guidance for dark energy model building beyond this class.

</details>


### [98] [Non-Stationary Wormholes and Matter Source Reconstruction in General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14357)
*Geová Alencar,Tiago Crispim,Roberta Dárlla,Ricardo Landim*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了嵌入于演化宇宙背景中的非定态虫洞几何，利用Simpson-Visser型时间坐标正则化与齐次Cuscuton场构造无初奇点的正则FLRW宇宙背景，并分析两类动力学虫洞（四维缺陷虫洞与Perry-Mann型拓扑荷虫洞）的物质支撑机制；结果表明：前者可由零势能标量场支持，后者需引入mimetic物质；进一步推广至可分离非定态时空时，导出强约束的标度条件，排除了在满足光滑性与反射对称性前提下构造正则、渐近平直虫洞的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服标准FLRW宇宙中初始奇点问题，并探索在正则、无奇点宇宙背景下能否自洽构建物理上可接受的非定态虫洞解。

Method: 采用(n+1)维FLRW度规的时间坐标Simpson-Visser正则化，结合齐次Cuscuton场实现尺度因子在t→0处有限非零；构造两类具体非定态虫洞度规（缺陷型与Perry-Mann型），通过Einstein方程与物质场运动方程分析其能量条件与源项结构；进而推广至一般可分离非定态时空，推导标度不变性所诱导的约束条件。

Result: 1) 四维缺陷虫洞可由无势能标量场支持；2) Perry-Mann型虫洞需mimetic物质支撑空间结构，Cuscuton主导宇宙演化；3) 可分离非定态情形下导出严格标度条件，导致最小耦合标量场与非线性电磁场失去静态重构中的函数自由度；4) 无法同时满足光滑性、反射对称性与渐近平直性以构造正则虫洞。

Conclusion: 非定态虫洞可在正则宇宙背景中被具体构造，但其存在高度依赖于特定几何与物质模型；广义构造受强动力学约束，尤其排除了满足基本物理合理性的渐近平直非定态虫洞解。

Abstract: We investigate non-stationary wormhole geometries in General Relativity embedded in evolving cosmological backgrounds. We employ a Simpson-Visser-like regularization of the temporal coordinate of the $(n+1)$-dimensional FLRW metric, together with a homogeneous Cuscuton field, to construct cosmological backgrounds in which the scale factor remains finite and non-zero at the would-be initial singularity. We then consider two specific dynamical wormhole geometries: the four-dimensional defect wormhole and the topologically charged Perry-Mann type wormhole. We show that the former can be supported by a canonical scalar field with vanishing potential, while the latter requires a mimetic matter sector to support its spatial structure, with the Cuscuton governing the cosmological evolution in both cases. We subsequently generalize the analysis to a separable non-stationary spacetime and derive a scaling condition that strongly constrains the admissible matter sources. In particular, for minimally coupled scalar and nonlinear electrodynamics fields, this condition removes the functional freedom available in static reconstruction schemes. We show that, within this framework, regular, asymptotically flat wormhole geometries cannot be obtained while simultaneously satisfying the required smoothness and reflection symmetry conditions. Our results illustrate both the possibility of constructing specific non-stationary wormholes in regular cosmological backgrounds and the restrictions that arise when attempting to generalize such constructions to arbitrary spatial geometries.

</details>


### [99] [Post-Newtonian secular dynamics of hierarchical triples II. Canonical derivation of the 1PN\,$\times$\,quadrupole cross terms and of the quadrupole-squared term](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14505)
*Hideyoshi Arakida*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过两阶段Lie-Hori-Deprit变换，从1PN ADM三体哈密顿量出发导出分层三体系统的久期哈密顿量，系统解决了Paper I中遗留的相对论交叉项问题；确认了1PN×单极、1PN×四极交叉项（含电/磁部分）及四极²阶久期哈密顿量的完整形式，并给出了高阶项对偏心率增强与并合时间的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Paper I中未闭合的相对论交叉项问题，特别是1PN与四极矩、八极矩等高阶引力多极展开之间的耦合效应，厘清不同理论框架（ADM、EIH、有效场论）在久期动力学中的等价性与参数映射关系。

Method: 基于1PN精度的ADM三体哈密顿量，在全正则变量下实施两阶段Lie-Hori-Deprit摄动变换；引入双参数记账规则（δ表1PN小参数，ε表层次性小参数），严格分离各阶贡献；利用Tremaine虚时间方法处理共振下的规范项消去。

Result: 1. 1PN单极相互作用等效于内轨道约化质量的束缚能重整化；2. δε^{3/2}阶无实物理项（否定了LR提出的libration项）；3. 1PN×四极电/磁交叉项与Kuntz-Serra-Trincherini的有效场论结果完全一致（磁项为首例独立推导）；4. 给出下一阶1PN×四极项闭式解，显含外轨道近心点幅角ω₂；5. 四极²阶久期哈密顿量完备，其2ω₂谐波为可消去的规范项；6. EIH与ADM在1PN阶等价，仅轨道根数定义不同，给出振荡-接触映射关系；7. 数值积分表明，ω₂依赖项使八极驱动的偏心率增强达Paper I的2–3倍，但LR所报加速并合现象未再现。

Conclusion: 本文建立了分层三体系统在1PN+四极+八极精度下的自洽久期理论框架，统一了多种相对论引力表述，澄清了关键争议项的物理存在性与数值权重，为致密天体并合率建模提供了更可靠的摄动基准。

Abstract: We derive the secular Hamiltonian of a hierarchical triple from the 1PN ADM three-body Hamiltonian by a two-stage Lie-Hori-Deprit transformation carried out entirely in canonical variables, and use it to settle the relativistic cross terms left open in Paper I. A bookkeeping rule enumerates all contributions to each order in the 1PN parameter $δ$ and the hierarchy parameter $\varepsilon$. The 1PN monopole interaction reduces to a renormalization of the inner mass by its binding energy, and the order $δ\varepsilon^{3/2}$, at which Lim and Rodriguez (LR) found a libration term, is empty. The electric and magnetic 1PN $\times$ quadrupole cross terms agree exactly with the effective-field-theory result of Kuntz, Serra, and Trincherini; for the magnetic term this is the first independent derivation. We give the next-to-leading order 1PN $\times$ quadrupole term in closed form; it depends on the outer argument of pericenter $ω_2$. At quadrupole-squared order we obtain the complete secular Hamiltonian, which generates the secular equations of Will and of Conway and Will; its $2ω_2$ harmonic is a gauge term, removable even on the commensurability between the ZLK period and the nodal precession, by a generator given in closed form via Tremaine's fictitious time. The EIH and ADM descriptions coincide at 1PN order; the formulations differ only in the definition of the elements, for which we give the osculating-contact map. In secular integrations of LR's triples, the eccentricity enhancement with the octupole is controlled by the $ω_2$-dependent part of the next-to-leading order term and is two to three times that of Paper I, while LR's accelerated mergers remain absent.

</details>


### [100] [Stability conditions for light rings in circular stationary axisymmetric spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14747)
*Jake O. Shipley*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究四维定常轴对称时空中的光环（light ring）稳定性，利用零测地线的哈密顿形式与Killing对称性，将横向Hessian简化为纯曲率表达式，其本征值由Ricci标量Φ₀₀和Weyl标量Ψ₀给出；稳定性条件为Φ₀₀ > |Ψ₀|，等价于严格零能量条件（SNEC）主导局部潮汐剪切；该判据不依赖Weyl张量迹，仅需验证SNEC是否被违反；应用于真空、电真空及标量场模型，得出稳定光环在实标量场中被禁止，但在具谐波Killing依赖的复标量场中可存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强引力场中光子轨道（即光环）的稳定性对黑洞阴影观测、引力波回音、强场唯象学及黑洞唯一性/稳定性理论至关重要；现有判据多依赖具体度规解，缺乏普适几何-物理判据。

Method: 采用零测地线的Hamiltonian形式，结合时空的Killing对称性构造有效势；在光环处计算横向Hessian，并利用Newman–Penrose形式与Sachs光学方程将其表为NP标量Φ₀₀和Ψ₀；通过Einstein方程将Φ₀₀关联至物质能动张量Tₐb，导出基于零能量条件的稳定性判据；对各类物质场（真空、电磁场、实/复标量场）进行对称性分析与约束推导。

Result: 得到普适稳定性判据：光环比稳定当且仅当Φ₀₀ > |Ψ₀|；等价于4πTₐb pᵃpᵇ > |Ψ₀|，其中pᵃ为其切向量；特别地，若SNEC在光环处被违反，则必不稳定；实标量场因必须同时保持两个Killing等距而无法支持稳定光环比；复标量场若具有e^{i(mϕ + ωt)}型Killing谐波依赖，则可规避该 obstruction。

Conclusion: 光环比稳定性本质上由局域曲率的聚焦（Ricci）与剪切（Weyl）竞争决定；该判据统一涵盖多种物质源，揭示了能量条件与强场光子结构之间的深层联系；为未来黑洞替代模型与奇异星的可观测特征提供了关键理论约束。

Abstract: We consider the stability of light rings in four-dimensional circular stationary axisymmetric spacetimes. Using a Hamiltonian formalism for null geodesics and the Killing symmetries of the spacetime, we show that the transverse Hessian of the geodesic potential, which controls the light ring's stability, reduces there to a pure curvature expression. Its eigenvalues are $μ_{\pm} = Φ_{0 0} \pm \lvert Ψ_{0} \rvert$, where $Φ_{0 0}$ is a Ricci scalar and $Ψ_{0}$ a Weyl scalar of a complex null tetrad adapted to the light ring. Through the Sachs optical equations, these quantities are responsible for focussing and shear of a null geodesic congruence. Stability requires both eigenvalues to be positive, so a light ring is stable if and only if $Φ_{0 0} > \lvert Ψ_{0} \rvert$ there. Assuming the Einstein field equations with generic stress--energy tensor $T_{a b}$, we recast this as $4 πT_{a b} p^{a} p^{b} > \lvert Ψ_{0} \rvert$, where $p^{a}$ is the light ring's tangent vector: for stability, the focussing by matter must dominate the local tidal shear. The trace of the Hessian alone provides an inexpensive test of stability, requiring no knowledge of the Weyl curvature, since a light ring at which the strict null energy condition is violated cannot be stable. Applying the test to specific matter models, we recover known results for vacuum and electrovacuum, with and without a cosmological constant. We also demonstrate that stable light rings are forbidden for a minimally coupled real scalar field with any self-interaction potential, since such a field must be invariant under both isometries. We show that a complex scalar field escapes this obstruction whenever it carries harmonic dependence on the Killing coordinates.

</details>


### [101] [Analytic Electromagnetic Effective Source for Geodesic Orbit in Schwarzschild Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14909)
*Xuchen Lu,Yungui Gong,Bokai Zhang,Chao Zhang,Wenting Zhou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了Schwarzschild时空中测地线运动的解析电磁有效源，通过角向完备化与标架分解将矢量球谐穿刺模表示为有限个标量模之和，导出两宇称扇区的耦合系数，并从Maxwell模方程获得有效源；点粒子δ贡献被抵消，偏心轨道数值结果表明模式源在粒子附近有限，为未来1+1维剩余场计算提供源项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度建模小质量天体在弯曲时空中的运动需计入自作用效应，而有效源方法是实现高阶自作用计算的重要途径。

Method: 采用角向完备化（angular completion）与正交标架分解（tetrad decomposition），将矢量球谐 puncture 模展开为有限个标量球谐模之和；推导偶/奇宇称扇区的耦合系数；由分离变量后的Maxwell模方程构造有效源；验证δ函数型点粒子贡献的精确抵消。

Result: 得到了Schwarzschild时空中任意（含偏心）测地轨道的解析电磁有效源表达式；各l模有效源在粒子位置处有限；数值验证显示残差场模源在粒子邻域无发散。

Conclusion: 该解析有效源形式克服了传统δ分布源带来的数值奇异性，为1+1维时域剩余场计算提供了严格、稳定且可直接离散化的源项，是迈向二阶自作用与波形建模的关键一步。

Abstract: Self-force effects are essential for accurately describing the motion of small bodies in curved spacetime. The effective-source approach privides a promising route to higher-order self-force calculations. In this paper, we construct analytic electromagnetic effective source for geodesic motion in Schwarzschild spacetime. An angular completion and a tetrad decomposition express the vector-harmonic puncture modes as finite sums of scalar modes. We derive the coupling coefficients for both parity sectors and obtain the effective sources from the Maxwell mode equations. The point-particle delta contribution cancels, and representative eccentric-orbit results show finite mode sources near the particle. These expressions provide the source terms for future $1+1$-dimensional residual-field calculations.

</details>


### [102] [Quantum Energy Inequalities with a twist: rotating frames along stationary worldlines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14927)
*Christopher J. Fewster,Daniel Lomax Ford*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了闵氏时空中静止类时世界线上的无质量标量场与电磁场的量子能量不等式（QEI）闭式界，涵盖惯性、匀加速及匀速旋转运动；特别分析了不同标架（尤其是Fermi-Walker与Frenet-Serret标架）对界紧致性的影响，发现电磁场最优界由Fermi-Walker标架实现，而标量场则不然。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子能动张量的能量密度可为负，但其沿类时世界线的时间平均受量子能量不等式（QEIs）约束；然而一般情形下QEI界难以获得闭式表达，亟需针对典型时空与运动学构型发展可计算、紧致的界。

Method: 利用四维Frenet-Serret形式论刻画具有常曲率不变量的静止类时世界线；在Minkowski时空中，对无质量标量场与电磁场，显式计算任意恒定角速度相对Frenet-Serret标架的正交标架下的QEI下界；通过变分优化角速度参数，确定最优标架；结合解析推导与数值搜索确定标量场的全局最优界。

Result: 获得了标量场与电磁场沿任意常曲率静止世界线的QEI闭式下界；证明电磁场的最紧QEI界在Fermi-Walker平移标架下实现；发现标量场的最紧界不在Frenet-Serret或Fermi-Walker标架中，而需非零恒定相对角速度，并给出其解析结构与数值极小值；显著改进了Fewster & Thompson (2023) 的结果。

Conclusion: QEI界的紧致性强烈依赖于观测者标架的选择，且标量场与电磁场存在本质差异：前者最优标架非Fermi-Walker型，揭示了量子场论中局部能量测量对参考系转动自由度的敏感性；该工作为弯曲时空QEI的构造与应用（如奇点定理、虫洞物理）提供了关键平坦时空基准。

Abstract: The energy densities of the quantised scalar and electromagnetic fields need not be positive, but their averages along timelike worldlines are constrained by Quantum Energy Inequalities (QEIs) that hold in flat and curved spacetimes for arbitrary Hadamard quantum field states. However the general QEI bound is not easy to compute in closed form. Here, we compute closed form expressions for the massless scalar and electromagnetic fields along stationary worldlines in Minkowski spacetime. These worldlines are solutions to the four-dimensional Frenet-Serret equations with constant curvature invariants and include inertial, uniformly linearly accelerated, and uniformly rotating worldlines as special cases. The QEI bound depends on a choice of tetrad along the worldline, and we compute it for any tetrad with constant angular velocity relative to the Frenet-Serret tetrad. Optimising over the angular velocity, we show that the tightest electromagnetic QEI bound occurs when the tetrad is invariant under Fermi-Walker tetrads. Surprisingly, the Fermi-Walker tetrad does not produce the tightest QEI bound for massless scalar fields, and we investigate the optimal bound both analytically and numerically. Our results and methodology significantly improve on those in [CJ Fewster & J Thompson, Class. Quantum Grav. 40 (2023) 175008], in which only Frenet-Serret tetrads were considered for massless scalar fields.

</details>


### [103] [Scalar wave dark matter imprints on binary black hole mergers: dependence on field mass and density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15053)
*Yu-Peng Zhang,Shao-Wen Wei,Yu-Xiao Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过数值相对论模拟，研究了复杂标量场暗物质环境对非自旋双黑洞并合的影响，发现标量场质量与密度共同调控并合动力学、引力波辐射特性及反冲速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索暗物质环境（特别是标量场）如何修改双黑洞并合过程及其引力波信号，以期为LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA观测提供新物理指纹。

Method: 采用数值相对论模拟非自旋双黑洞在复标量场云中的并合，覆盖质量比q=1–4，系统扫描无量纲标量场质量m̂_Φ = m_Φ M₀与初始振幅Π₀（控制场密度），并保持初始标量能密剖面与二体几何不变以分离场质量的动力学效应。

Result: 标量环境普遍加速并合；不等质量系统中差异性标量吸积改变视界质量比，增强不对称性；高密度抑制引力波能量与角动量辐射；轻质量标量场辐射更远，重质量场局域化强、向内能量输运更强；标量角动量输运呈非单调甚至方向反转；引力波与标量波线动量相对取向决定反冲增强或抑制，最大反冲达2063.6 km/s（q=2, m̂_Φ=0, Π₀=0.0100），远高于真空值137.8 km/s。

Conclusion: 标量场质量m̂_Φ与密度Π₀协同主导双黑洞系统的不对称演化、动力学时标、多信使辐射通道（引力波/标量波）及动量平衡，为暗物质-致密天体相互作用提供了可检验的理论预言。

Abstract: Dark matter environments can modify binary black hole mergers and imprint their gravitational-wave signals. We perform numerical-relativity simulations of nonspinning binary black holes immersed in complex scalar-field clouds, considering mass ratios $q=1,2,3,$ and $4$ and varying both the dimensionless scalar-field mass $\hat m_Φ=m_ΦM_0$ and initial amplitude $Π_0$, which controls the density. Our initial data keep the scalar energy-density profile and binary geometry unchanged when varying $\hat m_Φ$ at fixed $Π_0$, allowing the dynamical effect of the field mass to be isolated. We find that the scalar environment generally accelerates the merger and significantly modifies the gravitational-wave signal. For unequal-mass binaries, differential scalar-field accretion changes the horizon-mass ratio during inspiral, weakly at low density but substantially at high density, thereby increasing the binary asymmetry before merger. Dense scalar environments suppress the energy and angular momentum carried by gravitational waves. Lighter scalar fields propagate more efficiently to large radii, whereas heavier fields remain more localized and show stronger inward energy transport. Scalar angular-momentum transport is more complex, showing nonmonotonic behavior and even direction reversal near the binary. For unequal-mass systems, gravitational and scalar radiation both carry net linear momentum, and their relative orientation determines whether the recoil is enhanced or suppressed. The largest kick is $2063.6~{\rm km,s^{-1}}$ for $q=2$, $\hat m_Φ=0$, and $Π_0=0.0100$, compared with $137.8~{\rm km,s^{-1}}$ in vacuum. These results show that the scalar-field mass and density jointly control the binary asymmetry, dynamics, radiative channels, and momentum balance.

</details>


### [104] [Non-Commutativity in Geodesic Congruences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15105)

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Frobenius定理的时空非对易几何实现，保持流形光滑性，修改Raychaudhuri方程并引入非对易能量条件（NCEC），从而推广聚焦引理与奇点定理；应用于FLRW宇宙学，导出非对易修正项θ^NC，该修正分离为标准流体稀释的普适修正和暗能量密度非线性项，为暗能量演化提供纯几何起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在不破坏时空光滑性、不修改爱因斯坦场方程、不引入新标量场的前提下，为暗能量演化提供几何起源，并拓展广义相对论中的奇点定理至非对易动力学框架。

Method: 基于Frobenius定理推广切向量与测地线分离矢量间的对易关系，构造非对易运动学变形；推导含修正膨胀标量\tilde θ = θ + θ^{NC} 的Raychaudhuri方程；定义类时/类光非对易能量条件（NCEC）；据此重证聚焦引理、共轭点存在性及Penrose-Hawking型奇点定理；最后将该结构应用于FLRW度规，分析其对Friedmann加速度方程与连续性方程的影响。

Result: 获得修正的Raychaudhuri方程与NCEC；证明在NCEC下，即使强能量条件（SEC）被违反，测地线仍可聚焦并导致不完备性；成功推广了正交于Cauchy面的类时测地线与正交于陷获面的类光测地线的奇点定理；在FLRW背景下，θ^{NC} 导致宇宙学方程出现两项非对易修正：一项普适地改变所有流体的能量密度稀释律（如ρ ∝ a^{-3+ε}），另一项表现为暗能量密度ρ_Λ的非线性函数，即ρ_Λ^{eff} = ρ_Λ + f(ρ_Λ)，且f ≠ 0。

Conclusion: 时空非对易性可通过几何方式嵌入经典广义相对论框架，无需修改引力动力学或引入新自由度；其运动学效应足以重构宇宙加速膨胀的物理解释——暗能量由测地线丛的非对易膨胀修正θ^{NC} 自然涌现，具有纯微分几何本源。

Abstract: We propose a geometric realization of spacetime non-commutativity where the spacetime manifold remains smooth while the commutation relation between tangent and separation vectors of geodesic congruences is generalized via Frobenius' theorem. This non-commutative kinematic deformation leads to a modified Raychaudhuri equation with a shifted expansion scalar $\tilde θ=θ+θ^{\rm NC}$. We introduce a timelike and null-like Non-Commutative Energy Condition (NCEC) under which focusing and geodesic incompleteness can, in some regimes, persist even when the standard strong energy condition is violated. Using this, we generalize the key focusing lemmas, conjugate-point propositions, and singularity theorems for both timelike geodesics orthogonal to a Cauchy surface and null geodesics orthogonal to a trapped surface. Applied to a FLRW spacetime, we argue that $θ^{\rm NC}$ modifies both the Friedmann acceleration equation and the continuity equations. The non-commutative corrections separate into a universal modification of the dilution of the standard cosmological fluids and another contribution to the dark energy sector that is non-linear in energy density. Crucially, this provides a purely geometric origin for the evolution of dark energy, without introducing exotic dark energy fields or modifying Einstein's field equations.

</details>


### [105] [Primordial Graviton Entanglement in Quasicrystalline Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15249)
