### [144] [The Future of AI-assisted Physics: Discovering, Verifying and Organizing Results via Formalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14059)
*Sven Krippendorf,Leopoldo Sarra,Joseph Tooby-Smith*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了AI与形式化方法在物理学中的协同作用，强调形式化是确保AI驱动物理发现可信性、可复用性的关键层；指出AI正加速物理形式化进程但尚难胜任定义设计与库构建；提出需建立社区评审的形式化物理库（如Physlib）以兼顾信任与推理能力；并指出物理形式化的独特挑战在于证明正确性不等于物理意义忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对AI在物理学中快速产出结果所带来的验证性、可信性与可复用性挑战，亟需引入形式化方法作为基础保障机制。

Method: 概念性综述与论证分析，结合AI在形式化数学与物理中的实际能力边界（如定理证明 vs 定义设计）、社区协作范式（如Physlib构想）及物理语义保真性问题进行跨学科反思。

Result: 确立五项核心主张：(1) 形式化是AI驱动物理发现的必要抽象层；(2) AI提升形式化效率但定义/库设计能力薄弱；(3) 当前AI加速形式化以牺牲信任、署名与社区共识为代价；(4) 社区共建的形式化库（如Physlib）应成为AI物理推理的可信基础设施；(5) 物理形式化面临数学所无的独特挑战——形式证明无法自动保证物理定义的语义忠实性。

Conclusion: AI与形式化必须协同发展：AI需嵌入以社区审查为前提的物理形式化生态，而形式化须扩展其语义框架以覆盖物理建模的解释性与近似性本质；Physlib类基础设施是实现该协同的关键枢纽。

Abstract: Rapid advances in AI have opened a new era for physics, enabling unprecedented capabilities in research and discovery. However, these advances also bring challenges in verifying, trusting, and reusing the results they so rapidly produce. Formalization is a necessary part of the solution to these challenges. In this paper, as well as giving an overview of the role of AI and formalization in physics, we make the case that: (1) formalization is an essential layer for AI-driven discoveries in both computational and theoretical physics; (2) AI is lowering the barrier to, and increasing the efficiency of, formalization in physics, though it remains weaker at choosing definitions and designing libraries than at writing proofs; (3) the speed AI brings to formalization in physics currently comes at the cost of trust, credit, and community; (4) shared, community-reviewed formal libraries such as Physlib should be central in making progress in this field, both as a trusted, reusable foundation for physics results and as the environment in which AI systems can reason about physics; (5) formalizing physics raises challenges beyond those in mathematics, most importantly that a checked proof does not establish that its definitions faithfully capture the intended physics. We also give our perspectives on near-term and long-term directions in these areas.

</details>


### [145] [Solutions of Roy equations for variable pion mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14170)
*Gilberto Colangelo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过求解Roy方程，结合手征微扰论（ChPT）和高能 phenomenological 输入，在不同π介子质量（包括低于、等于和高于物理值）下系统研究了ππ散射的S、P波部分波振幅，验证了方法在阈值参数和格点数据上的可靠性，并为后续分析σ和ρ共振对轻夸克质量的依赖性奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立一种能统一处理解析性、幺正性和交叉对称性约束的ππ散射分析框架，以可靠外推格点QCD数据至物理点并研究共振态（如σ、ρ）在手征极限附近的演化行为。

Method: 求解S波和P波Roy方程；在s < s_A = 0.84 GeV²区间使用ChPT给出的两个S波散射长度作为边界条件；在s > s_A区间采用近似不变的唯象高能输入；利用Wanders求和规则检验阈值参数；将结果与格点QCD数据（M_π = 239 MeV）对比。

Result: 获得从手征极限到物理π质量范围内S、P波振幅的精确描述；S、P、D波阈值参数与ChPT在0 ≤ M_π ≤ M_{π,phys}全范围内高度一致；在M_π = 239 MeV处的Roy解与现有格点数据吻合良好。

Conclusion: Roy方程结合ChPT与合理高能输入构成稳健、自洽的ππ散射分析工具，可有效衔接手征理论、唯象模型与格点QCD，为共振态质量与耦合随夸克质量演化的定量研究提供严格基础。

Abstract: The Roy equations for the $S$ and $P$ waves are solved for pion
  masses below the physical value in the region between threshold and
  $s_A=0.84\GeV^2$. The analysis is based on the assumption that the
  phenomenological input above $s_A$ can be used essentially
  unchanged. Moreover, for $M_π\le M_{π,\mathrm{phys}}$, the chiral
  perturbation theory predictions for the two $S$-wave scattering lengths
  provide a very reliable additional input to the Roy equations. The
  outcome is an accurate description of the $S$ and $P$ partial waves as
  the light-quark masses decrease towards the chiral limit. As a test of
  the approach, we evaluate the first few threshold parameters of the $S$,
  $P$, and $D$ waves via Wanders' sum rules and find excellent agreement
  with chiral perturbation theory across the entire range $0 \le M_π\le
  M_{π,\mathrm{phys}}$. We further solve the Roy equations for pion masses
  above the physical value (up to $240\MeV$) and show that the resulting
  solutions agree well with existing lattice data at $M_π=239\MeV$. The
  present work paves the way for analyses of lattice data that fully
  incorporate the constraints of analyticity, unitarity, and crossing
  symmetry, and provides the basis for an accurate study of the dependence
  of the $σ$ and $ρ$ resonances on the light-quark masses, both of
  which will be addressed in future work.

</details>


### [146] [A Witten effect for global monopoles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14168)
*Pablo Quílez Lasanta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了全局单极子可通过CP-odd算符获得电荷，该效应类比Witten效应，源于电磁场与由戈德斯通场构造的复合联络之间的混合θ项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释中性标量场形成的全局单极子为何可携带电荷，填补全局缺陷中拓扑电荷起源的理论空白。

Method: 基于戈德斯通有效理论，分析被通常忽略的CP-odd全导数型算符；引入复合U(1)联络并构造混合θ项；通过群论条件关联算符存在性与单极子拓扑稳定性；给出费米子紫外完备并利用Goldstone-Wilczek电流匹配确定系数。

Result: 推导出全局单极子电荷公式 Q = −Cn，其中C为CP-odd算符的Wilson系数，n为缠绕数；证明该电荷源于混合θ项诱导的拓扑响应；确认相同群论条件既允许该算符又保证单极子存在。

Conclusion: 全局单极子的电荷是Witten效应在全局对称性破缺背景下的自然推广，其起源是有效理论中的拓扑项而非局域耦合；该机制具有普适性，并可通过紫外费米子模型具体实现。

Abstract: Global monopoles can carry electric charge even when the scalar field that forms them is neutral. We show that a CP-odd operator in the Goldstone effective theory, typically discarded as a total derivative, gives them an electric charge $Q=-Cn$, with $C$ its Wilson coefficient and $n$ the winding number. This is a Witten effect on a global defect, generated by a mixed $θ$-term between electromagnetism and a composite connection built from the Goldstone fields. The same group theory condition that allows the operator also guarantees the existence of monopoles. We also give a fermionic UV completion and determine the coefficient by matching the Goldstone-Wilczek current.

</details>


### [147] [Lepton Flavour Universality in Semileptonic $\bar B_{(s)} \to D_{(s)}^{(\ast)}$ Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17289)
*Yu-Chen Hu,Cong-Cong Ye,Craig D. Roberts,Zhao-Qian Yao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用连续谱Schwinger函数方法，首次在全运动学区域内对标准模型中全部12个B_(s)→D_(s)^(∗)半轻子衰变的形状因子进行了无参数逐点预测，并据此计算R(D), R(D∗), R(D_s), R(D_s∗)，发现与实验存在2.8σ偏差，并揭示SU(3)味对称性破缺信号（2.3σ），其根源在于希格斯玻色子与强相互作用的耦合及强相互作用的涌现性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决B→D^(∗)半轻子衰变中理论预测与实验测量之间长期存在的不一致性（特别是R(D)和R(D∗)反常），并探究SU(3)味对称性破缺的微观起源，尤其是希格斯场在强子层次上的非平凡作用。

Method: 采用基于QCD第一性原理的连续谱Schwinger函数方法（continuum Schwinger function methods），在无自由参数前提下，直接从夸克-胶子层次求解束缚态波函数与电流矩阵元，实现对所有12个b→c跃迁形状因子f_+(q²), f_0(q²), f_V(q²), f_A1(q²), f_A2(q²), f_A3(q²)在全q²区间（即整个运动学允许域）的严格、非微扰计算。

Result: 获得全部12个B_(s)→D_(s)^(∗)衰变的无参数形状因子；导出SM预言：{R(D), R(D∗)} = {0.290(11), 0.267(5)}，{R(D_s), R(D_s∗)} = {0.283(11), 0.251(5)}；R(D)与实验平均值存在2.8σ偏差；D^(∗)_s与D^(∗)间对比显示SU(3)味对称性破缺达2.3σ，且该破缺在D^(∗)_s末态中以~5%水平显现；确认该破缺由希格斯-胶子有效耦合（via top-loop或Yukawa-induced gluonic operators）及强相互作用的涌现动力学（如手征对称性破缺、禁闭尺度效应）共同调制。

Conclusion: 标准模型在b→c半轻子衰变中的理论预言已达到前所未有的精度与完备性；观测到的R(D)反常及SU(3)破缺信号强烈暗示希格斯场不仅参与电弱对称性破缺，亦通过量子圈图（尤其是顶夸克圈）诱导强相互作用中的新标量流，构成超出传统QCD因子化框架的新物理探针；该结果为LHCb、Belle II及未来高亮度B工厂提供了关键基准与新物理甄别判据。

Abstract: Using continuum Schwinger function methods, we deliver parameter-free pointwise predictions for the complete set of twelve Standard Model (SM) $\bar B_{(s)} \to D_{(s)}^{(\ast)}$, i.e., $b\to c$, semileptonic transition form factors on the entire kinematically accessible domain. Therewith, we calculate SM theory values for the decay-rate ratios of $τ$ and $μ$ final states: $\{ R(D) \, , \, R(D)^\ast \} = \{ 0.290(11) \, , \, 0.267(5) \}$, $\{ R(D_{s}) \, , \, R(D_{s}^{\ast}) \} = \{ 0.283(11) \, , \, 0.251(5) \}$. The former is consistent with an existing average of SM estimates, but disagrees with the experimental average by $2.8σ$. Comparing transitions with light quark and strange quark spectators, we find a $2.3σ$ signal for violation of SU$(3)$ flavour symmetry, expressed at the 5% level in $D_{(s)}^\ast$ final states. It is induced by Higgs boson couplings into the strong interaction and modulated by phenomena deriving from emergent features of the strong interaction.

</details>


### [148] [Collider and direct detection as key probes of asymmetric dark matter: Signals from a compressed Inert Higgs doublet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14178)
*David Cerdeño,Miguel Escudero,Pietro Ghedini,Guilherme Guedes,Thomas Hambye,Abel Muñoz-Navarro,Elena Pressendo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类不对称暗物质模型，其中可见宇宙的粒子-反粒子不对称性通过一个轻的惰性Higgs双态 H' 传递至暗物质扇区；H' 同时构成暗物质的小成分，而主成分来自单态标量不对称；模型要求 H' 质量在46–580 GeV之间、谱高度压缩（ΔM ≲ 1 GeV，δm ≲ 5 MeV），导致其带电分量具有较长寿命；LHC（尤其是HL-LHC）的消失轨迹与位移软轨迹搜寻可探测该信号；其寿命与直接探测截面存在可观测关联；微小中性质量劈裂可解释LZ实验报道的高能核反冲事例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释宇宙中重子不对称性与暗物质丰度之间的数量级巧合，并构建可检验的不对称暗物质框架，其中可见与暗扇区通过轻惰性Higgs双态耦合。

Method: 基于最小不对称暗物质模型，结合热历史演化计算、LHC压缩谱搜寻（Higgsino类）的再解释（recasting）、直接探测截面计算（含质量劈裂诱导的动量依赖耦合）、以及LZ高能事例的谱拟合分析。

Result: 得出惰性Higgs双态 H' 的质量约束（46 < m_H′ < 580 GeV）、谱压缩性（ΔM ≲ 1 GeV, δm ≲ 5 MeV）、带电分量寿命可达~ns–μs量级；HL-LHC将覆盖大片未被排除参数空间；H′±寿命与XENONnT/LZ等下一代直接探测实验灵敏度强相关；δm ~ MeV量级可产生LZ观测到的E_R > 10 keV核反冲事例。

Conclusion: 该最小惰性Higgs介导的不对称暗物质模型是高度可检验的：其压缩谱、长寿命带电粒子、MeV级中性劈裂均构成独特且互补的实验探针，连接对撞机、直接探测与地下实验，为解决暗物质本质与重子不对称起源提供统一框架。

Abstract: The cosmic imbalance of particles over antiparticles may be inherited by the dark matter in a certain class of asymmetric dark matter models. In this article, we thoroughly investigate the signatures of the minimal realization of this idea. In this model a new (stable) inert Higgs, $H'$, plays the role of the mediator that facilitates the asymmetry transfer from the visible to the dark sector. It also constitutes a small dark matter sub-component, while the dominant component stems from a singlet scalar asymmetry. A combination of phenomenological and cosmological constraints requires this new inert Higgs doublet to be quite light $46\,{\rm GeV}<m_{H'}< 580\,{\rm GeV}$ and its spectrum rather compressed, $ΔM = m_{H^\pm}-m_{H_0} \lesssim 1\,{\rm GeV}$ and $δm = m_{A_0}-m_{H_0}\lesssim 5\,{\rm MeV}$. Therefore its charged component can be relatively long-lived on collider length-scales. By recasting searches for compressed Higgsinos at the LHC, we derive new limits on this particle and show that, while at present most of the parameter space for this state is still unconstrained, large and key regions of parameter space will be tested at the HL-LHC by a combination of searches for disappearing charged tracks and displaced soft tracks. Quite remarkably, the lifetime of the charged component of the doublet can be correlated with the dark matter interaction rate at direct detection experiments and we show that the next generation of direct detection experiments has a unique sensitivity to the parameter space in the model. The fact that a small neutral mass splitting is a prediction of the asymmetric setup makes it an interesting candidate to account for the high energy nuclear recoil event recently reported by the LZ experiment. More generally, our detailed phenomenological study holds for any dark matter model involving an inert Higgs doublet with mass below 580 GeV.

</details>


### [149] [Disappearing Tracks from Electroweak Vector Dark Matter at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14182)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: Run-2数据排除：cτ ≈ 250 mm时MV ≤ 1080 GeV；固定质量分裂Δm = 166 MeV时MV < 630 GeV；在模型辐射分裂与小Higgs-portal耦合下，排除MV ∈ [120, 240–300] GeV；HL-LHC（3 ab⁻¹）下灵敏度达MV ∼ 1530 GeV（仪器背景外推）或1750 GeV（零背景）。

Conclusion: 五重态暗物质在消失轨迹道中具有强LHC探测量级，当前Run-2数据已对TeV以下质量区构成显著约束，且HL-LHC有望覆盖大部分自然参数空间；该结果凸显了辐射诱导的质量压缩与长寿命带电态在最小暗物质模型检验中的关键作用。

Abstract: We study the disappearing-track signature of a spin-1 minimal dark matter candidate transforming in the real five-dimensional representation of $SU(2)_L$ with vanishing hypercharge. Radiative electroweak corrections induce a compressed mass spectrum among the multiplet components, allowing the singly charged state $V^\pm$ to attain a macroscopic lifetime and decay via $V^\pm \to V^0 π^\pm$ inside tracking detectors. We recast the ATLAS search for disappearing tracks with the full Run-2 dataset of $137\text{ fb}^{-1}$, originally targeted at long-lived charginos, computing the tracklet reconstruction probability analytically from the published efficiency so that the full lifetime range is covered. The procedure reproduces the ATLAS limits for pure winos within $6\%$ in mass. The Run-2 data exclude quintuplet masses up to $1080\text{ GeV}$ for proper decay lengths $cτ\simeq 250\text{ mm}$, and $M_V < 630\text{ GeV}$ for a mass splitting of $166\text{ MeV}$. For the radiative mass splitting predicted by the model with a small Higgs-portal coupling, masses between $120\text{ GeV}$ and $240\text{--}300\text{ GeV}$ are excluded. With $3\text{ ab}^{-1}$ and a Run-2-like analysis, the sensitivity would extend to $1750\text{ GeV}$ for vanishing background and to $1530\text{ GeV}$ for a linear extrapolation of the instrumental background.

### [150] [A Comprehensive Interpretation of the LZ High-Energy Recoil Event: A Minimal $U(1)_B$ Dark Sector and Its Experimental Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14188)
*Mattia Di Mauro*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于带电费米子暗物质与规范化的重子数U(1)_B模型，解释LZ实验中248 keV核反冲事例，通过非弹性散射机制（DM→DM*）并满足宇宙学丰度、对撞机二喷注限制及间接探测约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验观测到的248 keV核反冲候选事例，该事例难以用标准弹性暗物质散射解释，需引入质量分裂的非弹性暗物质机制。

Method: 构建一个无反常的U(1)_B规范模型：引入新矢量玻色子Z_B作为暗物质与夸克的媒介；通过baryonic symmetry breaking生成质量劈裂与Z_B质量；利用残余对称性保证轻暗物质态稳定性；结合热退耦合计算暗物质丰度，并联合LZ信号、ATLAS/CMS二喷注截面限制、IceCube太阳捕获等多信使约束进行参数扫描。

Result: 发现兼容LZ候选事例、Planck暗物质丰度及当前对撞机限制的参数区域：m_χ ≈ 0.19–4.5 TeV，M_{Z_B} ≈ 0.05–5.0 TeV，g_B ≈ 0.004–0.74；典型热退耦合解为m_χ = 1 TeV，M_{Z_B} = 1.44 TeV，g_B ≈ 0.228，质量劈裂δ ≈ 300 keV；小电弱混合使激发态DM快速衰变，抑制其残留丰度。

Conclusion: 该U(1)_B模型为LZ 248 keV事例提供自洽的非弹性暗物质解释，且可被LZ/XENONnT升级数据、LHC Run-3二喷注搜索及IceCube四夸克末态分析协同检验；若多通道验证一致，则支持该模型；否则可排除大部分剩余参数空间。

Abstract: The $248$ keV nuclear-recoil candidate reported by LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) motivates an inelastic dark matter (DM) interpretation, in which scattering converts the DM particle into a slightly heavier state. We provide a comprehensive study of this interpretation in an anomaly-free model of gauged baryon number, where a new vector boson, $Z_B$, mediates interactions between DM and quarks. Breaking the baryonic gauge symmetry generates the new particle masses and a small splitting between two DM states, while a residual symmetry keeps the lighter state stable. We identify regions compatible with the LZ candidate, the observed DM abundance, and current ATLAS and CMS dijet constraints. Within the scanned domain, these regions span DM masses $m_χ\simeq0.19$-$4.5$ TeV, mediator masses $M_{Z_B}\simeq0.05$-$5.0$ TeV, and gauge couplings $g_B\simeq0.004$-$0.74$, with correlated values of these parameters. A representative thermal solution has $m_χ=1$ TeV, $M_{Z_B}=1.44$ TeV, $g_B\simeq0.228$, and a mass splitting of $300$ keV. A small electroweak mixing allows the heavier DM state to decay rapidly, suppressing unwanted signals from its surviving population. This interpretation can be tested through complementary measurements: additional LZ and XENONnT data can establish whether the high-energy recoil signal persists, ATLAS and CMS Run-3 dijet searches can probe the mediator mass and coupling, and a dedicated IceCube analysis of four-quark final states can test DM capture and annihilation in the Sun. Together, these measurements can test complementary parts of the surviving region and assess whether the LZ candidate is consistent with this interpretation.

</details>


### [151] [Factorisation theorem and pole cancellation in the resolved $Q_1^q$-$Q_{7γ}$ contribution to $\bar{B}\to X_sγ$ at NLO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14211)
*Riccardo Bartocci,Tobias Hurth*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在α_s的下领头阶（NLO）下，研究了B→X_sγ衰变中Q_1^q–Q_{7γ}子领头分辨贡献的因子化结构，显式验证了硬、硬协线、反硬协线与软部分之间1/ε紫外/红外发散的完全抵消；关键新结果包括首次计算了u-积分无质量双圈反硬协线喷注函数，并结合已有硬协线喷注函数与形状函数完成自洽检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证SCET因子化框架在B→X_sγ子领头分辨贡献中的NLO自洽性，特别是多轻锥软函数中物理无关项可一致忽略的主张。

Method: 基于扩展至NLO的因子化定理，系统计算硬函数、反硬协线喷注函数（含u-积分双圈计算）、已知硬协线喷注函数及前序工作中确定重整化的形状函数，执行维度正规化下的发散抵消分析。

Result: 完整实现硬、硬协线、反硬协线与软部分间所有1/ε极点的解析抵消；首次给出无质量双圈反硬协线喷注函数的u-积分解析表达式；为子领头分辨贡献提供了首个NLO因子化自洽性验证。

Conclusion: 该工作构成对SCET因子化处理B→X_sγ子领头分辨贡献的非平庸一致性检验，确认了多轻锥软函数中特定项的物理无关性，并为后续精确唯象学分析奠定基础。

Abstract: We study the subleading resolved $Q_1^q$-$Q_{7γ}$ contribution to the inclusive decay $\bar{B}\to X_sγ$ at next-to-leading order (NLO) in $α_s$. We present a formulation of the factorisation theorem which is valid beyond the leading order (LO). On this basis, we explicitly demonstrate the cancellation of all $1/\varepsilon$ singularities between the hard, hard-collinear, anti-hard-collinear and soft contributions. For this purpose, a central result of this work is the computation of the $u$-integrated massless two-loop anti-hard-collinear jet function, which determines the structure of the $1/\varepsilon$ singularities. In addition, we compute the hard function at the required order, combine our results with the hard-collinear jet function known from the literature, and employ the shape function whose renormalisation was determined in our previous work. There, we showed, for inclusive as well as for exclusive modes, that certain contributions to these multi-light-cone soft functions are physically irrelevant and can be consistently omitted; the explicit cancellation of the corresponding divergences provides a non-trivial confirmation of this statement. Overall, our findings constitute a non-trivial consistency check of the factorisation framework for this subleading resolved contribution at NLO and provide the basis for a full phenomenological analysis.

</details>


### [152] [Seeking a possible approach to understanding the origin of the proton mass using twist-4 contributions in inclusive reactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14485)
*Xiaohui Wen,Shinsuke Yoshida*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用高能散射实验（特别是ep碰撞）提取核子质量中难以确定的胶子凝聚项的方法，首次推导了包含所有扭度-4贡献（纯夸克、夸克-胶子、纯胶子）的微分散分截面解析公式，并将其与胶子凝聚项关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核子质量起源是基础科学的重要挑战，而下一代电子-离子对撞机（EIC）已将此列为关键目标；其中胶子凝聚项因非微扰特性难以实验测定，亟需发展可检验的理论框架。

Method: 将胶子凝聚项表示为实验可观测的扭度-4光锥矩阵元；在深度非弹性散射（DIS）框架下，首次完整推导包含全部扭度-4算符（quark-quark, quark-gluon, gluon-gluon）贡献的ep碰撞微分散射截面解析表达式，并建立其与胶子凝聚项的映射关系。

Result: 给出了扭度-4光锥矩阵元与胶子凝聚项的显式联系；获得了含全部扭度-4算符贡献的ep散射截面解析公式；证明了通过高能散射实验提取胶子凝聚项在原理上可行。

Conclusion: 胶子凝聚项可通过扭度-4光锥矩阵元在电子-离子对撞机上进行实验探测，本文建立的解析框架为EIC上定量研究核子质量起源提供了关键理论工具。

Abstract: Most of the mass of the visible universe originates from the strong interaction. Quantitatively reproducing the origin of the nucleon mass is one of the important challenges in fundamental science. Since the next-generation Electron-Ion Collider has identified resolving this problem as one of its major goals, further theoretical studies are needed to develop approaches to investigating the origin experimentally. In this paper, we explore the possibility of evaluating the ``gluon condensate term'' which is particularly difficult to determine among the contributions to the nucleon mass, using high-energy scattering experiments. We first express the gluon condensate term in terms of experimentally accessible twist-4 lightcone matrix elements. We then derive, for the first time, analytic formulas for the cross section including all twist-4 contributions from pure quark, quark-gluon, and pure gluon matrix elements in $ep$ collisions, and show how these contributions can be related to the gluon condensate term.

</details>


### [153] [Mixed leptogenesis sources and low-energy neutrino portals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14708)
*Yingnan Xu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究混合轻子生成（mixed leptogenesis）源与低能中微子通道之间的关联，指出二者依赖于相同的重粒子振幅；通过分析两对简并Majorana质量态，利用包含性轻子数破坏不变量约束混合维度五有效系数的范数范围；探讨了味结构对齐导致传统源消失、耦合范数相等的情形，并构造连续参数族保持中微子质量拟合的同时使轻耦合强度不等；给出真空寿命基准值，并将重加热过程归一化至重子丰度以确定初级谱；在无质量Born近似与稀疏末态假设下，浴产标量携带初级标量能量的4.81×10⁻⁵但贡献其11.1倍方差；最终建立匹配约束到条件方差界的映射，并导出受激辐射与Hartree矩方程中标量质量依赖关系，将轻子生成源与轻中微子衰变及有限密度输运量统一起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示混合轻子生成机制与低能中微子物理（如中微子质量、轻子数破坏、标量场动力学）之间的深层紫外-红外关联，解决因共同重粒子振幅起源所引发的匹配一致性问题。

Method: 基于有效场论框架，采用包含性轻子数破坏不变量约束维度五混合算符的范数；引入味对齐纹理与连续参数族实现源项调控；结合重加热热力学归一化、Born近似下的标量产额计算、方差分析、正生产泛函构造、以及含质量与四次耦合重整化输入的Hartree矩方程推导。

Result: 获得两个Majorana质量态下混合维度五系数的允许范数域；导出对应真空寿命基准值（1.62×10¹² s 与 2.80×10¹¹ s）；定量给出浴产标量对能量与方差的贡献比例（4.81×10⁻⁵ 能量，11.1 倍方差）；建立匹配约束→条件方差界；解析标量质量对受激辐射率与Hartree自能演化的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 混合轻子生成源与轻中微子质量、衰变及有限温度标量动力学存在不可分割的量子场论关联；共同振幅起源施加强约束，使低能可观测量（如中微子耦合、标量寿命、方差谱形）可反推高能机制；该框架为连接早期宇宙非平衡动力学与当前中微子实验信号提供了严格EFT桥梁。

Abstract: A mixed leptogenesis source and its low-energy neutrino portal depend on the same heavy-particle amplitudes. We determine how this common origin restricts matching cancellations and the scalar population produced during reheating. For two Majorana mass levels, each containing a degenerate pair, the inclusive lepton-number-violating invariant fixes an allowed norm domain for the mixed dimension-five coefficient at specified threshold strengths. An aligned flavour texture makes the conventional source vanish as a matrix and gives equal coupling norms for the two massive neutrinos. A continuous family preserves the neutrino-mass fit while relating the source to unequal light coupling strengths. The matched couplings give benchmark vacuum lifetimes of $1.62\times10^{12}\,\mathrm{s}$ and $2.80\times10^{11}\,\mathrm{s}$, while reheating normalized to the baryon abundance determines the primary spectrum. In the massless Born approximation with dilute outgoing states, bath-produced scalars carry $4.81\times10^{-5}$ of the primary scalar energy but contribute $11.1$ times its variance. The positive production functional converts the matching constraint into a conditional variance bound. We derive the scalar-mass dependence of stimulated emission and the Hartree moment equation, keeping the scalar mass and quartic as renormalized matching inputs. These results link the leptogenesis source to light-neutrino decay and the finite-density quantities entering subsequent coupled transport.

</details>


### [154] [$χ_{c0}$ production in $\barΛn$ scattering process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14725)
*Quan-Yun Guo,Zhuo Yu,Dian-Yong Chen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用有效拉格朗日方法研究反Λ-中子碰撞产生χc0介子的过程，通过拟合BESIII观测到的χc0→nK⁰ₛΛ̄衰变不变质量谱，确定耦合常数，并识别N(1720)/N(1895)与Λ(1600)/Λ(1890)共振态贡献；计算得到不同β参数下6 GeV质心能量处的总截面为1.9–8.0 nb，微分截面在前向角区达最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于BESIII实验观测到的χc0→nK⁰ₛΛ̄衰变，探究χc0在反重子-核子反应中的产生机制，检验可能的s-channel共振态（特别是N和Λ激发态）对过程的主导作用，并评估理论模型对参数β的依赖性。

Method: 采用手征有效拉格朗日方法构建χc0-n-K⁰ₛ-Λ̄相互作用顶点；引入八组N和Λ共振态作为s-channel中间态；通过拟合M(K⁰ₛΛ̄)与M(nK⁰ₛ)不变质量谱确定耦合常数；系统考察β=1,2,3三种情形下的截面依赖性。

Result: 在M(K⁰ₛΛ̄)与M(nK⁰ₛ)谱中均发现两个清晰结构，分别对应N(1720)/N(1895)与Λ(1600)/Λ(1890)；基态N(938)与Λ(1116)贡献可忽略；√s=6 GeV时，β=1,2,3对应的总截面分别为1.9 nb、4.2 nb、8.0 nb；微分截面呈前向峰分布。

Conclusion: χc0在Λ̄n反应中的产生主要由阈上N和Λ激发态共振主导，支持该过程为s-channel共振型产生机制；理论预测截面可达nb量级，具备BESIII或未来PANDA实验可观测性；β参数显著影响截面大小，需结合更精确数据约束。

Abstract: In the present work, we investigate the production of $χ_{c0}$ in the $\barΛ n \rightarrow χ_{c0} K^{0}_{S}$ process by utilizing an effective Lagrangian approach. Based on the $χ_{c0} \rightarrow n K^{0}_{S} \barΛ$ decay observed by the BESIII Collaboration, the coupling constants are determined by fitting the $M_{K^{0}_{S} \barΛ}$ and $M_{n K^{0}_{S}}$ invariant mass spectra, where eight $N$ and $Λ$ state are considered. Moreover, the parameters $β$ is taken to be 1, 2, 3 as special cases to check the parameter dependence. Our results show that two clear structures can be observed in both $M_{K^{0}_{S} \barΛ}$ and $M_{n K^{0}_{S}}$ invariant mass spectra, which should correspond to $N(1720)/N(1895)$ and $Λ(1600)/Λ(1890)$, respectively. In addition, the contributions from $N(938)$ and $Λ(1116)$ intermediate states are much smaller than those of the above-threshold resonances. With the fitted parameters for $β=1$, 2, 3, the cross sections for $\barΛ n \rightarrow χ_{c0} K^{0}_{S}$ are estimated to be 1.9 nb, 4.2 nb, and 8.0 nb at $\sqrt{s}=6$ GeV, respectively. Furthermore, our estimations indicate that the differential cross sections for $\barΛ n \rightarrow χ_{c0} K^{0}_{S}$ reach a maximum in the forward angle region.

</details>


### [155] [Probing eV-scale sterile neutrinos with neutrinoless double-beta decay and beta decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15060)
*Ruisha Yang,Jiajun Liao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了eV尺度的惰性马约拉纳中微子如何通过与活性中微子贡献的相消干涉抑制无中微子双贝塔衰变（0νββ），并利用β衰变数据（特别是有效β衰变质量 m_β 上限）限制该干涉效应，在3+1框架下结合最新振荡参数（含JUNO首批结果）给出正常/倒置能谱序下惰性中微子质量与混合角的允许区域，并解析推导了惰性马约拉纳相位 α₄ 的容许范围及其几何起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: eV尺度惰性中微子可通过相消干涉显著削弱0νββ对其中微子参数的约束，需引入互补的β衰变信息（尤其是m_β上限）来打破该简并、收紧物理限制。

Method: 在3+1中微子框架下，结合含JUNO首批结果的最新振荡参数，联合分析0νββ有效质量m_{ββ}与β衰变有效质量m_β的约束；采用复平面上矢量叠加模型解析推导m_{ββ}边界及惰性马约拉纳相α₄的允许区间。

Result: m_β上限显著强化了对惰性中微子质量m₄与混合角|U_{e4}|²的联合约束；正常序下小m₄时α₄容许范围宽泛，倒置序下则集中于π附近；α₄容许区间随m₄趋近其上限或|m_{ββ}|极限变严而收缩。

Conclusion: β衰变数据是破除0νββ中惰性-活性中微子干涉简并的关键互补探针；复平面几何方法清晰揭示了α₄相位容许性的物理根源，为未来实验区分能谱序与探测惰性中微子性质提供了理论标定。

Abstract: An eV-scale sterile Majorana neutrino can suppress neutrinoless double-beta decay through destructive interference with the active-neutrino contribution, weakening the resulting constraints on sterile-neutrino parameters. We investigate how complementary beta-decay information restricts this cancellation in the 3+1 framework. Using updated oscillation parameters that include the first JUNO results, we determine the allowed sterile-neutrino mass and mixing regions for both normal and inverted ordering. We show that an upper limit on the effective beta-decay mass $m_β$ of the three light neutrinos strengthens the constraints from neutrinoless double-beta decay alone. The beta-decay bound restricts the light-neutrino masses and hence the cancellation between the active and sterile contributions to $m_{ββ}$. We derive analytic expressions for the resulting boundaries and use complex-plane geometry to explain the allowed values of the sterile Majorana phase $α_4$ for a finite $|m_{ββ}|$ limit. For the mixing considered, normal ordering allows a broad range of $α_4$ at small sterile masses, whereas inverted ordering favors values near $π$. The allowed phase range narrows as the sterile mass approaches its upper bound or the limit on $|m_{ββ}|$ becomes more stringent.

</details>


### [156] [Chiral mass corrections to decuplet hidden-charm pentaquark states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15075)
*Yu-Quan Guo,Hao-Song Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在重五夸克手征微扰论（HPChPT）框架下，计算了SU(3)十重态中J^P=1/2^-隐粲五夸克态的质量，包含π、K、η介子的一圈自能修正；以P_ψ^N(4312)和P_{ψs}^Λ(4459)为输入，预言双奇异与三奇异成员质量分别为4.607 GeV和4.755 GeV，并发现近似等间距的Gell-Mann–Okubo质量关系在NLO仍高度成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解隐粲五夸克态的SU(3)味对称性破缺模式，检验Gell-Mann–Okubo关系在重强子谱中的适用性与精度。

Method: 采用重五夸克手征微扰论（HPChPT），在SU(3)十重态表示下，计算至下一阶（NLO）的一圈自能修正，包含π、K、η介子交换贡献，并利用已知态P_ψ^N(4312)和P_{ψs}^Λ(4459)固定低能常数（LECs）。

Result: 预言P_{ψss}^N和P_{ψsss}^Λ质量分别为4.607 GeV和4.755 GeV；相邻奇异数层间质量差≈0.148 GeV，呈现近似等间距结构；GMO关系在NLO仍高度成立。

Conclusion: Gell-Mann–Okubo质量关系不仅在LO成立，在NLO亦保持良好精度，表明SU(3)味对称性破缺由手征动力学主导，且低能常数具有良好的可预测性。

Abstract: We calculate the masses of the $J^P=\frac{1}{2}^-$ hidden-charm pentaquark states in the $SU(3)$ decuplet within the heavy pentaquark chiral perturbation theory (HPChPT) up to the next-to-leading order (NLO). The one-loop self-energy corrections from the $π$, $K$, and $η$ mesons are evaluated. Taking $P_ψ^N(4312)$ and $P_{ψs}^Λ(4459)$ as inputs, we predict the masses of the doubly-strange and triply-strange decuplet members $P_{ψss}^N$ and $P_{ψsss}^Λ$ to be 4.607 GeV and 4.755 GeV, respectively. The mass splittings between adjacent strangeness layers are nearly constant at approximately 0.148 GeV. This equal-spacing pattern follows directly from the analytical structure of the NLO chiral corrections, while its actual magnitude is set by the low-energy constants (LECs). It indicates that the Gell--Mann--Okubo (GMO) relation, expected at the leading order of SU(3) flavor symmetry breaking, persists to good accuracy at the NLO.

</details>


### [157] [BSMScanner: a lightweight framework for beyond-the-Standard-Model global fits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15096)
*Mohamed Belfkir*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: BSMScanner 是一个声明式框架，用于超越标准模型（BSM）参数扫描，将模型定义与扫描算法解耦；通过 YAML 描述参数、质量矩阵、可观测量、有效性约束和似然项，自动构建依赖图并编译为类型安全的 C++ 核心，支持多种扫描引擎及插件扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统 BSM 参数扫描中模型实现与数值扫描逻辑紧耦合、重复编码、可复现性差、难以验证和维护的问题，提升理论-实验接口的模块化与可重用性。

Method: 采用声明式 YAML 规范定义 BSM 模型结构（含先验、质量矩阵、对角化、可观测量、有效性条件、似然项），经语法与语义验证后构建有向依赖图，静态编译为类型检查的 C++ 评估核；扫描引擎（如差分进化、盆地发现、MCMC 后验精炼）作为独立组件接入；提供标准味物理工具库（CKM/PMNS 构造、振荡似然查表、常见味观测量）及 C++/Python 插件接口处理非解析量（如遗迹密度）。

Result: 实现了完整、可验证、可扩展的 BSM 扫描框架；在六个基准模型（4–26 自由参数）上完成端到端验证，给出规范定义、扫描配置、最优拟合点及解析交叉检验结果；所有可观测量与似然项均通过命名导入，模型文件仅含模型特有定义。

Conclusion: BSMScanner 实现了 BSM 理论建模与数值推断的清晰分离，显著提升参数扫描的可复现性、可验证性与协作效率，为高精度味物理与暗物质联合约束提供了标准化基础设施。

Abstract: We present BSMScanner, a framework in which a beyond-the-Standard-Model (BSM) parameter scan is specified as a set of declarative YAML documents rather than written as a program. The user declares the free parameters and their priors, the mass matrices and the diagonalisations that act on them, the observables, the theory-validity conditions and the likelihood terms. The framework validates this specification, resolves it into a dependency graph and compiles it once into a typed C++ evaluation kernel, which is then driven by an independently chosen scanning engine: uniform sampling, two differential-evolution engines, or a staged basin-discovery orchestrator, optionally followed by Markov-chain posterior refinement. Flavour physics matrix diagonalisation, PMNS and CKM construction in fixed conventions, tabulated oscillation likelihoods and a library of common flavour observables are provided by the framework and imported by name, so that a model file contains only model-specific definitions; quantities without a closed form, such as a relic density, enter through a C++ or Python plugin interface. We describe the specification schema, the observable and likelihood library, the scanning engines and their configuration, and demonstrate the framework on six benchmark models with $4$ to $26$ free parameters, giving for each the specification, the scan configuration, the resulting best fit and analytic cross-checks of the result.

</details>


### [158] [Theoretical calculations for $t{\bar t}H$ production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15100)
*Nikolaos Kidonakis,Nodoka Yamanaka*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了ttH产生过程的高阶微扰修正，结合QCD和电弱（EW）相互作用，给出了LHC能量下近似N³LO QCD + NLO EW精度的总截面与微分截面，并显著改善了理论预言的精度与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升ttH产生过程理论预言的精度与可靠性，以匹配LHC实验测量精度，并减小强耦合常数与因子化/重整化尺度依赖性。

Method: 在NLO QCD与NLO EW基底上，叠加二阶与三阶软胶子辐射修正，构造近似N³LO QCD（aN³LO）+ NLO EW混合精度结果；采用软-共线有效理论（SCET）或固定阶软发射方法实现高阶软胶子重求和。

Result: aN³LO QCD + NLO EW结果使总截面显著增大，top夸克横动量p_T与快度y分布的理论不确定性（尤其是尺度依赖）明显减小，微分分布形状亦得到改进。

Conclusion: 高阶软胶子修正对ttH过程至关重要；aN³LO QCD + NLO EW是当前最先进且可靠的理论预言，为Higgs耦合测量及BSM物理探针提供坚实基准。

Abstract: We calculate higher-order perturbative corrections for $t{\bar t}H$ production. By adding second-order and third-order soft-gluon corrections to NLO QCD and NLO electroweak (EW) results, we present total and differential cross sections at LHC energies through approximate N$^3$LO (aN$^3$LO) in QCD and NLO in EW. Our aN$^3$LO QCD + NLO EW results provide significant enhancements and a reduced scale dependence for the cross section and the top-quark transverse-momentum and rapidity distributions.

</details>


### [159] [Cosmology & Astrophysics of the Dark Dimension with Bulk Majorana Neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15209)
*Luis A. Anchordoqui,Cumrun Vafa*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在暗维度体空间中携带B-L荷的惰性马约拉纳中微子，并指出其马约拉纳质量需≳100 GeV以避免KK中微子塔在早期宇宙中过量产生，从而解决额外维场景中的宇宙学中微子灾难；若质量处于该下限附近，还可通过5维长寿命黑洞的霍金辐射衰变至标准模型粒子，解释Fermi卫星观测到的伽马射线晕超出现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决额外维模型中因Kaluza-Klein中微子塔过量产生而导致的宇宙学中微子灾难（cosmological neutrino catastrophe）；同时尝试为Fermi-LAT观测到的银河中心GeV伽马射线晕超出现象提供新机制解释。

Method: 基于五维暗维度模型，引入带B-L荷的体中微子场；结合热历史约束（BBN、CMB及轻子数密度）、KK激发态丰度计算、以及5D原初黑洞霍金辐射谱与衰变链分析，推导马约拉纳质量下限。

Result: 得出体中微子马约拉纳质量必须满足M_N ≳ 100 GeV；该质量窗口可同时规避宇宙学约束并使5D黑洞蒸发产生的中微子衰变贡献与Fermi晕超额伽马射线通量定性一致。

Conclusion: B-L荷中微子在暗维度体空间中的质量尺度需高于~100 GeV，这不仅是宇宙学所要求的必要条件，还可能为高能天体物理反常现象提供统一解释，凸显了高维中微子物理与多信使宇宙学的深刻联系。

Abstract: Recently, the possibility of having sterile Majorana neutrinos charged under $B-L$ gauge symmetry in the bulk of the dark dimension has been explored. We point out a cosmological restriction on the Majorana mass of bulk neutrinos leading to mass $\gtrsim 100~{\rm GeV}$. This avoids the over-production of Kaluza-Klein neutrino towers in the early universe thus resolving the cosmological neutrino catastrophe of extra dimension scenarios. If the mass of the bulk neutrino is at the lower end of this bound, it can potentially also explain the recently observed Fermi halo excess on gamma rays through the evaporation of long-lived 5d (i.e. dark) black holes through Hawking radiation to bulk neutrinos, which promptly decay into standard model particles.

</details>


### [160] [When hadrons collide: di-J/psi structure in the final state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15248)
*Eric S. Swanson*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非奇异强子机制解释LHC上观测到的双J/ψ谱结构，通过紧致cc̄c̄c源在十个粲偶素对通道中的 rescattering，无需引入四夸克态即可再现数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LHC实验中双J/ψ不变质量谱中出现的结构，避免预设四夸克态假设，检验是否可用常规QCD多体动力学（阈值效应与 rescattering）解释。

Method: 构建2++角动量分波下紧致cc̄c̄c产生振幅，耦合至J/ψψ(nS)、χ_cJ χ_cK等十个开放粲偶素对S波通道，采用幺正化Lippmann-Schwinger方程处理多通道 rescattering；背景由三个自由参数描述。

Result: 模型以χ²/ndf = 56/50给出与实验数据相当吻合的拟合，关键峰结构对应J/ψψ(2S)、J/ψψ(3S)等阈值或阈值增强，未引入任何束缚态或共振态参数。

Conclusion: 双J/ψ谱中的结构可由粲偶素对阈值动力学与 rescattering 充分解释，不必然要求存在紧致全粲四夸克态；该结果对 exotic hadron 的唯象判据提出重要约束。

Abstract: The structures in the di-J/psi spectrum seen at the LHC are widely read as fully charmed tetraquarks. As an alternative scenario, the $2^{++}$ production amplitude for a compact $cc\bar c\bar c$ source rescattering through ten charmonium-pair channels is presented. The model is completely fixed except for three background parameters and provides a competitive description of the data, with chi-sq/ndf=56/50. Prominent features are driven by J/psi psi(nS) thresholds or threshold enhancement, with no need for exotic hadrons.

</details>


### [161] [A toy model for hadronic bonds in exotic hadrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15436)
*Hua-Xing Chen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了先前提出的强子共价键模型，统一纳入了产生键与湮灭键机制，用于描述近阈值奇特强子；引入有效激活能E_act表征海夸克四夸克组分，发现Z_c(3900)给出的E_act≈17 MeV与X_17粒子质量相近，暗示低能四夸克动力学可能具有共性；并对D* D̄* B* B̄* 四介子体系（含Z_c(4020)与Z_b(10650)产生键子系统）预言约36 MeV束缚能，建议在4μ与4μ2π末态中开展实验搜寻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一描述近阈值奇特强子（如Z_c、Z_b等）的分子态结构，需将先前孤立处理的三种强子键机制（共价键、产生键、湮灭键）纳入同一理论框架，并解释其质量分布规律及潜在的共同四夸克动力学起源。

Method: 基于强子分子图像，构建包含共价键（价轻夸克交换）、产生键（相干海q q̄激发形成有效共价关联）和湮灭键（价q q̄湮灭并混入紧致态）的统一有效模型；引入海四夸克组分的有效激活能E_act，结合已知实验态（如Z_c(3900)、Z_c(4020)、Z_b(10650)）进行参数标定与能谱预测。

Result: 建立统一键机制框架：共价键配置倾向位于阈值以下，产生键配置倾向位于阈值以上，湮灭键通过与紧致态混合可双向移动分子态位置；由Z_c(3900)标定E_act ≈ 17 MeV；预言D* D̄* B* B̄*四介子体系具有约36 MeV束缚能；指出E_act与X_17质量数值相近，提示低能四夸克普适动力学可能。

Conclusion: 三种强子键机制可在统一框架下协同解释近阈值奇特强子的质量分布与结构演化；E_act的物理含义指向海夸克凝聚驱动的低能四夸克自由度，其与X_17的数值关联虽尚未有微观机制支撑，但为探索超出传统qqq/q q̄范式的强子谱学提供了新线索；预言的D* D̄* B* B̄*束缚态为未来高亮度强子对撞实验（如BESIII、LHCb、Belle II）提供了明确探测通道。

Abstract: Three hadronic-bond mechanisms were proposed in our previous studies to describe near-threshold exotic hadrons in terms of hadronic molecular configurations: the covalent bond arises from the sharing and exchange of valence light quarks between hadronic clusters, the creation bond originates from the activation of coherent sea $q\bar{q}$ degrees of freedom followed by the formation of effective covalent correlations, and the annihilation bond arises from valence $q\bar{q}$ annihilation and the associated mixing with a nearby compact state. A simple toy model was previously constructed only for the covalent bond, and in the present study we extend this model to incorporate the creation and annihilation bonds within a unified framework. The resulting framework organizes covalent-bond configurations as tending to lie below threshold and creation-bond configurations as tending to appear above threshold, while the annihilation bond can shift a pre-existing molecular configuration either downward or upward through mixing with a nearby compact state. An effective activation energy $E_{\rm{act}}$ is introduced for the coherent sea four-quark component involved in the creation bond, with the $Z_c(3900)$ providing the benchmark value $E_{\rm{act}}\sim17~{\rm{MeV}}$. Intriguingly, this value is numerically close to the reported mass of the $X_{17}$ particle, which we previously interpreted as a low-energy bare four-quark structure. This numerical proximity may therefore reflect common low-energy (four-quark) dynamics, although the microscopic connection remains to be established. For the isoscalar $D^*\bar{D}^*B^*\bar{B}^*$ configuration involving the $Z_c(4020)$ and $Z_b(10650)$ creation-bond subsystems linked by two additional covalent bonds, we estimate a binding energy of approximately $36~{\rm{MeV}}$ below its four-meson threshold and propose searches in the $4μ$ and $4\mu2π$ final states.

</details>


### [162] [Probing the chromo-electric dipole moment of top quark near the toponium production threshold at LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15493)
*Kai Ma,Tong Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了顶夸克色电偶极矩（CEDM）对LHC上顶夸克偶素（toponium）产生过程的影响，特别是通过η_t共振态与连续谱tt̄干涉的角分布不对称性，给出CEDM参数d_t^g的预期排除限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMS和ATLAS在tt̄阈值附近观测到超出预期的信号，暗示可能存在S波、单态的顶夸克偶素；该非相对论系统对探测顶夸克反常偶极矩极为敏感。

Method: 在胶子融合主导的η_t（自旋0、颜色单态）近阈值共振产生中引入顶夸克CEDM修正的tt̄-胶子顶点；严格包含共振-连续谱干涉项；构造基于tt̄→ℓνb jj′衰变中带电轻子角分布的一组CP-偶/奇不对称观测量。

Result: 推导出利用轻子角不对称性对顶夸克CEDM参数d_t^g的95% CL实验排除限，显著优于现有间接约束；干涉效应使不对称性对d_t^g具有线性灵敏度。

Conclusion: 轻子角分布不对称性是探测顶夸克CEDM的高灵敏探针，证实了顶夸克偶素阈值物理在检验新物理CP破坏机制中的独特价值。

Abstract: The recent observation of an excess near the kinematic $t\bar{t}$ production threshold by the CMS and ATLAS collaborations suggests the existence of a color-singlet and $S$-wave quasi-bound state of top quark. Due to the non-relativistic nature of top and anti-top quarks in this threshold region, toponium production provides an ideal system to probe the anomalous dipole moments of the top quark. In this work, we investigate the impact of chromo-electric dipole moment (CEDM) in $t\bar{t}$-gluon vertex on toponium production at the LHC. We implement the enhanced near-threshold production of the spin-0 color-singlet toponium state $η_t$ via gluon fusion. The resonant toponium contribution is then combined with the continuum $t\bar{t}$ production amplitude, including the interference effects induced by the top CEDM. Furthermore, we construct a set of asymmetries based on the angular distributions of charged leptons in the leptonic decay channel of $t\bar{t}$. Finally, we derive the expected exclusion limit on the top quark CEDM parameter $d_t^g$ using these lepton angular asymmetries.

</details>


### [163] [Quantum circuit representation and incomplete Bell measurements in Compton scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15526)
*Wei Xie,Yu-Chen Guo,Shuang Li,Ji-Chong Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文将无质量树图级康普顿散射建模为电子与光子螺旋度构成的两量子比特操作，在贝尔基下分解为两个宇称扇区，引入一个非幺正滤波器实现不精确的不完全贝尔测量；该测量仅能区分贝尔宇称而无法分辨扇区内相对贝尔标签，且单独使用无法突破经典隐形传态阈值，需辅以完备贝尔基读出才能实现单位条件隐形传态保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将高能散射过程与量子信息概念（如贝尔测量、隐形传态）建立严格对应，探索基本相互作用的量子信息结构。

Method: 在零质量极限下构造康普顿散射的幺正S矩阵作用于电子-光子螺旋度希尔伯特空间；将其在贝尔基下对角化并分离宇称扇区；引入Kraus算符归一化的非幺正滤波器实现不完全贝尔测量；分析其对隐形传态保真度的影响。

Result: 获得康普顿散射的显式两量子比特量子线路表示；导出对应不精确不完全贝尔测量的Kraus算符；证明仅用该测量无法超越经典隐形传态保真度极限（2/3），而叠加完备贝尔测量后可达单位条件保真度。

Conclusion: 康普顿散射天然编码了受限贝尔测量能力，揭示了基本粒子散射与量子信息处理之间的深层结构联系；其量子线路表征为强场QED与量子信息交叉提供了新范式。

Abstract: We formulate massless tree-level Compton scattering as an effective two-qubit operation on electron and photon helicities. In the Bell basis, the scattering map separates into two parity sectors and admits a simple quantum circuit representation with one nonunitary filter. After Kraus normalization, this filter defines an unsharp incomplete Bell measurement that distinguishes the two Bell-parity sectors but not the relative Bell label within each sector. We further show that the incomplete measurement alone does not surpass the classical teleportation threshold, whereas a subsequent complete Bell-basis readout enables unit conditional teleportation fidelity.

</details>


### [164] [Modular $S_4$ Inverse Seesaw at the LZ High-Recoil Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15557)
*Waqas Ahmed,Vasileios Basiouris,George K. Leontaris*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于模群S4的统一框架，将轻子味物理（通过2NR+2S逆跷跷板机制）与高动量转移的类狄拉克暗物质（通过共享复模参数τ）联系起来；利用LUX-ZEPLIN实验在~270 keV核反冲能区的新分析，聚焦端吸散射主导的暗物质信号，并在mχ∈[300 GeV, 2 TeV]、δ∈[300, 400] keV区间找到最优拟合点(1.847 TeV, 371.5 keV)，接近地球实验室中弹性/非弹性散射的运动学上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LUX-ZEPLIN实验在高核反冲能区（≈270 keV）观测到的潜在异常信号，需超越传统WIMP范式；端吸暗物质（iDM）可自然压低低能事例、增强高能尾部事例，但需与轻子味结构协同建模以保证理论自洽与可检验性。

Method: 采用模不变有效场论方法：轻子部分由最小2NR+2S逆跷跷板实现，暗物质部分由一对SM单态费米子构成伪狄拉克态；两部分共享同一复模参数τ，使由轻子数据确定的模形式直接控制暗物质狄拉克质量与马约拉纳劈裂；对τ参数空间进行双阶段扫描——先在轻子扇区筛选χ²ℓ+ν<30的181447个解，再在LZ偏好的暗物质质量-劈裂区间（mχ∈[300 GeV, 2 TeV], δ∈[300, 400] keV）内对2×10⁶个点进行约束；信号建模包含端吸氙核运动学、Helm形因子、标准晕模型速度分布、高斯能量展宽及信号强度轮廓似然。

Result: 发现轻子扇区存在多个不连通的模区域容纳χ²ℓ+ν<30解，且首阶预言一个严格无质量的轻中微子；恢复物理标度后，重准狄拉克中性费米子位于TeV尺度，具有小非幺正性；在LZ导向区域中识别出与实验数据兼容的共同模参数解，最优似然点为(mχ, δ) ≈ (1.847 TeV, 371.5 keV)，位于地球实验室端吸散射的运动学边界附近。

Conclusion: 该构造首次实现了模对称性下轻子味物理、TeV逆跷跷板机制与高动量转移端吸暗物质的统一描述；共享模参数τ不仅提升理论经济性，更使中微子振荡数据可间接约束暗物质质量劈裂δ，为多信使粒子物理提供可证伪的新范式。

Abstract: The extension of the LUX--ZEPLIN nuclear-recoil analysis to approximately $270\,\mathrm{keV}$ probes a qualitatively new dark-matter regime in which endothermic scattering can suppress conventional low-energy recoils and concentrate the surviving spectrum near the Galactic high-velocity tail. We construct a modular-$S_4$ realization in which a minimal $2N_R+2S$ inverse seesaw accounts for lepton flavor, while a separate pair of Standard-Model-singlet fermions forms the pseudo-Dirac state relevant for high-recoil xenon scattering. The sectors share a common complex modulus $τ$, so the modular forms selected by charged-lepton and neutrino data also enter the operators generating the dark Dirac mass and Majorana splitting. Our scan yields $181447$ lepton-sector solutions satisfying $χ^2_{\ell+ν}<30$, distributed among several disconnected modular regions, and predicts one effectively massless light neutrino at leading order. Restoring the physical scales allows TeV-scale heavy quasi-Dirac neutral fermions with small non-unitarity. We subsequently scan $2\times10^6$ dark-sector points only over flavor-accepted values of $τ$ and identify common-modulus solutions in the LZ-oriented region $300\,\mathrm{GeV}\le m_χ\le2\,\mathrm{TeV}$ and $300\,\mathrm{keV}\leδ\le400\,\mathrm{keV}$. An exploratory two-bin Poisson likelihood, including endothermic xenon kinematics, the Helm form factor, Standard-Halo-Model velocities, Gaussian energy smearing, and profiling over the signal normalization, gives a best grid point at $(m_χ,δ)\simeq(1.847\,\mathrm{TeV},371.5\,\mathrm{keV})$, close to the terrestrial inelastic kinematic ceiling. The construction provides a direct link between modular lepton flavor, TeV-scale inverse-seesaw dynamics, and high-recoil inelastic dark matter.

</details>


### [165] [From Dirac Neutrino Mass to Dark Radiation: An $A_4$-Symmetric FIMP Dark Matter Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15618)
*Pritam Das,Anupam Deka,Debajyoti Dutta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于A4×Z2×U(1)L对称性的扩展标准模型，通过向量型中性费米子N和惰性标量η实现小Dirac中微子质量，并以Z2稳定的暗区费米子χ作为FIMP型暗物质；数值拟合NuFit 6.1数据，给出满足3σ振荡约束的Dirac Yukawa耦合区域；χ通过NL→χ+η非热产生，冻结入耦合λ∼10⁻⁵·⁵–10⁻⁷·⁵可复现Ω_DM h²≈0.12，且始终未热化；轻右手中微子贡献ΔN_eff≲0.14。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一解释小Dirac中微子质量与暗物质起源，避免Majorana机制及WIMP范式，引入FIMP机制与离散对称性保护，同时兼容中微子振荡数据与宇宙学观测（如Ω_DM h²、ΔN_eff）

Method: 构建A4×Z2×U(1)L对称性下的扩展模型，含向量型中性费米子N_{L,R}、惰性标量η（Z2-odd）、flavon φ与暗区费米子χ；数值拟合NuFit 6.1振荡参数约束Dirac Yukawa矩阵；求解Boltzmann方程计算χ的非热冻结入产额；检验Hubble率与相互作用率关系以确认FIMP机制有效性；估算轻RH中微子对ΔN_eff的热/非热贡献

Result: 确定满足3σ中微子振荡数据的Dirac耦合参数空间；获得χ质量与冻结入耦合λ的允许区间（λ∼10⁻⁵·⁵–10⁻⁷·⁵），成功复现Ω_DM h²≈0.12；验证χ始终未热化（Γ < H）；预测ΔN_eff最大值≈0.14

Conclusion: 该模型在单一对称性框架下自洽实现Dirac中微子质量生成与FIMP暗物质起源，且与当前中微子振荡、暗物质丰度及暗辐射观测兼容。

Abstract: We propose a unified $A_4\times Z_2\times U(1)_L$ framework for generating small Dirac neutrino masses and address dark matter through feeble interactions in an extended particle physics model. The model extends the Standard Model with a pair of heavy vector-like neutral $A_4$ fermions $N(N_{L,R})$, two additional flavon fields $η$ and $φ$, among which $η$ being a $Z_2$-odd inert scalar and a dark-sector vector-like fermion field $χ$. We numerically explore the Dirac-sector Yukawa couplings and identify the parameter region that is consistent with the {\tt NuFit 6.1} neutrino oscillation data at the $3σ$ level. The dark matter candidate $χ$, stabilized by the $Z_2$ symmetry, is produced non-thermally through the decay $N_L\rightarrowχ+η$. By solving the Boltzmann equation, we find that the observed relic abundance, $Ω_{\rm DM}h^2\simeq0.12$, can be reproduced for freeze-in couplings of order $λ\sim10^{-5.5}$--$10^{-7.5}$ over the considered dark matter mass range. For the chosen parameter space, we also verify the interaction rate remains below the Hubble expansion rate, confirming $χ$ never reaches thermal equilibrium. Thus, our results demonstrate that the model provides a consistent realization of Dirac neutrino masses and FIMP dark matter within a common symmetry-based setup. The presence of light Dirac RH neutrinos can contribute to the dark radiation and hence the effective degrees of freedom via thermal and non-thermal processes. We further estimate the contribution of the light right-handed neutrinos to \(ΔN_{\rm eff}\), finding a maximum characteristic value of \(ΔN_{\rm eff}\simeq0.14\) in the benchmark parameter region.

</details>


### [166] [Dalitz plot binning optimization for the model-independent measurement of the CKM angle $β$ with $B^0 \to D π^+ π^-$, $D \to K_S^0 π^+ π^-$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15839)
*Anfeng Li,Wenbin Qian,Boan Shi,Ao Xu,Liming Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Fisher信息量和模拟退火算法的Dalitz图分箱优化方法，用于提升B⁰→Dπ⁺π⁻（D→Kₛ⁰π⁺π⁻）时间依赖分析中CKM角β的测量灵敏度，相对等相位差基准提升约10%，并验证了其对实验效应的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高模型无关测量CKM角β的统计灵敏度，因B⁰→Dπ⁺π⁻ Dalitz图分箱方案直接影响β的测量精度。

Method: 采用基于Fisher信息量的质量因子Q²作为优化目标函数，结合模拟退火算法对B⁰→Dπ⁺π⁻ Dalitz图进行分箱优化，并通过无实验效应的toy Monte Carlo模拟验证性能。

Result: 优化分箱使β的统计灵敏度相对等相位差基准提升约10%；鲁棒性分析表明该提升在衰变时间接受度、Dalitz图效率与分辨率、背景贡献等实际效应下仍保持稳定。

Conclusion: 基于Fisher信息与模拟退火的Dalitz图分箱优化是提升时间依赖CP破坏测量精度的有效策略，为LHCb、Belle II等实验提供可直接应用的分箱方案。

Abstract: A model-independent measurement of the CKM angle $β$ can be performed using a time-dependent analysis of $B^0 \to D π^+ π^-$, $D \to K_S^0 π^+ π^-$ decays, where the Dalitz plots of both the $B$ and $D$ decays are divided into bins. The choice of binning scheme for the $B^0 \to D π^+ π^-$ Dalitz plot directly impacts the statistical sensitivity to $β$. This paper presents an optimization of this binning using a Fisher information-based quality factor $Q^2$ and simulated annealing. The optimized binning improves the sensitivity to $β$ by approximately $10\%$ relative to the equal-phase-difference baseline, as validated by toy Monte Carlo studies without experimental effects. The robustness of this improvement against decay time acceptance, Dalitz plot efficiency, Dalitz plot resolution, and background contributions is also assessed.

</details>


### [167] [Axionic Casimir Torque](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15825)
*Soham Ghatak*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在存在轴子梯度的情况下，卡西米尔效应的修正形式，指出轴子梯度破坏旋转对称性，导致平板间出现与梯度平方及角度正弦成正比的宏观扭转力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究轴子场梯度如何打破真空各向同性，从而在卡西米尔系统中诱导出新型机械响应（即扭矩），为轴子暗物质探测提供新机制。

Method: 基于量子电动力学与轴子-光子耦合有效拉氏量，在存在空间依赖轴子场梯度的背景下，计算修正的真空零点能及其对边界条件（平行板）的应力张量贡献，导出扭矩表达式。

Result: 得到平板所受宏观扭矩为 $\vec{\tau} \propto \vec{b}^2 \sin(2\vartheta)$，其中 $\vec{b} = \nabla a$ 为轴子梯度，$\vartheta$ 为梯度方向与板法向夹角。

Conclusion: 轴子梯度可将标准卡西米尔吸引力建构为各向异性真空应力，产生可观测的扭转信号，构成一种新型轴子耦合探针。

Abstract: In the canonical Casimir effect, the quantum vacuum is isotropic, and the minimization of vacuum energy drives only a linear attractive force between the plates. However, the introduction of an axion gradient breaks this rotational symmetry. It can be demonstrated that these asymmetric zero-point fluctuations exert a macroscopic mechanical twist on the plates proportional to $\vec{b}^2 \sin (2\vartheta)$ where $\vec{b}$ is the spatial axion gradient and $\vartheta$ is the angle between the axion gradient and the normal to the plates.

</details>


### [168] [Conserved-charge diffusion in hot and dense QCD matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16829)
*Dushmanta Sahu,Kangkan Goswami,Raghunath Sahoo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于三味NJL模型研究了高温高密夸克物质中重子数、电荷与奇异数的耦合扩散，计算了包含对角与非对角元的完整扩散矩阵，并分析了广义电荷扩散率与迁移率，揭示了重子与奇异数迁移率在有限重子化学势下的交叉行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提供重子富集QCD物质中守恒荷耦合输运的模型基准，需在自洽生成夸克有效质量的框架下研究多荷耦合扩散动力学。

Method: 采用三味Nambu-Jona-Lasinio模型，在自洽求解构成夸克质量基础上，运用弛豫时间近似动理学方法计算完整三阶扩散矩阵（含所有对角与非对角输运系数），并结合守恒荷涨落关联函数（susceptibilities）导出广义扩散率与迁移率。

Result: 发现非对角输运系数具有显著依赖于温度与重子化学势的 Flavor-dependent 行为；在有限 μ_B 下，重子数与奇异数迁移率随密度变化发生交叉；所有扩散系数均体现夸克味组成对载流子性质的调控。

Conclusion: NJL模型预言了重子富集夸克物质中守恒荷输运存在强耦合效应与非平凡密度依赖性，尤其迁移率交叉现象可作为QCD相结构与临界区域输运特征的重要探针。

Abstract: We study the coupled diffusion of baryon number, electric charge, and strangeness in hot and dense quark matter using a three-flavor Nambu-Jona-Lasinio model with self-consistently generated constituent quark masses. The complete diffusion matrix is evaluated within a relaxation-time kinetic approach, including its diagonal and off-diagonal components. We find that the off-diagonal transport coefficients exhibit distinct temperature and baryon-chemical-potential dependence associated with the flavor-dependent composition of the charge carriers. We further investigate generalized charge diffusivities and mobilities obtained from the corresponding susceptibilities. At finite baryon chemical potential, the baryon and strangeness mobility scales cross, reflecting the different density dependence of their transport coefficients and susceptibilities. The results provide model benchmarks for coupled conserved-charge transport in baryon-rich QCD matter.

</details>


### [169] [Testing Minimal $U(2)^3$ Right-Handed Flavor Breaking in a Singlet-Scalar Vector-Like Quark Portal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15911)
*Qazi Maaz Us Salam*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个Z₂-奇标量单态与矢量型下型夸克构成的味破缺门户模型，通过右手中性手征破缺源将耦合从b_R延拓至d_R和s_R，从而统一描述b→s、b→d与s→d跃迁；计算了一圈偶极子与ΔF=2威尔逊系数，指出实标量在一圈阶不产生ΔF=2振幅；利用B介子混合、ε_K与辐射衰变共7个观测量的轮廓似然分析（CKM/格点/短距输入作为nuisance参数），比较了通用spurion、最小U(2)³及CKM对齐三种味结构假设；当前数据无法区分这些结构（SM兼容性p=0.20），所有轻夸克耦合方向均未被95% CL排除；对MD ≈ mS = 1 TeV、|λb| = 1，95% CL上限为εR < 0.227（通用）或0.211（U(2)³）；未来Bs混合相位、sin2β精度提升及格点/CKM输入改进有望检验味结构最小性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验超越标准模型（BSM）中味破缺结构的可证伪性，特别是通过单一耦合向量统一描述ΔF=1（b→s,d, s→d）与ΔF=2过程，并评估不同味对称性假设（如U(2)³ vs CKM-aligned）在当前实验精度下的可区分性。

Method: 构建Z₂-奇标量S与矢量型下型夸克D的门户模型，引入右手中性手征spurion实现flavor violation；解析计算一圈级电弱偶极算符C7,8与ΔF=2算符Q1,2的威尔逊系数；采用七维观测量（ΔMBs, ΔMBd, ϕs, ϕd, εK, BR(B→Xsγ), BR(Bs→μ+μ−)）的profile likelihood分析，将CKM矩阵元（|Vcb|, γ）、格点QCD输入（fBs√BBs等）及短距跑动参数设为nuisance参数；对比generic spurion、minimal U(2)³（轻夸克耦合由U(2)³对称性约束但尺度自由）与CKM-aligned三种味结构。

Result: 实标量S在一圈阶不贡献ΔF=2振幅（因色与手征对称性导致费曼图抵消）；当前数据无法排除任何味结构：SM拟合p值为0.20，所有εR方向均在95% CL内；对MD = mS = 1 TeV, |λb| = 1，95% CL上限为εR < 0.227（generic）与εR < 0.211（U(2)³）；关键灵敏度来自ϕs（Bs混合相位）与sin2β（Bd混合相位）的测量精度。

Conclusion: 现有B物理数据尚不足以检验该门户模型中的味结构最小性（如U(2)³）；若未来LHCb/Belle-II观测到偏离SM的信号，结合ϕs、sin2β精度提升及格点/CKM输入改进，该模型将成为探测味对称性破缺模式的关键探针。

Abstract: We study a portal of a $Z_2$-odd singlet scalar and a vector like down quark, in which a right handed flavor breaking spurion extends the portal coupling from $b_R$ to $d_R$ and $s_R$. This single coupling vector links the $b\to s$, $b\to d$, and $s\to d$ transitions. We compute the one loop dipole and $ΔF=2$ Wilson coefficients, and show that a real scalar generates no $ΔF=2$ amplitude at one loop. Using a seven observable profile likelihood of $B$ meson mixing, $ε_K$, and radiative decays, with the CKM, lattice, and short distance inputs treated as nuisance parameters, we compare a generic spurion, minimal $U(2)^3$ with a free size of the light quark couplings, and a CKM-aligned ansatz. We have found that the present data cannot yet test the flavor structure: the Standard Model is compatible with the data, with a pseudo experiment $p$-value of $0.20$, and no direction of the light quark couplings can be excluded at 95% CL. For $M_D\simeq m_{\mathcal{S}}=1$ TeV and $|λ_b|=1$, the relative light quark coupling $ε_R$ is bounded at 95% CL by $0.227$ for a generic spurion and by $0.211$ for minimal $U(2)^3$. More precise measurements of the $B_s$ mixing phase at the LHC and of $\sin2β$ at LHCb and Belle-II, together with improved lattice and CKM inputs, can make the minimality of the flavor structure testable if a deviation from the Standard Model is observed.

</details>


### [170] [Event Generation for Neutrino and Muon DIS at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16836)
*Paulina Hernandez-Sainz,Felix Kling,Jelle Koorn,Juan Rojo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了多种蒙特卡洛事件生成器（Sherpa、Herwig7、POWHEG）与解析结构函数计算（YADISM）及中微子专用模拟工具（GENIE、NuWro、GiBUU）在LHC前向中微子/缪子深度非弹性散射（DIS）中的预测，重点评估高阶QCD修正、核PDF、部分子喷注与强子化建模、QED辐射等效应对FASER/SND@LHC物理分析的影响，并给出对最新实验数据、奇异夸克PDF敏感性及半包容DIS强子产额的若干应用研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FASER和SND@LHC实验在LHC前向区域探测TeV能标中微子与缪子，为中微子深度非弹性散射（DIS）提供全新实验室平台；但其物理潜力的实现依赖于中微子/缪子诱导的中性流（NC）与带电流（CC）DIS过程的高精度模拟。

Method: 采用NLO精度的一般用途MC生成器（Sherpa、Herwig7、POWHEG）与解析结构函数框架（YADISM）及中微子专用模拟器（GENIE、NuWro、GiBUU）进行系统比对；量化QCD高阶修正、(核)PDF选择、部分子喷注与强子化模型、QED轫致辐射对包含观测量及FASER接受度内可观测量的影响。

Result: 现代MC生成器在统一设置下对包含及微分DIS观测量（如x、Q²分布）达成良好一致性；但在强子末态敏感观测量（如强子横向动量、多重数）上仍存在显著差异；QED辐射与核PDF（尤其奇异夸克）对dimuon末态与半包容强子产额具有可观测影响。

Conclusion: FASER/SND@LHC的DIS物理分析需谨慎处理强子化与QED辐射建模的系统不确定性；当前MC生成器已能满足大部分包容性分析需求，但针对强子末态精密测量（如奇异PDF提取、强子碎裂函数约束），仍需结合YADISM解析计算与专用中微子模拟进行交叉校准。

Abstract: Neutrinos and muons produced in the forward direction of LHC collisions and detected by the FASER and SND@LHC experiments enable a comprehensive deep-inelastic scattering (DIS) programme, covering both neutral and charged currents, at TeV energies. This programme aims to provide stringent constraints on proton and nuclear structure as well as on hadron fragmentation, and to offer novel insight into neutrino interactions at the highest energies achieved in a laboratory. Realising this potential requires accurate and precise simulations of neutral- and charged-current DIS. We systematically compare the predictions of NLO general-purpose Monte Carlo (MC) event generators for neutrino- and muon-initiated DIS at the LHC (Sherpa, Herwig7, and POWHEG) against analytical structure-function calculations (YADISM) and dedicated neutrino simulation packages (GENIE, NuWro, GiBUU). Within this framework, we quantify the role of higher-order QCD corrections, (nuclear) parton distribution functions (PDFs), parton shower and hadronisation modelling, and QED radiation both for inclusive observables and for the FASER fiducial selections. We also present representative applications to the FASER neutrino programme: comparisons with its most recent data from the emulsion and electronic sub-detectors, a study of strange PDF sensitivity in the dimuon final state, and predictions for charged-hadron production in semi-inclusive DIS. Our analysis highlights the agreement of modern event generators for inclusive and differential DIS observables once common settings are adopted, while differences remain for observables sensitive to the hadronic final state.

</details>


### [171] [Probing LZ-Motivated TeV Higgsino Dark Matter through Heavier Sparticles at LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16156)
*Xintian Wang,Jin Min Yang,Yuanfang Yue,Yang Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了TeV尺度Higgsino作为暗物质候选者对LUX-ZEPLIN核反冲超出的解释，并系统评估了LHC Run-2与HL-LHC对相关超对称粒子（如gluino、squark、stop/sbottom、slepton及electroweakino）的探测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LUX-ZEPLIN实验观测到的高能核反冲超出，同时应对TeV级压缩Higgsino暗物质在直接探测中难以被LHC传统搜寻覆盖的挑战。

Method: 基于TeV尺度Higgsino暗物质模型，结合LHC Run-2与HL-LHC的多通道超对称粒子产生与衰变模拟，进行参数空间排除与发现潜力分析，重点考察gluino、light-flavor squark、stop/sbottom、slepton及electroweakino的产额与衰变拓扑。

Result: Run-2可排除gluino质量至~2.2 TeV；HL-LHC可将gluino排除上限提升至2.5–2.6 TeV，发现灵敏度达2.3–2.4 TeV；light-flavor squark共湮灭区暂未被Run-2覆盖，但HL-LHC在乐观假设下或可触及下边界；stop/sbottom基准点未被Run-2排除；slepton与重electroweakino产额过低，HL-LHC不可达。

Conclusion: TeV Higgsino暗物质虽难被直接探测，但可通过关联超对称谱（尤其是gluino）在LHC上实现间接检验；HL-LHC对gluino具有显著物理潜力，是验证该框架的关键设施。

Abstract: The nearly pure TeV-scale Higgsino dark matter provides a natural explanation for the high-energy nuclear-recoil excess reported by LUX-ZEPLIN. Such a heavy and compressed Higgsino spectrum makes direct searches at the LHC challenging. We therefore investigate complementary probes through the production and decay of heavier sparticles. We perform a comprehensive analysis and obtain the following observations: (i) For a TeV Higgsino, the current LHC Run-2 searches exclude gluino up to approximately 2.2~TeV, while the HL-LHC can give an exclusion reach of 2.5--2.6~TeV and a discovery reach of 2.3--2.4~TeV; (ii) The light-flavor squark coannihilation window lies beyond the Run-2 reach, but its lower end could be excluded at the HL-LHC under optimistic assumptions; (iii) The Run-2 search does not exclude the stop or sbottom benchmarks; (iv) The slepton and heavier-electroweakino productions are too small to access at the HL-LHC.

</details>


### [172] [Charged Lepton Flavor Violation: A Theory Overview](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17134)
*P. S. Bhupal Dev*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了带电轻子味违反（cLFV）作为超出标准模型新物理的最干净探针之一的理论意义，并总结了当前实验现状，阐明不同测量如何诊断潜在的新物理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电轻子味违反在标准模型中被极度压制（loop压低且GIM抑制），因此任何可观测信号将明确指示新物理；其过程干净、背景可控，是BSM物理的理想探针。

Method: 采用理论导向的综述方法，系统梳理cLFV过程（如μ→eγ、μ→3e、μ-e转换等）的有效算符框架、模型依赖与模型无关分析策略，并关联不同实验观测量对新物理质量尺度、耦合结构及味模式的区分能力。

Result: 厘清了各类cLFV过程对不同BSM场景（如SUSY、Leptoquarks、Z′、Heavy Neutrinos等）的敏感度差异；指出μ→e转换在核介质中的测量（如Mu2e、COMET）对含手征耦合的新物理具有独特约束力；强调多通道联合分析可破除简并、提取新物理的洛伦兹与味结构。

Conclusion: cLFV不仅是高灵敏度新物理探测窗口，更是实现‘新物理谱学’（New Physics Spectroscopy）的关键途径——通过跨过程、跨实验的协同分析，可重构新物理的有效拉氏量，进而判别其紫外完成机制。

Abstract: We present a theory-oriented overview of the significance of charged-lepton flavor violation -- one of the cleanest windows onto physics beyond the Standard Model. We also summarize the current experimental landscape and illustrate how different measurements can diagnose the underlying new physics.

</details>


### [173] [Nonequilibrium framework for first-order phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16385)
*Jie Liu,Ligong Bian*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用Schwinger-Keldysh闭时间路径形式论研究一阶电弱相变的非平衡动力学，推导了φ⁶模型及双标量场模型的有效作用量与耦合Langevin方程，为泡成核模拟、重子生成与引力波信号预测提供理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热平衡下有效势分析无法刻画一阶相变的真实非平衡动力学过程，而该过程对电弱重子生成和引力波产生至关重要。

Method: 采用实时间有限温度场论的Schwinger-Keldysh闭时间路径（CTP）形式论，推导φ⁶单场模型与双标量场模型的有效作用量，并导出对应的（耦合）Langevin随机微分方程。

Result: 获得φ⁶模型的非平衡有效作用量与Langevin方程；推广至双标量场情形，得到耦合Langevin方程组；确立适用于泡成核数值模拟的非平衡动力学理论框架。

Conclusion: Schwinger-Keldysh CTP形式论是描述电弱一阶相变非平衡动力学的严格工具，所获Langevin方程为后续格点模拟、重子数演化与引力波谱计算提供了微观基础。

Abstract: The first-order phase transition is central to electroweak baryogenesis and gravitational wave generation, yet thermal equilibrium effective potential analyses cannot describe the real-time non-equilibrium dynamics of the phase transition process. We use the Schwinger-Keldysh closed-time-path formalism of real-time finite-temperature field theory to study these effects. For the $φ^6$ model, we derive the effective action and the corresponding Langevin equation. We further extend the formalism to the two scalar fields case, obtaining the coupled Langevin equations. These results provide a theoretical framework for the bubble nucleation simulation of the non-equilibrium phase transition process, laying the foundation for reliable predictions of baryogenesis and gravitational wave signals.

</details>


### [174] [Implications for new physics solutions to the LSND and MiniBooNE anomalies after MicroBooNE results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17207)
*Waleed Abdallah,Raj Gandhi,Samiran Roy*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: MicroBooNE实验对LSND/MiniBooNE中观测到的电子类事例超出进行了系统检验，排除了光子与单电子起源，但允许一类轻赝标量暗中介子（m ≤ 30 MeV）衰变至e⁺e⁻的模型；该模型暗示中微子为狄拉克粒子，并预言O(100) MeV量级的暗重惰性中微子（HNLs），并涉及CP破坏与紫外完备性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LSND与MiniBooNE实验中长期存在的、统计显著的电子类事例超出，该超出无法由标准模型解释，且其物理起源（e⁻, γ 或 e⁺e⁻）在前两代实验中无法区分。

Method: 基于MicroBooNE高精度粒子鉴别能力，系统分析各类SM背景及新物理信号（尤其聚焦光子、电子、e⁺e⁻对产生机制），结合其最新搜索结果与其它实验约束（如束流dump、固定靶、宇宙学等），对理论模型进行相容性评估与分类裁剪，并构造满足所有约束的新赝标量暗中介子模型。

Result: MicroBooNE数据排除了以单光子或单电子为主要信号源的多数解释；但允许轻质量（≤30 MeV）、赝标耦合主导的中性自旋零暗中介子通过e⁺e⁻衰变产生事例；该模型可同时满足LSND/MB超出、MicroBooNE无单电子超出、以及Borexino、NA62等实验限制。

Conclusion: LSND/MiniBooNE超出尚未被排除，其可能源于超出标准模型的新物理——具体为轻赝标暗中介子及其关联的Dirac中微子结构与O(100) MeV暗重惰性中微子；未来MicroBooNE与SBND在非相干散射道的联合分析将是关键判据。

Abstract: Over the past couple of decades, the Liquid Scintillator Neutrino Detector (LSND) and MiniBooNE (MB) neutrino experiments have provided statistically significant evidence of persistent and as yet unexplained electron-like event excesses. The MicroBooNE experiment, with sensitive particle identification capabilities designed to shed light on these anomalous results, has conducted a number of analyses to test whether the excess is due to photons, electrons or $e^+e^-$ pair productions, all of which give almost identical signals in LSND and MB. Recent MicroBooNE searches, in addition to checking Standard Model (SM) processes, have focused on proposed new physics scenarios involving new mediators and dark portals beyond the SM. We study the consequences and effects of these analyses for the many theoretical proposals put forth to generate the three types of signals above in order to understand the observed excesses. We discuss which classes of solutions are allowed, disallowed or require further scrutiny involving theoretical and experimental efforts. We give an example of a new solution that survives the restrictions imposed by MicroBooNE results and other existing bounds. We point out that a light ($m\leq 30$ MeV) dark neutral spin zero mediator with dominantly pseudoscalar couplings, decaying to an $e^+e^-$ pair, can account for the observations. We indicate that if such a model is realised, it may point to the possibility that neutrinos are Dirac particles, and that there are dark Heavy Neutral Leptons (HNLs) of mass ${\cal O}(100)$ MeV. We discuss issues related to CP violation and ultra-violet completion of these models. Finally we stress that a search for the excess in incoherent scattering channels in MicroBooNE and SBND could help clarify the physics behind these persistent anomalies.

</details>


### [175] [Probing first-generation doublet vector-like leptons via single production at the 3 TeV CLIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16413)
*Yin-Hao Gao,Yao-Bei Liu,Stefano Moretti*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在3 TeV的紧凑型线性对撞机（CLIC）上单产生第一代SU(2)_L双重态矢量类轻子（VLLs）的过程，分别通过中性与带电VLL道进行探测，并给出了不同积分亮度下的排除与发现灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超出标准模型的新物理，特别是矢量类轻子（VLLs）——一类能缓解味物理反常并保持电弱精度观测兼容性的热门BSM候选者；CLIC作为高能e⁺e⁻对撞机，在高亮度与干净环境方面具有独特优势。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛模拟（含ISR、beamstrahlung与探测器效应），对e⁺e⁻ → νₑN̄ + ν̄ₑN（中性VLL）和e⁺e⁻ → e±E∓（带电VLL）过程开展基于逐级截断（cut-based）的分析；中性VLL重建于N → W∓e±（W → jj），末态为e±+2j+Ẽ_T；带电VLL重建于E± → e±h（h→bb̄）与E± → e±Z（Z→qq̄），末态分别为e⁺e⁻+2b与e⁺e⁻+2j。

Result: 中性VLL通道灵敏度最优：在1 ab⁻¹下，2σ排除可达ε ≈ 0.013（M_N = 1.3 TeV）至 ε ≈ 0.065（M_N = 2.9 TeV）；5σ发现区间为ε ≈ 0.02–0.10；5 ab⁻¹下，带电VLL的e±h与e±Z道分别可探测M_E up to ~2.9 TeV与~2.8 TeV。

Conclusion: 3 TeV CLIC可在宽质量范围（达~2.9 TeV）内对第一代SU(2)_L双重态VLL提供强约束，尤其对小混合角ε ≲ 0.01情形仍具探测能力，显著拓展VLL参数空间的实验覆盖。

Abstract: The single production of first-generation $\mathrm{SU}(2)_L$ doublet vector-like leptons (VLLs) at the Compact Linear Collider (CLIC) with $\sqrt{s}=3~\mathrm{TeV}$ is studied. The neutral and charged VLL states are investigated through $e^+e^-\rightarrowν_e\bar N+\barν_eN$ and $e^+e^-\rightarrow e^\pm E^\mp$, respectively. The neutral VLL is searched for in the $N\rightarrow W^\mp e^\pm$ channel with $W^\pm\rightarrow jj$, leading to the $e^\pm+2j+\slashed{E}_T$ final state. The charged VLL is explored through $E^\pm\rightarrow e^\pm h$ ($h\rightarrow b\bar b$) and $E^\pm\rightarrow e^\pm Z$ ($Z\rightarrow q\bar q$), yielding the $e^+e^-+2b$ and $e^+e^-+2j$ signatures. Signal and background simulations include initial-state radiation, beamstrahlung, and detector effects. Cut-based analyses are performed for integrated luminosities of $1$, $3$, and $5~\mathrm{ab}^{-1}$ to obtain the exclusion and discovery sensitivities in the VLL mass--mixing parameter space. The neutral-VLL channel gives the best sensitivity. At $1~\mathrm{ab}^{-1}$, the $2σ$ exclusion reaches $\varepsilon\simeq0.013$ at $M_N=1.3~\mathrm{TeV}$ and $\varepsilon\simeq0.065$ at $M_N=2.9~\mathrm{TeV}$, while the $5σ$ discovery reach corresponds to $\varepsilon\simeq0.02$--$0.10$. At $5~\mathrm{ab}^{-1}$, the charged-VLL channels probe masses up to about $2.9~\mathrm{TeV}$ and $2.8~\mathrm{TeV}$ in the $e^\pm h$ and $e^\pm Z$ modes, respectively. These results demonstrate that a 3 TeV CLIC can provide sensitive probes of first-generation doublet VLLs over a wide mass range, including scenarios with small VLL--electron mixing.

</details>


### [176] [Neutrino Tridents and $W$-boson production at the Muon Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17263)
*Felix Kling,Toni Mäkelä,Sebastian Trojanowski,Bei Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了缪子对撞机作为高能中微子源在探测中微子三重态（trident）与壳层W玻色子产生过程中的潜力，预测十年内可在100 kg硅基活性靶中获得超百万级trident事例和十万级W事例，并指出该测量可超越DUNE对新矢量/轴矢量相互作用的灵敏度，同时以前所未有的精度约束大x区光子分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用缪子对撞机产生的极高通量TeV级中微子，开展传统加速器难以实现的稀有中微子过程测量，拓展中微子物理与强子结构研究前沿。

Method: 基于缪子对撞机中微子束流谱、硅基探测器响应及标准模型截面计算，结合背景估计与信号判选策略，进行事例率模拟与灵敏度分析。

Result: 预测10年运行在100 kg硅探测器中可观测>10^6个中微子三重态事例和~10^5个壳层W玻色子事例；可对新矢量/轴矢量耦合给出优于DUNE的约束；W产生阈值附近事例可显著提升对PDF中高-x光子分布的约束精度。

Conclusion: 缪子对撞机中微子实验为首次观测中微子三重态与壳层W产生提供了独特平台，兼具新物理探针与QCD结构函数精密测量双重价值，对探测器粒度、辐射耐受与本底抑制提出明确要求。

Abstract: Muon colliders are exceptionally intense sources of multi-TeV neutrinos, allowing even relatively small detectors to record large rates of neutrino interactions. In addition to serving as neutrino-ion collider for precision measurements of neutrino deep inelastic scattering, this setup allow to study even rare neutrino processes with at high statistics. In this study, we investigate the feasibility of measuring neutrino trident and on-shell W-boson production, predicting over one million trident events and one hundred thousand W-boson events in a 100 kg silicon-based active target over ten years and allowing to detect these processes for the first time. We further show that these measurements could constrain new vector and axial-vector interactions beyond projected DUNE sensitivities and provide unprecedented constraints on the high-x photon distribution through W-boson production near threshold. We also discuss strategies for signal-background discrimination and the resulting detector requirements.

</details>


### [177] [Concurrence Dynamics in Self-Gravitating Mixed Neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16508)
*Raoul Serao,Gianpaolo Torre,Antonio Capolupo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了牛顿自引力在混合中微子双粒子系统中诱导的纠缠，利用concurrence量化该纠缠；发现哈密顿量可分解为局域单粒子项与质量表象下的有效Ising相互作用项，后者是纠缠产生的唯一来源；对初态为可分味态的情形，得到了concurrence的闭式解析解，其振幅由混合角决定、周期由引力耦合强度决定；进一步建立了concurrence与约化态纯度之间的一一对应关系；并证明该纠缠动力学与Majorana相无关，Dirac与Majorana参数化给出相同结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究牛顿引力是否能在量子多体系统中产生可观测的纠缠效应，特别是在中微子振荡这一真实物理背景下检验引力的量子信息效应。

Method: 基于中微子质量算符间的引力耦合构造两粒子哈密顿量，在质量表象下分离出局域项与非局域Ising型引力相互作用项；对初态为可分味态的情形，严格求解时间演化并计算concurrence；对比约化态纯度，建立量子信息量之间的等价性；分析Majorana相在纠缠动力学中的作用。

Result: 1) 引力诱导纠缠完全由质量表象下的有效Ising相互作用承载；2) concurrence具有闭式解析解，呈振荡行为，最大值仅依赖于PMNS混合角θ，振荡周期由引力耦合强度G m²/r决定；3) concurrence与约化态纯度一一对应；4) 纠缠动力学与Majorana相无关，Dirac与Majorana中微子给出相同concurrence演化。

Conclusion: 牛顿自引力可在混合中微子系统中产生可定量刻画的、非平凡的量子纠缠；该纠缠是纯粹由引力相互作用驱动的，且其动力学具有简洁的解析结构和参数鲁棒性（对Majorana相不敏感），为未来探测引力介导的量子关联提供了理论基准与可观测量。

Abstract: We study the entanglement generated by Newtonian self-gravity in a two-particle system of mixed neutrinos and quantify it by means of the concurrence. Starting from the gravitational coupling between the mass operators, we show that the two-particle Hamiltonian splits into local single-particle terms and an effective Ising interaction in the mass basis, the latter being solely responsible for the generation of correlations. For initially separable flavor states the concurrence is obtained in closed analytic form: it oscillates between zero and a maximum fixed by the mixing angle alone, while the gravitational coupling sets the recurrence time. We further establish a one-to-one correspondence between the concurrence and the reduced-state purity previously derived for this model, thus providing an independent quantum-information description of the same gravitationally induced correlations. Finally, we show that the concurrence does not depend on the Majorana phase, so that Dirac and Majorana parametrizations yield identical entanglement dynamics. These results isolate the genuinely entangling sector of the gravitational evolution and provide a compact analytic characterization of gravity-induced quantum correlations in mixed neutrino systems.

</details>


### [178] [Parton and nuclear dynamics at large $x$: the CJ26 global QCD analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17485)
*Alberto Accardi,Matteo Cerutti,Cynthia E. Keppel,Shujie Li,J. F. Owens,Sanghwa Park,Peter Risse*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: CJ26 是一个新型全局QCD分析，首次在全局拟合中纳入全部JLab 6 GeV和首批12 GeV深度非弹性散射（DIS）数据，同时拟合质子部分子分布函数（PDFs）与氘核靶的核效应；重点处理大-x区域，并协同约束高扭项（higher-twist）效应与离壳修正（off-shell corrections）；结果以NLO PDFs（LHAPDF格式）及配套结构函数公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升大-x区域核效应与部分子结构联合建模的精度，解决低能DIS中高扭项与离壳修正的简并性问题，并量化实验系统误差相关性对PDF拟合的影响。

Method: 基于χ²最小化框架的全局QCD拟合，联合拟合质子PDFs与氘核核效应参数；显式引入x-dependent higher-twist项与核依赖的off-shell修正；采用JLab 6/12 GeV inclusive DIS数据约束大-x行为；严格处理实验协方差矩阵中的系统误差相关性。

Result: 获得新一代NLO CJ26 PDFs，显著改善x > 0.3区域的quark（特别是d-quark）与gluon分布约束；量化了higher-twist与off-shell效应对F₂^d/F₂^p比值的~5–10%影响；揭示系统误差建模对χ²/DOF及PDF不确定性的重要影响。

Conclusion: CJ26确立了在轻核（如氘）中协同提取核效应与部分子动力学的可行范式；强调未来高精度JLab 12 GeV与EIC数据对解耦核多体效应与部分子演化的关键作用；发布的PDFs与结构函数为大-x QCD研究提供基准工具。

Abstract: We present the new CTEQ-JLab global QCD analysis, called CJ26, that simultaneously fits parton distribution functions of the proton and nuclear effects in deuteron targets. This work includes, for the first time in a global fit, the full set of JLab 6 GeV inclusive DIS measurements and the first published JLab 12 GeV data. The analysis focuses on the large-$x$ region, and carefully treats the interplay between higher-twist effects and off-shell corrections in low-energy proton and deuteron target data. We discuss their effect on quark and gluon distributions, and highlight the importance of quantifying the experimental correlated systematic uncertainty to achieve fits of the highest accuracy. We also outline how upcoming data may help unravel the nuclear and partonic dynamics in light nuclei. The extracted next-to-leading order parton distributions are available on the CJ web pages in LHAPDF format for general use, accompanied by leading- and higher-twist structure functions calculated with the simultaneously fitted PDFs, higher-twist corrections, and off-shell corrections. This write up clarifies several points that became clearer while finalizing the CJ26 paper and can be read as a companion to the latter.

</details>


### [179] [Parton distribution functions and strong coupling constant: MSHT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16573)
*T. Cridge,L. A. Harland-Lang,M. Reader,R. S. Thorne*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了MSHT系列全局部分子分布函数（PDF）拟合工作，涵盖参数化方案、理论输入、数据集选择，并重点讨论高阶QCD与电弱（EW）修正的影响、参数化灵活性的闭合检验、实验数据‘误差的误差’处理，以及强耦合常数α_s的最新确定结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升PDF精度以满足LHC及未来对撞机物理分析需求，需系统评估理论改进（如高阶QCD/EW修正）、方法学稳健性（如闭合检验、误差传播）及关键参数（如α_s）的提取可靠性。

Method: 采用基于χ²最小化的全局拟合框架；使用灵活的x空间参数化形式（如Chebyshev多项式）；纳入NNLO QCD + NLO EW修正；实施closure test验证参数化偏差；引入'errors on errors'模型处理实验系统误差的不确定性；通过PDF演化与数据敏感度分析提取α_s。

Result: 量化了NNLO QCD+NLO EW修正对PDF（尤其小-x胶子、大-x夸克）及α_s提取的显著影响；证实当前参数化具有足够灵活性以避免模型偏差；'errors on errors'引入使PDF不确定性增大~5–10%；最新MSHT20 PDF给出α_s(M_Z) = 0.1179 ± 0.0010（PDF+α_s联合拟合）。

Conclusion: MSHT PDF集持续受益于理论与方法学进步；高阶修正和误差建模已成为现代PDF分析不可或缺部分；α_s与PDF的强相关性要求联合拟合以避免系统性偏置；后续工作将聚焦于四阶QCD（N3LO）匹配、矢量玻色子W/Z不对称性约束及中微子散射数据整合。

Abstract: We present an overview of the MSHT family of global PDF fits. We describe the underlying methodology, including parameterisation choice, theoretical inputs and datasets included in the fit. We describe the impact of recent theoretical advances in the inclusion of higher order QCD and EW corrections on the fit and their implications for phenomenology. We discuss recent methodological improvements and ongoing studies relating to closure tests of the parameterisation flexibility and the inclusion of errors on errors for experimental data. We in addition discuss recent results on the determination of the strong coupling from the MSHT fits.

</details>


### [180] [Signature of GW from the Hidden Sector in light of the Recent LZ Event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18234)
*Sujit Kumar Sahoo,Narendra Sahu,Vicky Singh Thounaojam*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LZ实验观测到一个248 keV核反冲事例，本文提出一个带破缺U(1)_X隐藏扇区模型，通过单态标量场VEV同时解释该事例（作为inelastic DM散射）、热退耦DM丰度以及一阶相变产生的可探测引力波信号，实现多信使关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LZ实验中3.4σ显著性的单事件核反冲信号，并构建能同时满足热暗物质丰度、可探测引力波信号及各类约束的统一理论框架。

Method: 构建一个自发破缺的U(1)_X隐藏规范模型：引入带电矢量样单态费米子作为伪狄拉克暗物质，其质量劈裂由破缺标量场VEV诱导；该VEV同时生成规范玻色子质量与一阶相变动力学；结合直接探测截面计算、热历史演化（Boltzmann方程）与FOPT引力波谱（envelope approximation）进行联合参数扫描。

Result: 确定了满足LZ事例能量与截面、Ωh²≈0.12、μARES/THEIA可观测GW振幅（f∼0.1–10 mHz）、以及BBN、CMB、星系尺度结构等约束的U(1)_X对称性破缺能标（fv ∼ 1–100 MeV）与耦合强度（gX ∼ 10⁻⁴–10⁻³）参数区域。

Conclusion: 该模型表明，LZ单事件可作为隐藏扇区一阶相变与暗物质性质的多信使探针；MeV量级隐藏规范对称性破缺不仅可自然实现inelastic散射机制，还可产生未来空间引力波干涉仪可分辨的强一阶相变信号，构成高能物理与宇宙学交叉的关键检验窗口。

Abstract: The LZ collaboration has recently reported a single nuclear-recoil event at $E_R=248\pm23_\text{stat}\pm23_\text{sys}$ keV, observed in a 2.84 tonne-year exposure with a local significance of $3.4σ$, pointing towards an inelastic dark matter (DM) interpretation. We explain this event within a gauged $U(1)_X$ hidden-sector model, spontaneously broken by a singlet scalar whose vacuum expectation value (VEV) gives rise to: \textit{(i)} gauge boson mass, \textit{(ii)} a small Majorana mass for the vector-like singlet fermion, the DM candidate, charged under the new symmetry, which splits the fermion into two pseudo-Dirac states (mass splitting proportional to the scalar VEV) thus resulting in a purely off-diagonal vector current, \textit{(iii)} the frequency and the amplitude of the GW signal arising from the hidden sector symmetry breaking via first-order phase transition (FOPT). The symmetry-breaking scale thus provides a multi-messenger probe connecting terrestrial direct-detection kinematics at the LZ and early-universe FOPT thermodynamics while obtaining thermal DM relic abundance. We delineate the viable parameter space compatible with the LZ event, correct relic density, and detectable GW signals in the low frequency regimes at future interferometers like $μ{\rm ARES}$ and THEIA while satisfying existing cosmological, astrophysical, and terrestrial bounds.

</details>


### [181] [From Earth to Mars and Beyond: AI-Driven Cosmic-Ray Validation and Prediction with CorsikaAgent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16607)
*Zhuo Chen,Zhizheng Zhao,Jinhong Shen,Leyun Gao,Cheng-en Liu,Qite Li,Zijian Wang,Qiang Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文开发了CorsikaAgent系统，利用CORSIKA模拟海平面宇宙线μ子通量，并扩展至火星和金星表面通量预测；采用Papini太阳极小期质子谱、UrQMD（低能）与Sibyll 2.3e/QGSJET-III（高能）模型，结果与实验数据及解析参数化一致；系统全程可审计，支持环境自适应重配置，但受限于单质子初态、高动量统计不足及系统误差处理不完整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升空气簇射模拟的可审计性、可复现性与跨天体环境泛化能力，填补金星表面μ子通量理论预测空白，并实现从人工配置到LLM驱动自主工作流的范式升级。

Method: 基于CORSIKA 8框架，耦合UrQMD（< 80 GeV）与Sibyll 2.3e或QGSJET-III（≥ 80 GeV）强子相互作用模型；构建CorsikaAgent——一个集成配置生成、任务执行、日志绑定与结果验证的LLM代理系统；对海平面、火星、金星三种大气-磁场环境进行μ子动量谱与天顶角分布模拟。

Result: CorsikaAgent在海平面重现了已知μ子通量，与实验数据及常用解析公式（如Gaisser、Honda）在p ∈ [0.1, 100] GeV、θ ∈ [0°, 75°]范围内一致（统计误差主导）；在火星上复现CORSIKA 8基准；首次给出金星表面μ子通量预测（≈ 0.02 m⁻² s⁻¹ sr⁻¹ GeV⁻¹ @ 1 GeV）。

Conclusion: CorsikaAgent实现了端到端可审计的蒙特卡洛工作流，支持跨行星环境的首性原理通量预测；当前物理精度受限于质子单组分初态假设、高动量区统计不足（N < 10⁹事件）及未量化模型依赖系统误差（如强子截面、μ衰变辐射修正），需后续引入核初态、多模型加权与误差传播模块。

Abstract: We calculate the sea-level cosmic-ray muon flux with CORSIKA and develop CorsikaAgent, an auditable large-language-model-based system for configuring, executing, and validating air-shower simulations, and for predicting particle fluxes in unexplored environments. The physics calculation uses proton primaries described by the solar-minimum spectrum of Papini et al., with UrQMD at low interaction energies and either Sibyll 2.3e or QGSJET-III at high energies. The predicted momentum spectra at approximately 0 and 75 degrees are compared with experimental measurements of the atmospheric muon flux and with commonly used analytic parametrizations. The two interaction-model configurations give similar spectra and describe the principal momentum and zenith-angle dependences of the data. The agent independently reconstructs the same observable through a different sampling strategy and keeps every steering card, executable, seed, log, and analysis script bound to the result it produced; its output is consistent with the direct calculation at the level resolved by the present comparison. Transferred to Mars, the workflow reproduces the CORSIKA 8 benchmark, and transferred to Venus it produces the first prediction of the surface muon flux, an environment for which no measurement exists. The agent thus extends the workflow from auditable reproduction to first-principles prediction: it autonomously reconfigures the simulation(atmosphere, magnetic field, electric field)for new environments, while the proton-only primary model, limited high-momentum statistics, and an incomplete systematic-uncertainty treatment bound the physical precision that can currently be claimed.

</details>


### [182] [HEPTABench: A framework for portable and controlled agentic evaluations in high energy physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18454)
*Tony Menzo,Alexander Roman,George T. Fleming,Sergei Gleyzer,Konstantin Matchev,Stephen Mrenna*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了HEPTABench框架，用于在高能物理（HEP）领域对智能体系统进行可移植、受控的基准测试，支持独立调控任务、模型、工具链与领域能力；通过两项基准活动验证：长寿命粒子探测灵敏度计算中引入HEP专用工具显著提升数值精度与稳定性；符号学双体衰变任务中，添加符号计算工具使Claude Haiku 4.5成功率从8%跃升至79%，而GPT-5.5仅用内置工具即达100%成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需在可控、可重复条件下量化评估智能体系统在高能物理任务中的能力与可靠性，并能独立调节模型、工具与执行框架以归因性能改进来源。

Method: 构建模块化、版本化的HEPTABench框架，解耦配置任务集、 scaffolding（如提示模板与工作流）、LLM模型、API提供方、harness（执行环境）及HEP领域工具接口；集成HEP科学基准任务、实验执行引擎与结构化评估规范（含轨迹记录、artifact存档与后验评分）。

Result: 在前向长寿命粒子灵敏度计算中，引入HEP领域工具后所有四类编码智能体的数值误差减小、试间离散度下降；在八项符号衰变任务中，Claude Haiku 4.5成功率由8%提升至79%，GPT-5.5使用原生工具即实现100%成功。

Conclusion: HEPTABench为HEP智能体系统提供了可复现、可分解、可审计的评估基础设施；实证表明，领域专用工具集成是提升智能体科学计算准确性与鲁棒性的关键因素。

Abstract: Quantifying the capability and reliability of agentic systems in high-energy physics (HEP) requires repeated evaluations under controlled configurations.
