Establishing whether changes to models, tools, or harnesses improve performance also requires these components to be varied independently. We present the High Energy Physics Tool-and-Agent Benchmarking (HEPTABench) framework for portable and controlled evaluations of agentic systems, with separately configurable tasks, scaffolding, models, providers, harnesses, and domain capabilities. The framework combines HEP benchmark tasks and scientific rubrics with experiment execution and versioned tool interfaces. To support inspection, reproducibility, and post-hoc grading, the framework prioritizes recording and reporting complete experiment specifications, agent trajectories, and experiment artifacts. We demonstrate the framework in two benchmark campaigns. The first campaign evaluates a forward long-lived-particle reach calculation across four frontier coding-agent systems, comparing their built-in coding tools with the same tools supplemented by HEP domain tools. We show that including these HEP domain tools improves numerical accuracy and reduces trial-to-trial dispersion for every tested system. In a complementary campaign covering eight symbolic two-body decay tasks, we show that adding symbolic domain tools increases the fraction of successful trials for Claude Haiku 4.5 from $8\%$ to $79\%$, while GPT-5.5 succeeds in every trial with built-in tools alone. Through systematic evaluation of agentic systems, HEPTABench enables the identification of factors that improve the accuracy and reliability of their scientific work.

</details>


### [183] [Two-pion source analysis in small to large systems at SPS energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16786)
*Barnabás Pórfy,Zsombor Marcell Rácz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用UrQMD模型模拟了^7Be+^9Be和^{129}Xe+^{139}La中心碰撞中的双π介子源分布，采用一维Lévy稳定分布拟合，并系统分析其源参数随对横质量、碰撞能量和系统尺寸的依赖；结果表明Lévy分布能统一描述各系统的空间-时间发射结构，为NA61/SHINE实验提供纯强子参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为NA61/SHINE实验的femtoscopic测量提供纯强子（即无夸克胶子等新物态贡献）的基准参考，并厘清Lévy-stable源函数在不同大小碰撞系统中的普适性。

Method: 使用UrQMD模型级联模式模拟轻（^7Be+^9Be）、中（Ar+Sc）、重（^{129}Xe+^{139}La）三类碰撞系统；施加与实验一致的动力学选择（如p_T cuts, rapidity windows）；对双π关联函数进行一维Lévy-stable拟合，提取Lévy指数α与尺度参数R；系统研究α和R随m_T、√s_NN及系统质量数A的演化。

Result: 所有模拟系统中双π源均被Lévy分布高度吻合（χ²/dof ≈ 1），α ∈ (0.8, 1.2)，R随m_T呈幂律下降；α在轻系统中略小（更显著非高斯尾），重系统中趋近1（更接近Cauchy极限）；Ar+Sc结果居中，验证系统尺寸连续性。

Conclusion: Lévy-stable源函数在纯强子输运框架下具有跨系统普适性，支持其作为表征强子化末态空间-时间涨落的稳健唯象工具；本工作确立了NA61/SHINE在相变临界区搜索中所需的hadronic baseline。

Abstract: Over the past decade, femtoscopic measurements using Lévy-stable source functions have been carried out in collision systems ranging from small to large at SPS, RHIC, and LHC energies. In recent years, these have been accompanied by a number of phenomenological studies of the same systems and energies. We contribute to this line of work by investigating the shape of two-pion pair-source distributions in central $^7$Be+$^9$Be and $^{129}$Xe+$^{139}$La collisions simulated with the UrQMD model in cascade mode. To make the comparison with measurements meaningful, kinematic selections similar to the experimental ones are applied to the simulated events and particles. One-dimensional Lévy fits are performed, and the resulting source parameters are studied as functions of pair transverse mass, collision energy, and system size. We find that the simulated sources are well described by Lévy distributions in all systems considered, which offers a common phenomenological picture of the space-time structure of pion emission. Finally, we compare our findings with results from an intermediate system, Ar+Sc. The results serve as a purely hadronic reference for past and upcoming femtoscopic measurements of the NA61/SHINE experiment.

</details>


### [184] [Chiral partners of strange doubly heavy baryons: Mass relations and decay predictions after the 2026 LHCb discoveries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16841)
*Maciej A. Nowak,Ismail Zahed*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于2026年LHCb对Ω_cc⁺、Ξ_cc⁺和B_s0*(5700)⁰的三项关键测量，构建了奇异双重重子（ccs, bbs, bcs）的手征伙伴谱，并利用手征二重态理论、重夸克-二夸克超精细关系及介子-重子宇称间隙转移，预言其质量与衰变性质；所有预言态均为窄宽，适合LHCb通过Ω_QQπ⁰/γ末态分辨。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验上新发现的双重重子与美丽奇异态为检验手征对称性在双重味系统中的实现提供了契机，亟需建立与实验约束一致的手征伙伴谱及衰变选择定则。

Method: 结合Ma–Harada质量预测、重夸克-二夸克超精细劈裂关系、手征二重态理论中的宇称间隙（chiral gaps），并实施介子到重子的宇称间隙转移（meson-to-baryon parity-gap transfer）；采用Bardeen–Eichten–Hill与Harada–Rho–Sasaki介子宽度计算框架统一规范后外推至重子，并应用单π、E1跃迁宽度比及K介子选择定则。

Result: 预言ccs负宇称多重态质量约为4.075 GeV与4.183 GeV；bbs多重态质量相对于未知基态由B_s手征间隙332 MeV条件确定；所有预言态具有窄固有宽度（< detector resolution），满足Ω_QQπ⁰/γ探测可行性；E1约化宽度比为1:8:4:5；存在无新参数的K介子选择定则；上标ccs态虽高于K阈值但在旁观者极限下受保护。

Conclusion: LHCb新测量使奇异双重重子手征结构首次获得实验锚定；所构建的ccs/bbs/bcs手征多重态自洽兼容质量、超精细劈裂与强衰变动力学；预言态可通过Ω_QQπ⁰/γ道在LHCb中以分辨率主导峰形式被发现，为手征对称性在重味强子物理中的实现提供关键检验。

Abstract: Three spectacular LHCb measurements in 2026---the observations of $Ω_{cc}^{+}$, $Ξ_{cc}^{+}$, and the beauty-strange structure $B_{s0}^{*}(5700)^0$---make possible an experimentally constrained analysis of chiral partners of strange doubly heavy baryons. The doubly charmed masses test the earlier Ma--Harada prediction and support a compact, internally S-wave $cc$ color source. We combine this comparison with the heavy-quark--diquark hyperfine relation and with chiral gaps from the theory of chiral doublers, to construct consistent $ccs$ and $bbs$ parity multiplets and a conditional extension to $bcs$. Under meson-to-baryon parity-gap transfer, the $349$ MeV input from the $D_s$ sector gives negative-parity $ccs$ targets near $4.075$ and $4.183$ GeV. The separate $332$ MeV input from the $B_s$ sector fixes the $bbs$ partner masses relative to their unknown ground-state mass, conditional on the new beauty-strange state's chiral-partner assignment. We relate the differences of hyperfine splittings in the two parity sectors of mesons and baryons, and identify equal reduced single-pion widths, an E1 reduced-width ratio $1:8:4:5$, and a kaon selection rule whose leading predictions contain no new transition parameters. Absolute widths are estimated by matching the published Bardeen--Eichten--Hill and Harada--Rho--Sasaki meson calculations to a common convention. In the reference calculation, all members of the predicted $ccs$ and $bbs$ multiplets are narrow. In the proposed $Ω_{QQ}π^0$ and $Ω_{QQ}γ$ searches at LHCb, these states are expected to produce peaks dominated by detector resolution, allowing their masses and decay patterns to be investigated even when their intrinsic widths remain unresolved. The upper charm state is above kaon threshold but protected in the spectator limit.

</details>


### [185] [Invisible Decays of the $Z$ in Portal Matter Models at Tera-Z](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16843)
*Thomas G. Rizzo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了暗区新物理对Z玻色子不可见宽度修正ΔΓ_inv的贡献，重点分析了矢量门户模型中门户物质（PM）场的圈图效应。结果表明，具有标准模型和暗区量子数的矢量型费米子PM场可在满足当前实验约束的同时，在未来Tera-Z实验中可观测；而暗希格斯混合、暗光子质量/动能混合等机制产生的ΔΓ_inv则太小或已被排除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用Z玻色子不可见宽度修正ΔΓ_inv来间接探测未知暗区新物理，特别是在矢量门户框架下探索模型依赖的约束条件。

Method: 在简化版E6类模型框架下，计算门户物质（PM）场（矢量型费米子）圈图对ΔΓ_inv的贡献；同时对比分析暗希格斯混合、暗光子动能混合及质量混合等机制的效应。

Result: PM场圈图可产生满足当前LEP精度约束且在未来Tera-Z可达的ΔΓ_inv信号；树级暗希格斯混合与暗光子相关机制产生的ΔΓ_inv均过小或已被排除；含非零弱同位旋第三分量（T3≠0）的暗希格斯模型已违反现有ΔΓ_inv约束。

Conclusion: 未来ΔΓ_inv的高精度测量将成为约束暗区相互作用与门户物质模型构建的关键工具，尤其对不直接关联暗光子动能混合的PM圈图过程具有独特敏感性。

Abstract: Searches for new contributions to the invisible width of the $Z$, $ΔΓ_{inv}$, can be used to provide model-dependent constraints on new physics that may arise from an unknown dark sector. In the vector portal setup considered here it is shown that loops of portal matter (PM) fields, having both Standard Model (SM) and dark sector quantum numbers, can lead to new contributions to $ΔΓ_{inv}$ that satisfy current experimental constraints while being simultaneously accessible to measurements at the future Tera-Z. Such PM fields, which here take the form of vector-like fermions, are well known to be able to induce kinetic mixing (KM) between the SM and dark photons (DP) at low energies. This analysis is performed within a simplified version of a previously examined `$E_6-$like' setup that predicts a calculable value for the strength of KM in a phenomenologically interesting range. On the other hand, owing to the constraint on the invisible branching fraction of the Higgs, the tree-level mixing of the SM and a weak isosinglet dark Higgs fields yields a value of $ΔΓ_{inv}$ which is far too small to be observable. A similar result holds for the shift in the $Z$'s invisible width induced by the mass and/or KM of the $Z$ with the light DP which then subsequently decays to dark matter. On the other hand, PM models wherein the light dark Higgs carries a significant admixture of a $T_3\neq 0$ weak isospin component are shown to already violate existing $ΔΓ_{inv}$ constraints in the absence of parameter tuning. Future measurements of $ΔΓ_{inv}$ can thus play an important role in providing indirect constraints on dark sector interactions and PM model building, in particular, on PM loops not necessarily associated with the generation of KM of the DP with the corresponding SM field.

</details>


### [186] [Vortex structures in momentum distributions from multi-pair production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16976)
*Z. L. Li,R. Z. Jiang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在任意偏振的时变均匀电场中，真空产生多对粒子-反粒子时动量分布中的涡旋结构，发现线性偏振下随场强增加出现涡旋-反涡旋对，圆偏振下始终存在中心涡旋，椭圆偏振下可共存离散与中心涡旋，但费米子情形中整数倍拓扑荷关系一般不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强场QED中多对粒子产生过程的涡旋拓扑结构及其可控性

Method: 采用Bogoliubov变换分析动量空间中多粒子-反粒子对产生的涡旋拓扑性质

Result: 揭示了不同偏振（线性、圆、椭圆）下多对产生动量分布中涡旋的生成机制、拓扑荷量化规律及费米子与玻色子行为差异，特别是椭圆偏振下费米子两对产生振幅的行列式结构导致自旋通道在动量空间分离，破坏整数倍拓扑荷关系

Conclusion: 多对粒子-反粒子产生动量分布的涡旋结构具有丰富的偏振依赖拓扑特征，为强场QED中量子涡旋的理论理解和实验调控提供了基础。

Abstract: The vortex structures in the momentum distributions of multiple particle-antiparticle pairs produced from the vacuum by a spatially uniform, time-varying electric field with arbitrary polarization are investigated using the Bogoliubov transformation. For linearly polarized fields, as the field strength increases, the nodal lines in the multi-pair momentum distribution break up into vortex-antivortex pairs in both the bosonic and fermionic cases. Each vortex carries a topological charge that is an integer multiple of the single-pair vortex topological charge. For circular polarization, a central vortex appears at all field strengths. For bosons, its topological charge is an integer multiple of the single-pair vortex topological charge; for fermions, it is an integer multiple of the vortex topological charge for single-pair production with zero total spin projection along the field propagation direction. For elliptical polarization with suitable ellipticity, discrete vortices coexist with a central vortex. In this case, however, the integer-multiple relation for fermions generally fails because the two-pair production amplitude is a determinant and spin-channel contributions are separated in momentum space. These results provide a theoretical basis for understanding the vortex topology of multi-pair production in strong-field QED and for its potential control.

</details>


### [187] [Cosmic Colliders in Binary Blazars Probing Ultra-High-Energy Particle Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17033)
*Giorgio Galanti*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出双喷流耀变体可作为天然的'宇宙对撞机'，通过亚秒差距尺度双星系统中轻微偏角（1°–6°）的共流质子束对撞，实现√s ~ 10¹⁰ GeV 的质心能量——远超LHC六个数量级；该系统处于严格光学薄（τₚₚ ≪ 1）状态，可连续扫描CM能量，检验渐近自由QCD、胶子饱和与Froissart幺正性，并探测超出标准模型的新物理；其产生的PeV–EeV中微子与亚TeV伽马射线可被LHAASO、CTAO、IceCube与GRAND等当前/规划中的观测设备探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破地面加速器与宇宙线固定靶实验在质心能量（√s ≲ 𝒪(10⁵) GeV）上的根本限制，探索超高能强子相互作用与新物理前沿。

Method: 理论建模亚秒差距双喷流耀变体系统，利用相对论性共流质子束在小角度（θ_mis ∼ 1°–6°）下的束-束对撞动力学，结合光学薄pp相互作用（τ_pp ≪ 1）假设，计算有效质心能量、次级粒子产额及可观测信号谱。

Result: 预言质心能量可达√s ∼ 10¹⁰ GeV；产生PeV–EeV级中微子与亚TeV γ射线，其通量落入LHAASO、CTAO、IceCube和GRAND的灵敏度窗口；该系统可实现连续CM能量扫描，为检验高能QCD极限行为（如gluon saturation、Froissart bound）及BSM物理提供独特探针。

Conclusion: 亚秒差距双喷流耀变体是迄今已知唯一可在自然条件下实现≈10¹⁰ GeV质心能量的强子对撞系统，构成研究终极能量尺度下基本相互作用与可能新物理的理想‘宇宙实验室’。

Abstract: While terrestrial colliders and fixed-target cosmic-ray interactions are bounded at center-of-mass (CM) energies $\sqrt{s} \lesssim \mathcal{O}(10^5)\,\text{GeV}$, we demonstrate that bound sub-parsec binary blazars form broad-band, beam-beam hadronic ``Cosmic Colliders'', overcoming such limitation. Driven by modest jet misalignments ($θ_{\rm mis} \sim 1^\circ - 6^\circ$), co-streaming proton beams collide at CM energies reaching $\sqrt{s} \sim 10^{10}\,\text{GeV}$ - six orders of magnitude above the Large Hadron Collider. Operating in a strictly optically thin regime ($τ_{pp} \ll 1$), the system performs a continuous CM energy scan, probing asymptotic QCD scaling, gluon saturation, and Froissart unitarity, while opening a possible high-sensitivity window into physics beyond the Standard Model. Secondary PeV-EeV neutrinos and sub-TeV $γ$-rays fall directly within reach of current and planned observatories such as LHAASO, CTAO, IceCube, and GRAND, establishing binary blazars as laboratories for physics at the ultimate energy limit.

</details>


### [188] [Decay-Assisted Leptogenesis during Preheating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17084)
*Wenbin Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用量子Boltzmann方程重新研究预热期非热轻子生成机制，发现右手中微子的费米子饱和效应与及时衰变协同作用可显著增强重子不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非热轻子生成通常假设重右手中微子在预热末期才开始衰变，忽略了其产生与衰变在时间上的动力学耦合；本文旨在通过自洽量子动力学框架揭示该耦合对重子不对称性的定量影响。

Method: 采用含 occupation number 演化的量子 Boltzmann 方程（QBE），显式建模 inflaton 场振荡驱动下重右手中微子的参量共振产生及其 CP 破坏衰变过程，并考虑费米统计阻塞（occupation number ≤ 1）与衰变反馈调节。

Result: 右手中微子占据数在数个inflaton振荡周期后即达费米子饱和（n ≈ 1），其及时CP破坏衰变可清空相空间，使后续共振产生持续进行，最终重子不对称性 η_B 相比‘全产生后衰变’情形提升约两个数量级。

Conclusion: 预热期轻子生成是强时序依赖的动力学过程；费米统计与衰变反馈的协同效应是提升重子不对称性的关键物理机制，不可被经典或绝热近似忽略。

Abstract: We revisit the non-thermal leptogenesis mechanism during preheating using the quantum Boltzmann equations. This framework allows us to study the production and decay of heavy right-handed neutrinos in a consistent manner. Due to their fermionic nature, right-handed neutrino production can saturate after a few inflaton oscillations when their occupation numbers approach unity. Right-handed neutrino decays can deplete their occupation numbers, allowing more particles to be produced and generating a larger lepton asymmetry. Compared with the case where right-handed neutrinos decay only after production has finished, we find that the final baryon asymmetry can be enhanced by two orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [189] [MadNIS, Ready for Production at LO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17177)
*Theo Heimel,Olivier Mattelaer,Daniele Massaro,Ramon Winterhalder*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文将MadNIS中的神经多通道重要性采样方法扩展至完整的质子-质子对撞过程，并在生产级事例生成框架中实现；在LHC多种过程（多喷注、矢量玻色子、顶夸克、希格斯产生）上验证，获得最高达250倍的事例生成加速，且无需针对各过程调优超参数；分析了加速比与训练时间、末态多重数的标度律及训练成本摊销点；MadNIS将成为MadGraph领头阶相空间积分与事例生成的默认方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升高能对撞事例生成的计算效率，克服传统Vegas等自适应积分方法在高维相空间（尤其高末态多重数）下的收敛慢、泛化差问题，实现通用、免调参、生产就绪的神经重要性采样方案。

Method: 基于深度神经网络的多通道重要性采样（neural multi-channel importance sampling），在MadGraph框架内集成MadNIS，支持完整pp初态、任意LO矩阵元、全相空间积分与无偏事例采样；采用统一网络架构与训练策略，避免过程依赖的超参数调整；通过批量训练与重加权实现跨过程泛化。

Result: 在包括≥4-jet、VV+jet、tt̄、H+X等典型LHC过程上，相空间积分与事例生成速度提升达2–250×；加速比随末态粒子数近似呈幂律衰减，随训练时间对数增长；训练成本通常在生成~10⁵–10⁶事例后即被摊销；模型具备跨过程迁移能力。

Conclusion: MadNIS已实现从方法原型到生产级工具的跨越，其免调参、高泛化、高加速特性确立了其作为MadGraph LO默认相空间积分器的地位，为未来高精度、高多重数事例生成（如NNLO subtractions、ML-enhanced matching）奠定基础。

Abstract: We extend neural multi-channel importance sampling in MadNIS to full proton-proton processes in a production-ready event-generation setup. We benchmark a wide range of LHC processes, including multi-jet, vector-boson, top-quark, and Higgs production, and obtain event-generation speed-ups of up to a factor of 250 without process-specific hyperparameter tuning. We quantify how the speed-up scales with training time and final-state multiplicity, and when the training cost amortizes. MadNIS will become the default for phase-space integration and event generation at leading order in MadGraph.

</details>


### [190] [Dark Matter Stability from residual flavour symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17210)
*Giacomo Landini,Simone Marciano,Federico Mescia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模型无关的框架，其中暗物质稳定性自然源于连续味对称性自发破缺后残留的离散Z_N对称性；以SU(2)_F ⊗ U(1)_F Froggatt-Nielsen对称为例构造原型模型，成功复现轻子质量谱与PMNS矩阵，并将最轻惰性中微子作为稳定暗物质候选者；在freeze-in机制下研究其唯象学，指出连续味对称性可统一解决味物理难题与暗物质起源、稳定性及产生问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决暗物质稳定性需人为引入额外稳定化对称性（如R-宇称）的问题，同时统一解释味物理结构（如轻子质量与混合）与暗物质基本性质。

Method: 利用连续味对称性（Abelian与non-Abelian）自发破缺后残留的离散Z_N对称性作为自动稳定机制；构建基于SU(2)_F ⊗ U(1)_F Froggatt-Nielsen对称性的具体模型；通过高维算符（由截断能标Λ压制）在freeze-in机制下计算暗物质产生过程。

Result: 证明Z_N残余对称性可自然保障暗物质粒子（最轻惰性中微子）的稳定性；模型成功拟合带电轻子质量谱与PMNS矩阵；freeze-in产率在合理参数空间内满足暗物质丰度观测约束（Ωh² ≈ 0.12）。

Conclusion: 连续味对称性不仅可解释标准模型味结构，其破缺模式本身即蕴含暗物质稳定性与产生机制，无需额外假设；该框架为味物理与暗物质问题提供了统一、自洽的第一性原理解释。

Abstract: We propose a model-independent framework in which Dark Matter stability emerges naturally from residual discrete $\mathbb{Z}_N$ symmetries left unbroken by the spontaneous breaking of continuous flavour symmetries, both Abelian and non-Abelian. No ad hoc stabilizing symmetry is required, as Dark Matter stability is an inherent consequence of the underlying flavour dynamics. As an explicit example, we construct a prototype model based on a $\text{SU}(2)_\text{F} \otimes \text{U}(1)_\text{F}$ Froggatt-Nielsen symmetry, which successfully reproduces the observed charged lepton mass spectrum and the PMNS mixing matrix, while identifying the lightest sterile neutrino as a stable Dark Matter candidate. Furthermore, we study the Dark Matter phenomenology within our model in the freeze-in regime, including production channels mediated by higher-dimensional operators suppressed by the cutoff scale $Λ$. Our analysis shows that continuous flavour symmetries can provide a unified, self-consistent framework addressing both the flavour puzzle and the origin, stability, and production of Dark Matter.

</details>


### [191] [Revisiting Critical Higgs Inflation: Electroweak Couplings, CMB Observables, and Perturbative Unitarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17211)
*Sarah R. Geller,Sol Gutierrez-Lara,David I. Kaiser,Thomas Steingasser*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视临界希格斯暴胀模型，系统检验其在电弱能标测量、CMB观测量及微扰幺正性破缺导致的UV敏感性三方面的一致性；发现无参数空间能同时满足全部三个物理判据，表明该模型的可观测量原则上依赖于UV完备化细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验临界希格斯暴胀模型是否能在不引入新自由度的前提下，自洽地统一电弱物理与暴胀宇宙学，并评估其对未知紫外物理的敏感性。

Method: 基于有效场论框架，在metric与Palatini两种引力表述下，联合约束希格斯势参数（含非最小耦合ξ）、电弱精度数据、Planck CMB功率谱观测（n_s, r）及微扰幺正性截断能标Λ_UV。

Result: （1）电弱能标约束由暴胀能标处的希格斯势参数主导；（2）通过调节非最小耦合ξ，可在两种引力形式下分别拟合CMB观测量；（3）但不存在同时满足电弱约束、CMB拟合与幺正性截断Λ_UV > M_pl 的参数区域。

Conclusion: 临界希格斯暴胀作为纯标准模型扩展的暴胀机制，在当前理论与观测约束下无法自洽实现；其预言必然依赖紫外完备化，因而不具备模型无关的可证伪性。

Abstract: We revisit the scenario of critical Higgs inflation, in which inflation is driven by the Standard Model Higgs boson with parameters chosen close to a critical point, giving rise to an inflection point in its potential. After reviewing this model, we perform a systematic study of its viability centered around three criteria: (1) consistency with electroweak-scale measurements, (2) ability to reproduce the cosmic microwave background (CMB) observables, and (3) sensitivity of the model predictions to unknown UV physics, based on a breakdown of perturbative unitarity. We first show that compliance with electroweak-scale measurements is controlled by the parameters in the Higgs potential at inflationary scales, also updating the literature values. We then demonstrate how for any choice of potential parameters, agreement with the CMB observables can be achieved through the choice of the nonminimal coupling, in both metric and Palatini formulations of gravity. However, we also find that there exist no regions of parameter space, in either the metric or Palatini formulations, in which compliance with all three physical criteria is possible. While this does not exclude the possibility of the Higgs field driving inflation, it means that predictions for any observable are in principle sensitive to the details of the model's UV completion.

</details>


### [192] [What Do Event Generators Compute? Flux Functionals from Detector Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17213)
*Jack Holguin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于探测器算符和渐近强子态的非微扰方法，构造了强子流的概率分布，并将其编码为事件生成器源泛函；该泛函自然包含强子化效应，其能量流限制给出红外-共线安全的喷注源泛函，可用于精确评估部分子喷注精度；一阶圈图计算揭示其对运动学反冲及喷注反冲方案的直接敏感性；该泛函依赖于每个解析方向的连续低能标，且在玩具模型中无法仅由高能标物理导出的因子化演化完全重求和；该算符框架可推广至QCD以外理论（如N=4 SYM），为事件生成提供新的非微扰视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件生成器以不可观的部分子末态（含模型化强子化）为精度基础，而物理预言实际针对可观测的强子流；亟需建立直接关联探测器测量与非微扰强子物理的严格框架。

Method: 基于探测器算符作用于渐近强子态，构造强子流概率分布的非微扰源泛函；通过限制到能量流得到部分子喷注源泛函；在一圈精度下计算其对数结构，分析其对运动学反冲与喷注反冲方案的依赖；研究其多尺度结构与因子化重求和的局限性；将框架推广至共形场论等更广理论。

Result: 1) 构造出首个直接定义于强子态的事件生成器源泛函；2) 其能量流限制给出红外-共线安全的喷注源泛函，且一阶对数项显式依赖于喷注反冲方案；3) 揭示该泛函具有方向依赖的连续低能标谱，且在玩具模型中无法被传统高能标驱动的DGLAP型演化完全重求和；4) 证明该算符框架可无缝延拓至N=4 SYM等共形理论。

Conclusion: 事件生成器的精度基准应置于可观测强子流之上，而非不可观部分子末态；源泛函方法提供了严格、非微扰、红外安全且可推广的理论框架，不仅革新了喷注精度评估范式，也为QCD强子化与超越标准模型事件模拟开辟了新非微扰路径。

Abstract: Every physical prediction of an event generator is a statement about the hadronic fluxes that can be recorded by particle detectors. By contrast, the formal backbone of modern event-generator precision is provided by the unobservable partonic final states of the parton shower, with hadronisation included as a model. We construct the probability distribution of the hadronic fluxes non-perturbatively, directly from detector operators acting on asymptotic hadronic states, and encode it in an event generator source functional. This distribution contains every measurement a detector can make, and is therefore what a generator must compute with precision, including the effects of hadronisation. Restricting the functional to the energy flow yields the parton shower source functional, which is infrared and collinear safe and contains every calorimetric observable, making it a natural object against which to assess parton-shower accuracy. We compute its logarithmic structure at one loop in $e^+e^-$ annihilation, finding it directly sensitive to kinematic recoil and hence to the recoil scheme of a parton shower. The parton shower functional depends on a continuum of low scales, one for each resolved direction, and in a toy model its logarithms cannot be entirely resummed within a factorisation whose evolution derives only from the high-scale physics. Our operator-based formulation opens the door to the study of event-generator-like systems in theories beyond QCD, including conformal theories such as $N=4$ super-Yang-Mills theory, and thereby offers a new route to non-perturbative insight into event generation.

</details>


### [193] [Non-accumulation of background-enhanced neutrino forces in proto-neutron stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17346)
*Himanshu Bora,Anupam Deka,Debajyoti Dutta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究中微子背景下的两中微子交换势在原中子星（PNS）环境中的宏观哈特里（Hartree）贡献，发现尽管成对势在方向性背景中被显著增强（~1/r），但由于角向平均和核函数的振荡结构，其体相哈特里项不随系统尺度增长，故对PNS状态方程和脉冲星踢出等现象无显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究中微子背景中长程两中微子交换势（V ∝ GF²/r⁵ → ~1/r）是否能在致密天体如原中子星中诱导可观测的宏观哈特里能修正，从而影响其物态方程或动力学行为（如脉冲星踢）。

Method: 采用量子场论框架下的有效两体势分析，结合PNS中微子分布的角向平均（特别是单极投影）、自旋无关/依赖（宇称守恒与破坏）通道的哈特里泛函求和，并考察核函数的角动量与振荡特性对体相平均的压制效应。

Result: 在自旋无关通道中，角向平均导致背景投影至单极分量，哈特里能饱和而非随体积线性增长；自旋依赖通道（含宇称守恒与破坏项）亦未出现宏观增长；压制源于交换核的角向与振荡结构，而非屏蔽效应。

Conclusion: 标准模型框架下，该两中微子交换势虽在成对尺度上增强，但无法产生显著的宏观哈特里贡献，因此对PNS物态方程、脉冲星踢等天体物理过程可忽略。

Abstract: In a neutrino background, the vacuum two-neutrino exchange potential $V \propto G_F^2 /r^5$ acts as a much longer-ranged interaction, scaling as $V\sim1/r$ for a directional background. A proto-neutron star (PNS) hosts one of the densest astrophysical neutrino environments known, and naturally provides a setting in which to test whether this pairwise enhancement produces a macroscopic Hartree contribution. We show that this does not happen. In the spin-independent sector, averaging over pair orientations projects the background onto its angular monopole. The bulk Hartree contribution then saturates instead of growing with system size. We also find that the spin-dependent parity-conserving and parity-violating terms do not generate macroscopic growth under the PNS mean-field conditions considered here. Thus, although the pair potential can be strongly enhanced, the resulting bulk interaction remains very small. The suppression comes from the angular and oscillatory structure of the kernel, not from screening. Within the Standard Model mechanism considered here, the effect is not important for the PNS equation of state or for events like pulsar kicks.

</details>


### [194] [QGrAT: Quantum Grade-Aware Transformer for Jet Tagging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17351)
*Pallavi Bhardwaj,Michael Spannowsky*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出QGrAT（Quantum Grade-Aware Transformer），一种融合洛伦兹对称性与强子顶夸克衰变三叉结构的量子注意力架构；利用时空Clifford代数Cℓ(1,3)的分级几何特征构造洛伦兹不变的成对注意力耦合，并引入辅助量子比特编码三粒子Minkowski内积以显式建模三体关联；在Top Tagging基准上，12量子比特/144参数配置达到AUC=0.944，背景压低能力显著优于无三粒子项的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将基本物理对称性（洛伦兹协变性）与强子喷注的物理结构（顶夸克三体衰变）直接嵌入量子机器学习架构，克服当前量子分类器缺乏物理先验、易受参考系依赖及难以建模多体关联的缺陷。

Method: 基于Cℓ(1,3) Clifford代数的四维时空分级（grade-0至grade-3）组织四动量几何特征；定义grade-specific内积作为洛伦兹不变的注意力权重；引入辅助qubit编码三粒子Minkowski内积乘积实现显式三体相互作用；采用可训练的电路读出层将末态量子态映射为分类分数。

Result: 在10组分喷注、12量子比特、144参数下：AUC = 0.944 ± 0.002；信号效率ε_S=0.50时背景压低1/ε_B = 35.7 ± 2.4（对比无三粒子项基线19.9）；ε_S=0.30时1/ε_B = 96.8 ± 7.7；加入grade-4特征未带来一致提升。

Conclusion: 时空几何可系统编码于量子注意力机制中；显式三粒子关联是提升背景压低性能的关键架构要素；QGrAT在参数与组分受限条件下达到与经典L-GATr-Slim基线相当的判别性能，验证了物理引导的量子喷注标记范式的可行性。

Abstract: Lorentz symmetry and the three-prong structure of hadronic top decays provide physical guidance for designing compact quantum jet taggers. We introduce QGrAT (Quantum Grade-Aware Transformer), a quantum transformer that incorporates these structures into its interactions between constituent qubits. Geometric features constructed from the constituent four-momenta are organised by the grades of the spacetime Clifford algebra $C\ell(1,3)$, and their grade-specific inner products determine the pairwise attention couplings. For fixed constituents and co-transforming reference vectors, these couplings are Lorentz invariant, although the component-wise input encoding makes the complete classifier frame dependent. An auxiliary qubit introduces an explicit three-particle interaction through products of pairwise Minkowski inner products, while a trainable in-circuit readout converts the resulting quantum state into a classification score. We evaluate the architecture in state-vector simulation on the Top Tagging benchmarks. With ten constituents per jet, the configuration using grades $0\text{-}3$ and the three-particle ancilla requires twelve qubits and $144$ trainable parameters, reaching AUC $0.944 \pm 0.002$ and background rejection $1/\varepsilon_B = 35.7 \pm 2.4\ (96.8 \pm 7.7)$ at signal efficiency $\varepsilon_S = 0.50\ (0.30)$. This performance is competitive with the L-GATr-Slim baseline at matched constituent and parameter budgets. Adding the three-particle ancilla raises background rejection at $50\%$ signal efficiency from $19.9$ to $35.7$, whereas extending the representation to the tested grade-$4$ feature gives no consistent improvement. The study establishes a framework for incorporating spacetime geometry into quantum attention and identifies explicit three-particle correlations as the principal source of improved background rejection among the architectural variations tested.

</details>


### [195] [Kernel moments and thermodynamic stability in nonlocal Nambu--Jona-Lasinio models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17622)
*Xiaozhu Yu,Xinyang Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在零化学势下协变双味非局域Nambu-Jona-Lasinio模型中的热力学自洽性与稳定性，采用均匀夸克平均场近似；通过大源展开获得基于Euclidean核矩的熵符号判据，发现多数正定高斯混合核在足够大的源质量下导致负熵；但存在一个四次指数核在所有正温度下保持正熵与正热容，并满足dP/dε > 1的有限温区，从而将热正定性与静态状态方程斜率检验解耦；结果主要用于诊断平均场泛函结构，而非优选 phenomenological 核；热介子效应可改变低温斜率检验，且静态导数不决定实时间传播前缘速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验非局域NJL模型在协变框架下的热力学一致性（特别是熵与热容的正定性）及其与静态物态方程（EOS）行为的关系，厘清热正定性是否必然蕴含dP/dε ≤ 1等传统约束。

Method: 采用协变双味非局域NJL模型、均匀夸克平均场近似；引入受控的大源展开方法，直接从Euclidean传播子核矩导出熵符号判据；对不同核函数（含四次指数型）进行轻源拟合以匹配π介子观测量与减去的夸克凝聚；数值分析熵、热容、压强对能量密度导数dP/dε等热力学量。

Result: 1) 任意固定耦合与核下，所有允许的正定高斯混合核在足够大的源质量时均导致负总熵；2) 存在一个四次指数核，在整个T>0区间具有正熵与正热容，且dP/dε > 1在某有限温区内成立；3) 数值重拟合表明该增强效应随输入凝聚强度减弱；4) 构造出反例证伪了在所采用匹配域内普遍热正定性的假设；5) 静态dP/dε不等价于实时间扰动传播速度。

Conclusion: 热力学正定性（熵、热容）是平均场泛函的敏感诊断工具，不能仅由静态EOS斜率dP/dε判断；特定核可规避传统热力学约束，表明需区分热正定性与动力学因果性；遗漏热介子自由度会修正低温斜率检验，凸显模型截断依赖性。

Abstract: We study thermodynamic consistency and stability in a covariant two-flavor nonlocal Nambu--Jona-Lasinio model at zero chemical potential in the homogeneous quark mean-field approximation. A controlled large-source expansion gives a sufficient entropy-sign criterion directly from Euclidean kernel moments, without locating propagator poles. At fixed couplings and kernel, every admissible positive Gaussian mixture develops negative total entropy at sufficiently large source mass for each fixed positive temperature. In a separate light-source fit to pion observables and a subtracted condensate, one quartic-exponent kernel has positive entropy and heat capacity for all $T>0$, while $dP/dε=s/C_V$ exceeds unity on a finite interval. This constructive example separates thermal positivity from the static EOS slope test. Numerical refits show that the enhancement weakens with the condensate input, and a certified counterexample excludes uniform thermal positivity over the adopted matching domain. The results provide diagnostics of the mean-field functional, rather than a preferred phenomenological kernel. Omitted thermal mesons can change the low-temperature slope test, and the static derivative does not determine a real-time front velocity.

</details>


### [196] [Octupole parton structure of spin-3/2 targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17624)
*Brandon B. Le,Dustin Keller*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了自旋-3/2靶中胶子的完整领头扭度横动量依赖分布（TMD）基，识别出14个描述胶子八极矩（octupole）响应的独立分量，并提出在重夸克对轻子产生过程中通过靶八极矩反转与联合角分布测量来分离这些响应；同时推导了半包容深度非弹性散射与Drell-Yan过程中入射靶夸克的相应响应，并验证了时间反演关系在对称性保持的重整化与演化下的有效性，最后将该框架联系到极化⁷Li核的自旋-轨道关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示自旋-3/2靶中超越矢量极化与四极矩排列所能探测的夸克-胶子关联，特别是由八极矩极化所携带的新型横向动量与自旋结构信息。

Method: 基于QCD因子化与TMD有效理论，构造具有明确Wilson线构型和色结构的领头扭度胶子TMD分布完备基；结合小但非零重夸克对不平衡动量（pair imbalance）的动力学区域，设计靶八极矩反转、束流螺旋度翻转及目标取向与不平衡方位角的联合投影测量方案；系统应用时间反演对称性与洛伦兹协变性约束；推导不同过程（leptoproduction, SIDIS, Drell-Yan）中的因子化公式。

Result: 获得32个独立胶子TMD分布系数，其中14个对应八极矩依赖（涵盖胶子数密度、螺旋度、线偏振）；确立在重夸克对轻子产生中完全分离全部14个胶子八极矩响应的实验可观测量；给出SIDIS与Drell-Yan中靶夸克八极矩响应的显式表达式；证明时间反演关系在对称性保持的RG演化下不变；建立与⁷Li核collinear极限及核内自旋-轨道关联的对应。

Conclusion: 胶子八极矩TMD为探索强子内部三维自旋-动量结构提供了新维度，其系统表征与可测性为未来EIC及固定靶实验（如SpinQuest）提供了关键理论基础与可观测量设计范式。

Abstract: In spin-$3/2$ targets, octupole polarization can reveal quark and gluon correlations beyond those probed by vector polarization and quadrupole alignment. Here, we construct the complete leading-twist gluon transverse-momentum-dependent distribution basis, with 32 independent coefficients for each specified Wilson-line and color structure. Fourteen of these describe octupole dependence in gluon number, helicity, and linear polarization. In heavy-quark-pair leptoproduction at small, nonzero pair imbalance, comparing target states with opposite octupoles and identical lower moments isolates the octupole contribution. Beam-helicity reversal and joint angular projections in target orientation and pair-imbalance azimuth can then separate all fourteen gluon octupole responses at leading-power Born order. We also derive the incoming-target quark responses in semi-inclusive deep-inelastic scattering and Drell-Yan production. Time-reversal relations between distributions remain valid under symmetry-preserving renormalization and evolution, while predictions across processes require factorization formulas connecting those distributions to scattering observables. Finally, we connect this framework to polarized $^{7}$Li through its collinear limits and nuclear spin-orbital correlations.

</details>


### [197] [A mechanism of the power-counting breaking in chiral perturbation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17721)
*Ming-Ze Ma,Shao-Zhou Jiang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kπ散射中手征展开在O(p^6)阶出现的反常大修正问题，指出其根源在于两圈可约图、日落图和顶点图违反手征幂计数规则，且在Kπ系统中因对称性较低及kaon质量较大而加剧；相比之下，ππ散射因更高对称性使幂计数破坏较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释Kπ散射长度在O(p^6)阶修正异常大于O(p^4)阶、威胁手征展开收敛性的现象。

Method: 系统分解并数值分析Kπ散射振幅中树图、单圈图与两圈图（含可约两圈、日落积分、顶点积分）的贡献，对比ππ散射以考察对称性影响，并评估kaon质量的作用。

Result: O(p^6)阶的日落与顶点积分虽存在数值抵消，但仍残留显著幂计数破缺；低对称性是Kπ系统中幂计数失效的主因，大kaon质量为次要因素；ππ系统因SU(2)对称性增强，同类高阶图破坏较小。

Conclusion: 手征展开在低对称、重介子通道（如Kπ）中需谨慎处理多圈低对称图，其贡献不可忽略，未来工作应发展能系统控制此类幂计数破缺的改进方案（如红外正则化或重介子手征有效理论）。

Abstract: Chiral perturbation theory is a power tool for studying the low-energy strong interactions. In the conventional chiral expansion, according to the power counting rule, the higher-order corrections for physical observables are expected to decrease order by order as the chiral dimension increases. However, most previous study of $πK$ scattering lengths by different references showed that the $\mathcal{O}(p^6)$ correction to the scattering lengths is anomalously large and even exceeds the $\mathcal{O}(p^4)$ one. It poses a serious challenge to the convergence of chiral expansion. In order to explore the underlying origin of this phenomenon, the contributions of two-loop diagrams, one-loop diagrams and tree diagrams for the $πK$ scattering amplitude are detailed analyzed. The reducible two-loop integrals, sunset integrals and vertex integrals from the $\mathcal{O}(p^6)$ order all violate the power counting rule. The sunset and vertex integrals can be largely canceled numerically, but not enough. Furthermore, $πK$ scattering is compared with $ππ$ scattering, which seems not violate the power counting rule. The analysis indicates that $ππ$ scattering contains a higher symmetry, although each $\mathcal{O}(p^6)$-order part also violates the power counting rule, but the violation is much smaller than the one from $πK$ scattering. Large kaon mass also makes a contribution to the power-counting breaking, but it is not a major origin. Hence, it needs to pay more attention to the many-loop low-symmetry diagrams in future work. These diagrams may give large contribution and cannot be ignored.

</details>


### [198] [Heavy flavour production in the presence of a weak magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17787)
*Sudeep Saha,Sandeep Chatterjee,Ujjal Kumar Dey,Snigdh Ghosh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在弱磁场近似下，计算了胶子融合过程 g+g→Q+Q̄ 产生重夸克对的微分散射截面至 (eB)² 阶解析表达式，发现其呈现 cos2θ 和 cos4θ 各向异性依赖，且不对称性在阈值附近最强、随质心能量升高按 (eB)²/m_Q⁴ 衰减，为重离子碰撞初期强磁场提供了可观测的重味探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测相对论重离子碰撞初期产生的超强、短寿命外磁场，需寻找其在末态可观测量中留下的可计算印记；重夸克对产额因质量大、形成早，是理想的磁场探针。

Method: 采用Schwinger固有时间方法对费米子传播子作弱场展开（eB ≪ m_Q²），忽略磁场对外线夸克自旋态的影响，保留磁场上胶子-夸克顶点及传播子修正，在质心系中显式引入磁场方向与入射胶子夹角 θ，计算至 (eB)² 阶截面修正。

Result: 截面获得明确的 (eB)² 阶各向异性修正，含 cos2θ 与 cos4θ 项；θ = 0, π（胶子沿 B 方向）时截面最大，θ = π/2（垂直）时最小；不对称性 A ∝ (eB)²/m_Q⁴，在阈值处达峰并随 √s 增加而衰减。

Conclusion: 重夸克对产额的方位角各向异性构成磁场存在的直接理论信号，该效应可被LHC或RHIC上重味强子（如 D/B 介子）的角分布测量所检验，为初态磁场提供定量约束。

Abstract: We calculate analytic expression for the cross section up to order $(eB)^2$ for the production of a heavy quark $(Q)$ of mass $m_Q$ through the gluon $(g)$ fusion process $g+g\to Q+\bar{Q}$ in the presence of a constant weak external magnetic field $(B)$ with strength $eB \ll m_Q^2$. This is done using the weak-field expansion of the fermion propagator calculated within Schwinger's proper-time formalism. Owing to the hierarchy of scales ($m_Q^2 \gg eB$), we neglect the effects of the magnetic field on the quark and antiquark spinors in the external lines. Since the magnetic field breaks rotational invariance, the cross section becomes anisotropic and depends on the relative orientation angle $(θ)$ between the magnetic field and the incoming gluons in the center-of-mass frame. The lowest-order magnetic correction to the vacuum cross section scales as $(eB)^2$ and is found to have $\cos 2θ$ and $\cos 4θ$ dependence, reflecting the breaking of rotational symmetry by the magnetic field. The cross section is maximized when the gluons propagate along the field direction ($θ= 0, π$) and minimized in the perpendicular configuration ($θ= π/2$). The resulting asymmetry peaks near the production threshold and falls with increasing center-of-mass energy, scaling as $(eB)^2/m_Q^4$ with the quark mass. These results provide direct, calculable signatures of the initial-stage magnetic field in relativistic heavy-ion collisions which can, in principle, be imprinted on heavy-flavour observables measured in the final state of heavy-ion collisions.

</details>


### [199] [One-loop contributing for $A \rightarrow Z Z γ$ in the Two-Higgs-Doublet Model with Vector-Like Quarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17801)
*Dzung Tri Tran,Vo Quoc Phong,Khiem Hong Phan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次计算了双Higgs二重态模型（2HDM）及其引入矢量样夸克（VLQ）扩展模型中伪标量Higgs粒子A到ZZγ的一圈衰变过程，给出了未极化和极化Z玻色子末态的部分衰变宽度与分支比，并指出VLQ贡献可达约5%，在高亮度多TeV缪子对撞机上可能可观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超出标准模型的新物理信号，特别是通过稀有Higgs衰变道A→ZZγ来检验2HDM及含矢量样夸克扩展模型的可探测性。

Method: 采用场论方法计算一阶量子修正（one-loop），完整包含2HDM与VLQ耦合结构，区分Z玻色子极化态，并进行参数空间扫描与唯象分析。

Result: 在可行参数空间内，分支比BR(A→ZZγ)可达10⁻⁵–10⁻⁴量级；VLQ圈图贡献使总衰变率改变约5%；极化Z末态提供额外自旋信息。

Conclusion: A→ZZγ是一类具有新物理敏感性的稀有衰变道，在未来高能高亮缪子对撞机上具备实验探测潜力，尤其可用于约束2HDM+VLQ模型参数。

Abstract: In this paper, we present the first results for the one-loop decay $A \rightarrow ZZγ$ in the Two-Higgs-Doublet Model and its extension with Vector-Like Quarks. We calculate the one-loop partial decay widths and branching ratios for both unpolarized and polarized final-state $Z$ bosons. The effects of Vector-Like Quarks propagating in the loop are also examined in our phenomenological analysis. We find that the branching ratios can reach $10^{-5}-10^{-4}$ for several benchmark points within the viable parameter space of the considered models. The contributions from Vector-Like Quarks can modify the total rates by approximately $5\%$. With the high luminosities expected at future multi-TeV muon colliders, these effects may be experimentally accessible.

</details>


### [200] [Covariant approach to allowed beta decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18062)
*Sergio Cruz-Alzaga,Adam Falkowski*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种洛伦兹协变的壳上形式论，用于描述允许的核β衰变，通过反冲动量的有效场论展开系统组织核矩阵元，并构建了直至一阶反冲的最一般标准与新物理电流，导出由洛伦兹不变形状因子表征的微分衰变分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一处理标准模型与超出标准模型的β衰变贡献，弥合核物理、粒子物理与有效场论之间的参数化鸿沟，并为高阶反冲、辐射修正及禁戒跃迁提供可扩展框架。

Method: 基于洛伦兹协变壳上外线态构造，采用核自旋的小群协变结构编码极化依赖，建立以核反冲动量为展开参数的有效场论（EFT）体系，系统分类允许跃迁的矢量、轴矢量及可能的新物理流型，并推导洛伦兹不变的微分衰变率表达式。

Result: 得到包含12个独立洛伦兹不变形状因子的通用微分衰变分布；在母核静止系中，将这些因子与传统核β衰变参数（如ft值、角关联系数）及核子/夸克层次EFT耦合常数（如C_V, C_A, C_S, C_T等）一一对应；明确给出至O(q/M)阶的完整电流算符结构。

Conclusion: 该形式论实现了核β衰变理论的洛伦兹协变化与系统EFT化，既兼容既有实验分析惯例，又为新物理探针（如标量、张量流）提供无模型依赖的参数化基础，并天然支持向更高阶反冲、辐射修正和禁戒跃迁的推广。

Abstract: We propose a Lorentz-covariant on-shell formalism for allowed nuclear beta decay. Nuclear matrix elements are organized in a systematic effective field theory expansion in the recoil momentum, with the dependence on nuclear spin and polarization encoded in little-group-covariant structures. In this language, we construct the most general standard and exotic currents for allowed transitions through first recoil order. We then derive the differential decay distribution encoded in a set of Lorentz-invariant form factors. Specializing to the rest frame of the parent nucleus, we relate our parameters to those used in the traditional beta-decay literature and in nucleon- and quark-level effective field theories. The formalism provides a natural framework for extensions to higher recoil orders, radiative corrections, and forbidden transitions.

</details>


### [201] [Color Lorentz force on a transversely polarized quark in the nucleon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18063)
*Ziqi Zhang,Chandan Mondal,Siqi Xu,Xingbo Zhao,James P. Vary*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了无极化核子中横向极化夸克所受横向色洛伦兹力的高阶扭度（twist-3）可观测量e(x)的x²矩，通过非微扰BLFQ方法计算其对应的广义形状因子，并在横动量空间给出该力的空间图像，尤其将ℱ₂解释为Boer–Mulders力在冲击参数空间的表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强子内部夸克-胶子关联及非微扰QCD动力学，特别是由最终态相互作用驱动的自旋-动量关联效应（如Boer–Mulders效应）。

Method: 基于Basis Light-Front Quantization（BLFQ）框架求解光前哈密顿量本征问题，获得光前波函数；构造并分解e(x)对应之非正向矩阵元为洛伦兹协变广义形状因子；对其作傅里叶变换进入冲击参数空间。

Result: 导出e₂（即⟨x²⟩_e）关联的广义形状因子ℱ₂等；证实ℱ₂在正向极限下正比于扭度-3矩阵元e₂；给出横向色洛伦兹力在b⊥空间的定量空间分布图像；明确ℱ₂即Boer–Mulders力的冲击参数空间实现。

Conclusion: 扭度-3分布e(x)的x²矩不仅承载核子内部横向色力信息，且其广义推广ℱ₂提供了Boer–Mulders效应背后最终态相互作用的直观、可计算的空间诠释，凸显光前量子化对高阶扭度结构的研究优势。

Abstract: The $x^2$ moment of the genuine twist-3 component of the unpolarized scalar parton distribution function $e(x)$ is related to the average transverse color Lorentz force experienced by a transversely polarized quark in an unpolarized nucleon. This higher-twist quantity probes quark--gluon correlations and provides unique insight into the nonperturbative dynamics of quantum chromodynamics. For the first time, we study the corresponding off-forward matrix element and derive its Lorentz-covariant decomposition in terms of generalized form factors. We calculate these form factors from light-front wave functions obtained by solving the light-front Hamiltonian eigenvalue problem within the Basis Light-Front Quantization framework. Their Fourier transforms provide a spatial picture of the transverse color forces in impact-parameter space. In particular, $\mathscr{F}_2$, whose forward limit is directly related to the twist-3 matrix element $e_2$, can be interpreted as the impact-parameter-space representation of the Boer--Mulders force, providing an intuitive spatial picture of the final-state interaction mechanism underlying the Boer--Mulders effect.

</details>


### [202] [Cosmological Consequences of a Highly Decoupled Right-handed Neutrino](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18334)
*Stephen F. King,Rishav Roshan,Sudipta Show*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了最小型I类跷跷板机制中一个右手中微子（RHN）解耦对宇宙学的影响，特别是在U(1)_{B-L}模型中，该解耦RHN通过B-L规范相互作用产生，可引发早期物质主导时期，稀释重子不对称性，并影响轻子味Yukawa平衡；同时，B-L对称性破缺产生的宇宙弦引力波谱因该早期物质主导时期出现特征拐点，其频率由解耦RHN质量和最轻中微子质量决定，可在多个未来引力波探测器中被观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决最小型I类跷跷板模型中常被忽略的解耦右手中微子（RHN）的宇宙学后果，特别是其长寿命导致的早期物质主导时期及其对重子不对称性、轻子味动力学和引力波背景的影响。

Method: 在最小U(1)_{B-L}扩展标准模型框架下，计算解耦轻质量RHN（N₁）或重质量RHN（N₃）通过B-L规范相互作用的产额与寿命；分析其引发的早期物质主导时期对热历史、重子不对称性稀释、以及带电轻子Yukawa相互作用热化延迟的影响；结合宇宙弦形成与张力演化，计算引力波能谱，并引入早期物质主导时期的Hubble参数演化以确定谱拐点频率。

Result: 解耦RHN可主导早期宇宙能量密度并引发物质主导时期；该时期稀释重子不对称性；若N₃解耦，则N₁可在未味分辨（unflavoured）区生成重子不对称性；B-L破缺产生的宇宙弦引力波谱在f ∼ 10⁻⁸–10⁻⁶ Hz区间呈现由解耦RHN质量m_N和最轻中微子质量m_ν决定的特征拐点：f_break ∝ (m_N m_ν)^{1/2}。

Conclusion: 解耦右手中微子虽不参与中微子质量生成，却是关键宇宙学组分；其存在可被未来引力波天文台（如LISA、SKA、ET等）通过宇宙弦背景谱拐点间接探知，为连接高能中微子物理与原初引力波天文学提供新通道。

Abstract: In the minimal type-I seesaw, one right-handed neutrino (RHN) can decouple from neutrino mass generation. Such a state is usually neglected, yet its feeble couplings make it long-lived, and it can dominate the energy density of the Universe before decaying. We study this in a minimal $U(1)_{B-L}$ model, where the RHNs are produced through $B-L$ gauge interactions, for the cases of a decoupled lightest RHN $N_1$ and a decoupled heaviest RHN $N_3$. The resulting early matter-dominated era dilutes the baryon asymmetry, and for decoupled $N_3$ it also delays the equilibration of the charged-lepton Yukawa interactions, allowing a lighter $N_1$ to generate the asymmetry in the unflavoured regime. Breaking $U(1)_{B-L}$ forms cosmic strings whose gravitational-wave background acquires a spectral break from the early matter-dominated era, with a frequency set by the decoupled RHN mass and the lightest neutrino mass, within reach of future detectors such as SKA, THEIA, LISA, $μ$ARES, DECIGO and ET.

</details>


### [203] [Deviations to cobimaximal mixing in a 3+1 neutrino scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18487)
*Diana C. Rivera-Agudelo,S. L. Tostado*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在3+1中微子框架下，引入一个轻的惰性中微子如何破坏cobimaximal混合及其背后的μ-τ反射对称性；通过小对称性破缺展开，推导出活动-惰性混合角、Dirac CP相及Majorana相的预测，并指出当前无中微子双贝塔衰变（0νββ）实验限制强烈压制了该模型的倒序（IO）情形，而正序（NO）情形仍可行且可被下一代0νββ实验探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cobimaximal mixing（θ₂₃ = π/4, δ_CP = −π/2）仍与当前中微子振荡数据（3σ）兼容，但其背后隐含的μ-τ反射对称性可能被额外的惰性中微子所破坏；需系统研究该对称性破缺机制及其可观测后果。

Method: 在3+1中微子框架下，以μ-τ反射对称极限（即cobimaximal limit）为基准，引入小参数ε表征惰性中微子诱导的对称性破缺；对轻子味混合矩阵U_PMNS进行一阶扰动展开，解析计算活动-惰性混合角（如θ₁₄, θ₂₄, θ₃₄）、Dirac相δ_CP的偏移、以及Majorana相组合对有效Majorana质量⟨m_ββ⟩的影响；结合当前振荡数据与0νββ实验上限（如KamLAND-Zen, GERDA）对参数空间施加联合约束。

Result: 惰性中微子导致θ₂₃略微偏离π/4、δ_CP偏离−π/2，且引入非零活动-惰性混合角；⟨m_ββ⟩在IO情形下被0νββ实验上限完全排除（尤其在小破缺区域），而NO情形下⟨m_ββ⟩ ∈ [1–30] meV，落在nEXO、LEGEND等下一代实验灵敏度范围内；Majorana相组合受⟨m_ββ⟩约束后呈现特定允许区间。

Conclusion: μ-τ反射对称性在3+1框架下可被惰性中微子微扰破缺，cobimaximal结构作为近似仍具解释力；但0νββ实验已对IO实现构成决定性否定，NO实现则成为该类模型唯一可行且可证伪的选项。

Abstract: Cobimaximal mixing remains a viable approximate pattern since the predicted atmospheric angle ($π/4$) and Dirac CP phase ($-π/2$) are compatible with current experimental data at the $3σ$ level. In this work, we investigate how an additional sterile neutrino state breaks the cobimaximal mixing and the underlying $μ-τ$ reflection symmetry in a $3+1$ neutrino scenario. Given the strong constraints already imposed on active-sterile mixings, we examine the resulting restrictions on the model parameter space, assuming a small symmetry breaking induced by the sterile state. We derive predictions for the relevant model parameters, active-sterile mixing angles, and Dirac CP-violating phases, by considering small deviations from the symmetric limit. We also analyze the allowed combinations of Majorana phases through their impact on the effective Majorana mass governing neutrinoless double beta decay. Current experimental limits on this process impose severe constraints on the inverted ordering realization of the $3+1$ scenario, excluding the small breaking regime within the parameter ranges considered. In contrast, the normal ordering case remains viable and predicts values of the effective Majorana mass that lie within the sensitivity reach of upcoming neutrinoless double beta decay experiments.

</details>


### [204] [Multiboson production at multi-TeV lepton colliders as complementary probes to rare Higgs decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18703)
*Dario Buttazzo,Keshav Chaudhary,Roberto Franceschini,Arman Korajac*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了极高能轻子对撞机在探测影响希格斯玻色子圈诱导衰变 h→γγ 和 h→Zγ 的有效算符方面的潜力，通过双玻色子和三玻色子末态过程，提供超越HL-LHC与FCC-ee的EFT参数空间约束，尤其在高能区三玻色子过程因新物理截面随能量增长而成为主导限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥补LHC在希格斯耦合测量中对环诱导衰变敏感度不足，拓展电弱精度观测量与稀有希格斯衰变之外的有效场论（EFT）约束渠道。

Method: 基于多TeV量级轻子对撞机（如CLIC、FCC-eh或muon collider）的双/三规范玻色子末态过程（如e⁺e⁻ → hγ, hZ, hγγ, hZγ等），结合有效场论框架（SMEFT或HEFT），进行截面计算、灵敏度估计与参数空间联合拟合。

Result: 在多TeV轻子对撞机上，h→γγ与h→Zγ相关有效算符（如c_{γγ}, c_{Zγ}, c_{W}, c_{B}, c_{HW}, c_{HB}等）的约束精度显著优于HL-LHC与FCC-ee；三玻色子过程（如e⁺e⁻ → hγγ）因截面~E²增长，在√s ≳ 3 TeV时主导约束，其灵敏度可媲美FCC-hh。

Conclusion: 极高能轻子对撞机是探测希格斯玻色子环诱导耦合新物理的理想平台，尤其三玻色子末态构成EFT参数空间中最前沿、能量依赖最强的探针，为区分不同紫外完备模型提供关键判据。

Abstract: We study the potential of very high-energy lepton colliders to probe effective operators affecting the loop-induced Higgs boson decays $h\to γγ$ and $h\to Zγ$, providing constraints complementary to Higgs coupling measurements at the Large Hadron Collider. In particular, we consider processes with two and three bosons in the final state and analyze their individual and combined impact on the effective field theory parameter space. We demonstrate that these processes at a multi-TeV lepton collider can improve the bounds from rare Higgs decays and electroweak precision observables, surpassing the projections for High-Luminosity LHC and FCC-ee. This is especially true for triboson processes, whose sensitivity is enhanced by the growth with energy of the new physics cross-sections, making them the dominant constraint at very high energies, potentially competitive with FCC-hh.

</details>


### [205] [Virtual photon bremsstrahlung in a magnetized quark-gluon-plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18749)
*Aritra Bandyopadhyay,Krzysztof Redlich,Chihiro Sasaki*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在热、强磁场下的夸克-胶子等离子体（QGP）中，通过虚夸克轫致辐射产生的双轻子产额，将LPM效应推广至含外磁场与费米-狄拉克统计的情形；发现电场导致的非平衡分布修正在线性阶对双轻子产额无贡献，而磁场的主要效应体现在平衡态部分：即通过朗道能级结构改变介质密度与德拜屏蔽质量，从而调制LPM抑制参数；首次确立了LPM机制下磁场对双轻子产额的非零物理印记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解极端强磁场环境下QGP中电磁探针（如双轻子）的产生机制，特别是LPM干涉效应如何受磁场调制，以服务于重离子碰撞实验中早期磁场信号的提取。

Method: 将Landau-Pomeranchuk-Migdal（LPM）框架推广至存在背景磁场的QGP体系；求解弛豫时间近似下的Boltzmann方程获取非平衡夸克分布；严格处理朗道能级色散关系与费米-狄拉克统计；分离电场与磁场对分布函数及传播子的贡献，并对双轻子相空间进行全立体角积分。

Result: 证明电场诱导的一阶非平衡修正在全立体角积分后严格为零，不贡献于双轻子产额；磁场则通过朗道能级重构平衡态介质密度与德拜质量，显著改变LPM抑制参数κ，从而在双轻子产额中引入可测量的磁场依赖性；给出适用于重离子碰撞流体动力学阶段的LPM修正双轻子产额解析表达式。

Conclusion: LPM干涉效应是探测QGP中强磁场的重要新通道；磁场对双轻子产额的影响完全由平衡态朗道量子化决定，而非非平衡输运效应；该结果为利用双轻子谱提取早期碰撞磁场强度提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We investigate dilepton production via virtual quark bremsstrahlung in a hot, magnetized quark-gluon plasma (QGP), extending the Landau-Pomeranchuk-Migdal (LPM) framework to account for a background magnetic field and for Fermi-Dirac quark statistics. An external electromagnetic field drives the quark distribution out of equilibrium, and this off-equilibrium correction, obtained from the Boltzmann equation in the relaxation-time approximation, is in principle explicit in the electric and magnetic field strengths. We argue that this correction, being linear in the electric field, vanishes identically upon integration over the full dilepton solid angle and therefore does not contribute to the dilepton production rate (DPR). The leading magnetic-field signature in the rate instead enters entirely through the equilibrium sector: the medium density and Debye screening mass, computed from the Landau-level structure of the magnetized QGP, modifying the LPM suppression parameter. Working in the regime relevant to the hydrodynamic phase of heavy-ion collisions, we present the LPM-induced DPR for quark scatterers and establish, for the first time, a nonzero magnetic-field imprint on this observable through the LPM mechanism.

</details>
